  ```ts
  const nodeRequire = createRequire(__filename);
  const read = (rel: string) => fs.readFileSync(path.join(ROOT, rel), 'utf8');
  const webpush = nodeRequire('web-push') as { generateVAPIDKeys: () => { publicKey: string; privateKey: string } };
  const ece = createRequire(nodeRequire.resolve('web-push'))('http_ece') as { decrypt: (body: Buffer, params: Row) => Buffer };

  const VAPID = webpush.generateVAPIDKeys();
  const setKeys = () => {
    process.env.VAPID_PUBLIC_KEY = VAPID.publicKey;
    process.env.VAPID_PRIVATE_KEY = VAPID.privateKey;
  };
  setKeys();

  interface Subscriber { ecdh: crypto.ECDH; authSecret: Buffer; p256dh: string; auth: string }
  function subscriber(): Subscriber {
    const ecdh = crypto.createECDH('prime256v1');
    ecdh.generateKeys();
    const authSecret = crypto.randomBytes(16);
    return { ecdh, authSecret, p256dh: ecdh.getPublicKey().toString('base64url'), auth: authSecret.toString('base64url') };
  }
  const S1 = subscriber();
  const S2 = subscriber();
  const FCM = 'https://fcm.googleapis.com/fcm/send/dQw4w9WgXcQ:APA91bHPRgkF3JUikC4ENAHEeMrd41Zxv3hVZjC9KtT8OvPVGJ';
  const APPLE = 'https://web.push.apple.com/QGuQyavXutnMH7Yfc6PB9mxXzqrBm2rWvzGJ7oM8hGfTxwvW-5rNjzQZq0G9IBbx';
  const D1 = { id: 'dev-fcm', endpoint: FCM, p256dh: S1.p256dh, auth: S1.auth };
  const D2 = { id: 'dev-apple', endpoint: APPLE, p256dh: S2.p256dh, auth: S2.auth };
  const SUBSCRIBER_OF: Record<string, Subscriber> = { [FCM]: S1, [APPLE]: S2 };

  const USER = { id: 7, pauseOnShabbat: true, zmanimCommunity: 'sephardi' };
  const TITLE = 'לאסוף את נועה מהגן';
  const BODY = 'תזכורת לשעה ארבע';
  const MESSAGE = { kind: 'reminder' as const, title: TITLE, body: BODY, url: 'https://famcal.bot/he/dashboard', tag: 'nr-clx7stateid', lang: 'he' as const };
  const HOUR = 3_600_000;
  const DAY = 86_400_000;

  // ---- The world: the devices, the next pause window, the push services ----

  let devices: Row[] = [];
  let deviceReads = 0;
  let readFails = false;
  const gone: string[] = [];
  const pauseAsks: { userId: number; from: string; withinMs: number }[] = [];
  let pauseAnswer: Date | null = null;
  type Answer = number | Error | { status: number; retryAfter: string };
  const answers = new Map<string, Answer[]>();
  const pushes: { url: string; method: string; headers: Headers; body: Buffer }[] = [];
  const pushesTo = (url: string) => pushes.filter((p) => p.url === url);
  const sendLines = () => logs.filter((l) => /^\[push\] user \d+: \S+ sent to \d+ of \d+ devices$/.test(l));

  function decrypt(push: { url: string; body: Buffer }): string {
    const s = SUBSCRIBER_OF[push.url];
    return ece.decrypt(push.body, { version: 'aes128gcm', privateKey: s.ecdh, authSecret: s.authSecret }).toString('utf8');
  }

  /** A VAPID Authorization header: its signature checked against its own key, and its claims. */
  function vapidOf(authorization: string | null): { ok: boolean; key: string; claims: Row } {
    const m = /^vapid t=([^,]+), k=(\S+)$/.exec(authorization ?? '');
    assert(m, `not a VAPID Authorization: ${String(authorization).slice(0, 20)}`);
    const [header, payload, signature] = m[1].split('.');
    const pub = Buffer.from(m[2], 'base64url');
    const key = crypto.createPublicKey({ key: { kty: 'EC', crv: 'P-256', x: pub.subarray(1, 33).toString('base64url'), y: pub.subarray(33).toString('base64url') }, format: 'jwk' });
    const ok = crypto.verify('sha256', Buffer.from(`${header}.${payload}`), { key, dsaEncoding: 'ieee-p1363' }, Buffer.from(signature, 'base64url'));
    return { ok, key: m[2], claims: JSON.parse(Buffer.from(payload, 'base64url').toString('utf8')) };
  }

  async function main() {
    await installSigninWorld();
    stubModule('src/services/web-push/devices.ts', {
      MAX_DEVICES: 10,
      devicesForSend: async (userId: number) => {
        deviceReads++;
        // A Prisma error's message can print the query's values: the sender must not log it
        if (readFails) throw Object.assign(new Error(`Invalid \`prisma.pushDevice.findMany()\` invocation near ${FCM}`), { name: 'PrismaClientUnknownRequestError' });
        return userId === USER.id ? devices.map((d) => ({ ...d })) : [];
      },
      deleteGoneDevice: async (id: string) => { gone.push(id); },
    });
    stubModule('src/services/zmanim/pause.ts', {
      isPaused: async () => false,
      nextPauseStart: async (user: Row, from: Date, withinMs: number) => {
        pauseAsks.push({ userId: user.id, from: from.toISOString(), withinMs });
        return pauseAnswer;
      },
    });
    // The push services: each endpoint answers from its queue (default 201), and every request is kept
    globalThis.fetch = (async (input: unknown, init: RequestInit = {}) => {
      const url = String(input);
      pushes.push({ url, method: String(init.method), headers: new Headers(init.headers), body: Buffer.from((init.body ?? new Uint8Array()) as Uint8Array) });
      const next = answers.get(url)?.shift() ?? 201;
      if (next instanceof Error) throw next;
      const { status, retryAfter } = typeof next === 'number' ? { status: next, retryAfter: undefined } : next;
      return new Response(null, { status, headers: retryAfter ? { 'retry-after': retryAfter } : {} });
    }) as typeof fetch;
    const retry = await load('src/utils/fetch-retry.ts');
    retry.__useRetryForTests({ sleep: async () => undefined, random: () => 0 });
    const payload = await load('src/services/web-push/payload.ts').catch(() => ({} as Row));
    const send = await load('src/services/web-push/send.ts').catch(() => ({ __resetPushForTests: () => undefined } as Row));

    const reset = () => {
      resetWorld();
      send.__resetPushForTests();
      setKeys();
      devices = [D1, D2];
      deviceReads = 0;
      readFails = false;
      gone.length = 0;
      pauseAsks.length = 0;
      pauseAnswer = null;
      answers.clear();
      pushes.length = 0;
    };
    const T0 = new Date();

    await check('W1 the payload: one declarative JSON (web_push 8030; title, body, navigate, lang, dir rtl for Hebrew and ltr otherwise, tag, silent); at most 2048 bytes of UTF-8, a long title cut first but never below half the room, then the body, each with "…" and never half a character; the Topic is 32 base64url characters of the tag\'s SHA-256, the same for the same tag and none of its text', () => {
      const parsed = JSON.parse(payload.buildPushPayload(MESSAGE));
      assert(show(parsed) === show({ web_push: 8030, notification: { title: TITLE, body: BODY, navigate: MESSAGE.url, lang: 'he', dir: 'rtl', tag: MESSAGE.tag, silent: false } }), `payload ${show(parsed)}`);
      const en = JSON.parse(payload.buildPushPayload({ ...MESSAGE, lang: 'en', silent: true })).notification;
      const ru = JSON.parse(payload.buildPushPayload({ ...MESSAGE, lang: 'ru' })).notification;
      assert(en.dir === 'ltr' && en.silent === true && ru.dir === 'ltr' && ru.lang === 'ru', `en ${show(en)} ru ${show(ru)}`);
      const bytes = (s: string) => Buffer.byteLength(s, 'utf8');
      const lone = /[\uD800-\uDBFF](?![\uDC00-\uDFFF])|(?<![\uD800-\uDBFF])[\uDC00-\uDFFF]/;
      const room = payload.MAX_PAYLOAD_BYTES - bytes(payload.buildPushPayload({ ...MESSAGE, title: '', body: '' }));
      assert(payload.MAX_PAYLOAD_BYTES === 2048, `MAX_PAYLOAD_BYTES ${payload.MAX_PAYLOAD_BYTES}`);
      const cases: [string, string, string][] = [
        ['both long (Hebrew, 2 bytes a letter)', 'ת'.repeat(1500), 'ב'.repeat(1500)],
        ['a long title, a short body', 'ת'.repeat(1500), BODY],
        ['a short title, a long body', 'היום', 'ב'.repeat(1500)],
        ['emoji (4 bytes, two UTF-16 halves)', '😀'.repeat(800), '😀'.repeat(800)],
        ['quotes and backslashes (escaped in JSON)', '"\\'.repeat(900), 'x'],
      ];
      for (const [label, title, body] of cases) {
        const out = payload.buildPushPayload({ ...MESSAGE, title, body });
        const n = JSON.parse(out).notification;
        assert(bytes(out) <= 2048, `${label}: ${bytes(out)} bytes`);
        assert(!lone.test(n.title) && !lone.test(n.body), `${label}: half a character`);
        if (title.length > 100) assert(n.title.endsWith('…'), `${label}: the title is not cut with "…"`);
        if (body === BODY || body === 'x') assert(n.body === body, `${label}: a short body was cut: ${n.body}`);
        if (title === 'היום') assert(n.title === 'היום' && n.body.endsWith('…'), `${label}: ${show(n)}`);
        if (body.length > 100 && title.length > 100) assert(bytes(JSON.stringify(n.title)) - 2 >= Math.floor(room / 2) - 8, `${label}: the title fell below half the room (${bytes(n.title)} of ${room})`);
      }
      const topic = payload.topicFor('nr-clx7stateid');
      assert(/^[A-Za-z0-9_-]{32}$/.test(topic) && topic === payload.topicFor('nr-clx7stateid') && topic !== payload.topicFor('nr-clx7stateie'), `topic ${topic}`);
      assert(topic === crypto.createHash('sha256').update('nr-clx7stateid').digest('base64url').slice(0, 32), 'not the SHA-256 of the tag');
      assert(!topic.includes('clx7') && !topic.includes('nr-'), `the topic carries the tag: ${topic}`);
    });

    await check('W2 the lifetime: reminder 1 h, alert 24 h, briefing 3 h, the notices 24 h, the test 10 min; an alert stops at its event start; a scheduled kind (reminder, alert, briefing, reconnect) stops at the next pause window, asked over its own lifetime; the partner notices and the test never ask; never above a day, never below a second', async () => {
      reset();
      assert(show(send.PUSH_LIFETIME_S) === show({ reminder: 3600, alert: 86400, briefing: 10800, 'partner-accepted': 86400, 'partner-done': 86400, reconnect: 86400, test: 600 }), `lifetimes ${show(send.PUSH_LIFETIME_S)}`);
      assert(show(send.PUSH_URGENCY) === show({ reminder: 'high', alert: 'high', briefing: 'normal', 'partner-accepted': 'normal', 'partner-done': 'low', reconnect: 'normal', test: 'normal' }), `urgency ${show(send.PUSH_URGENCY)}`);
      assert(show(send.PAUSED_KINDS) === show(['reminder', 'alert', 'briefing', 'reconnect']), `paused kinds ${show(send.PAUSED_KINDS)}`);
      for (const [kind, want] of Object.entries(send.PUSH_LIFETIME_S as Record<string, number>)) {
        assert(await send.pushTtlSeconds(USER, kind, T0) === want, `${kind}: ${await send.pushTtlSeconds(USER, kind, T0)}`);
      }
      assert(show(pauseAsks.map((a) => [a.withinMs, a.from])) === show([[3_600_000, T0.toISOString()], [86_400_000, T0.toISOString()], [10_800_000, T0.toISOString()], [86_400_000, T0.toISOString()]]), `asked ${show(pauseAsks)}`);
      assert(await send.pushTtlSeconds(USER, 'alert', T0, new Date(T0.getTime() + 30 * 60_000)) === 1800, 'an alert stops at its event');
      assert(await send.pushTtlSeconds(USER, 'alert', T0, new Date(T0.getTime() + 3 * DAY)) === 86400, 'at most a day');
      assert(await send.pushTtlSeconds(USER, 'alert', T0, new Date(T0.getTime() - 60_000)) === 1, 'an event already started: one second');
      pauseAnswer = new Date(T0.getTime() + 20 * 60_000);
      assert(await send.pushTtlSeconds(USER, 'reminder', T0) === 1200 && await send.pushTtlSeconds(USER, 'briefing', T0) === 1200, 'stops at the window');
      pauseAsks.length = 0;
      assert(await send.pushTtlSeconds(USER, 'partner-done', T0) === 86400 && await send.pushTtlSeconds(USER, 'partner-accepted', T0) === 86400 && await send.pushTtlSeconds(USER, 'test', T0) === 600, 'the notices are not paused');
      assert(pauseAsks.length === 0, `the notices asked the pause: ${show(pauseAsks)}`);
      assert(send.MAX_PUSH_TTL_S === 86400 && Object.values(send.PUSH_LIFETIME_S as Record<string, number>).every((s) => s <= 86400), 'a lifetime over a day');
    });

    await check('W3 a send: every device gets a POST built by web-push and sent by fetchWithRetry, with TTL, Urgency, Topic, aes128gcm and our VAPID header (no Content-Length of our own); its body decrypts with that device\'s keys to the payload; the result and one log line count the devices', async () => {
      reset();
      const result = await send.sendWebPush(USER, MESSAGE, T0);
      assert(show(result) === show({ sent: 2, total: 2, retriable: false }), `result ${show(result)}`);
      assert(pushes.length === 2 && pushesTo(FCM).length === 1 && pushesTo(APPLE).length === 1, `pushes ${pushes.map((p) => p.url.slice(0, 30))}`);
      const want = payload.buildPushPayload(MESSAGE);
      for (const push of pushes) {
        const h = push.headers;
        assert(push.method === 'POST', `method ${push.method}`);
        assert(h.get('ttl') === '3600' && h.get('urgency') === 'high' && h.get('topic') === payload.topicFor(MESSAGE.tag), `headers ${show([...h.keys()])} ttl ${h.get('ttl')} urgency ${h.get('urgency')}`);
        assert(h.get('content-encoding') === 'aes128gcm' && h.get('content-type') === 'application/octet-stream' && !h.has('content-length'), `encoding headers ${show([...h.entries()].filter(([k]) => k !== 'authorization'))}`);
        assert(decrypt(push) === want, 'the body does not decrypt to the payload');
        const vapid = vapidOf(h.get('authorization'));
        assert(vapid.ok && vapid.key === VAPID.publicKey && vapid.claims.sub === 'mailto:privacy@famcal.bot' && vapid.claims.aud === new URL(push.url).origin, `VAPID ${show(vapid.claims)}`);
        assert(vapid.claims.exp > Date.now() / 1000 && vapid.claims.exp <= Date.now() / 1000 + 86400, `exp ${vapid.claims.exp}`);
      }
      assert(show(sendLines()) === show(['[push] user 7: reminder sent to 2 of 2 devices']), `log ${show(logs)}`);
      assert(pauseAsks.length === 1 && pauseAsks[0].withinMs === 3_600_000, `the pause asked ${show(pauseAsks)}`);
    });

    await check('W4 the answers: 200 and 202 are sent; 404 and 410 delete the device; 429 then 201 is retried and sent; 503 to the end and no answer to the end are retriable; a Retry-After over 10 s fails at once (retriable); one device sent and one failing is not retriable; 400 and 413 are reported; 401 and 403 are reported once a day per device', async () => {
      reset();
      answers.set(FCM, [200]); answers.set(APPLE, [202]);
      assert(show(await send.sendWebPush(USER, MESSAGE, T0)) === show({ sent: 2, total: 2, retriable: false }), 'any 2xx');
      reset();
      answers.set(FCM, [404]); answers.set(APPLE, [410]);
      assert(show(await send.sendWebPush(USER, MESSAGE, T0)) === show({ sent: 0, total: 2, retriable: false }) && show([...gone].sort()) === show(['dev-apple', 'dev-fcm']), `gone ${show(gone)}`);
      assert(captured.length === 0 && pushes.length === 2, `a gone device is no error, and is not retried: ${show(captured)}, ${pushes.length} pushes`);
      reset();
      devices = [D1];
      answers.set(FCM, [429, 201]);
      assert(show(await send.sendWebPush(USER, MESSAGE, T0)) === show({ sent: 1, total: 1, retriable: false }) && pushesTo(FCM).length === 2, `429 then 201: ${pushesTo(FCM).length} pushes`);
      reset();
      answers.set(FCM, [503, 503, 503, 503]); answers.set(APPLE, [500, 502, 503, 504]);
      assert(show(await send.sendWebPush(USER, MESSAGE, T0)) === show({ sent: 0, total: 2, retriable: true }) && pushes.length === 8, `5xx to the end: ${pushes.length} pushes`);
      assert(logs.filter((l) => /^\[push\] user 7: reminder to device dev-(fcm|apple) not delivered \(gave up after 3 retries/.test(l)).length === 2, `the give-up lines ${show(logs)}`);
      reset();
      answers.set(FCM, Array.from({ length: 4 }, () => new TypeError('fetch failed'))); answers.set(APPLE, Array.from({ length: 4 }, () => new TypeError('fetch failed')));
      assert(show(await send.sendWebPush(USER, MESSAGE, T0)) === show({ sent: 0, total: 2, retriable: true }), 'no answer to the end');
      reset();
      devices = [D1];
      answers.set(FCM, [{ status: 429, retryAfter: '30' }]);
      assert(show(await send.sendWebPush(USER, MESSAGE, T0)) === show({ sent: 0, total: 1, retriable: true }) && pushes.length === 1, `Retry-After 30 s: ${pushes.length} pushes`);
      reset();
      answers.set(APPLE, [503, 503, 503, 503]);
      assert(show(await send.sendWebPush(USER, MESSAGE, T0)) === show({ sent: 1, total: 2, retriable: false }), 'one took it');
      for (const status of [400, 413]) {
        reset();
        devices = [D1];
        answers.set(FCM, [status]);
        assert(show(await send.sendWebPush(USER, MESSAGE, T0)) === show({ sent: 0, total: 1, retriable: false }) && pushes.length === 1 && gone.length === 0, `${status}`);
        assert(captured.length === 1 && captured[0].context === 'push-send' && captured[0].extra.status === status && captured[0].extra.device_id === 'dev-fcm', `${status} reported: ${show(captured)}`);
      }
      reset();
      devices = [D1];
      answers.set(FCM, [401, 403, 401]);
      await send.sendWebPush(USER, MESSAGE, T0);
      await send.sendWebPush(USER, MESSAGE, new Date(T0.getTime() + 60_000));
      assert(captured.length === 1 && captured[0].extra.status === 401, `once a day: ${show(captured)}`);
      assert(logs.filter((l) => l.startsWith('[push] user 7: reminder to device dev-fcm: refused (40')).length === 2, `each refusal logged: ${show(logs)}`);
      await send.sendWebPush(USER, MESSAGE, new Date(T0.getTime() + 25 * HOUR));
      assert(captured.length === 2, `the next day reports again: ${show(captured)}`);
    });

    await check('W5 the VAPID header: one per push service (its origin is the audience), the same one for an hour, a new one after it', async () => {
      reset();
      const authOf = (url: string) => pushesTo(url).map((p) => p.headers.get('authorization'));
      await send.sendWebPush(USER, MESSAGE, T0);
      await send.sendWebPush(USER, MESSAGE, new Date(T0.getTime() + 59 * 60_000));
      await send.sendWebPush(USER, MESSAGE, new Date(T0.getTime() + 61 * 60_000));
      const [fcm1, fcm2, fcm3] = authOf(FCM);
      const [apple1, apple2, apple3] = authOf(APPLE);
      assert(fcm1 === fcm2 && apple1 === apple2, 'not kept for the hour');
      assert(fcm1 !== apple1 && vapidOf(fcm1).claims.aud === 'https://fcm.googleapis.com' && vapidOf(apple1).claims.aud === 'https://web.push.apple.com', 'one per push service');
      assert(fcm3 !== fcm1 && apple3 !== apple1 && vapidOf(fcm3).ok && vapidOf(apple3).ok, 'not made anew after the hour');
      assert(!/setVapidDetails\(|vapidDetails\s*:/.test(read('src/services/web-push/send.ts')), 'send.ts gives web-push the keys (it would sign a new token every call)');
    });

    await check('W6 push off, no devices, a refused stored key, a failed device read: no keys sends nothing and reads nothing; no devices sends nothing (0 of 0); a device whose key the library refuses is reported and the others go; a failed read is retriable and reported by its kind only; none throws', async () => {
      reset();
      delete process.env.VAPID_PRIVATE_KEY;
      assert(show(await send.sendWebPush(USER, MESSAGE, T0)) === show({ sent: 0, total: 0, retriable: false }) && pushes.length === 0 && deviceReads === 0, `push off: ${pushes.length} pushes, ${deviceReads} reads`);
      assert(logs.includes('[push] user 7: reminder not sent: browser notifications are off (no VAPID keys)'), `push off log ${show(logs)}`);
      reset();
      devices = [];
      assert(show(await send.sendWebPush(USER, MESSAGE, T0)) === show({ sent: 0, total: 0, retriable: false }) && pushes.length === 0 && pauseAsks.length === 0, 'no devices');
      assert(show(sendLines()) === show(['[push] user 7: reminder sent to 0 of 0 devices']), `no devices log ${show(logs)}`);
      reset();
      devices = [{ id: 'dev-bad', endpoint: `${FCM}x`, p256dh: 'abc', auth: S1.auth }, D2];
      assert(show(await send.sendWebPush(USER, MESSAGE, T0)) === show({ sent: 1, total: 2, retriable: false }) && pushes.length === 1, 'a bad stored key');
      assert(captured.some((c) => c.context === 'push-send' && c.extra.device_id === 'dev-bad' && c.extra.purpose === 'build'), `reported ${show(captured)}`);
      reset();
      readFails = true;
      assert(show(await send.sendWebPush(USER, MESSAGE, T0)) === show({ sent: 0, total: 0, retriable: true }) && pushes.length === 0, 'a failed read');
      assert(logs.includes('[push] user 7: reminder not sent: PrismaClientUnknownRequestError') && captured.some((c) => c.context === 'push-send' && c.extra.user_id === 7), `read failure ${show(logs)} ${show(captured)}`);
    });

    await check('W7 the code and the rules: send.ts builds with generateRequestDetails and sends with fetchWithRetry (never sendNotification, never the Google policy); web-push 3.6.7 and @types/web-push 3.6.4 pinned exact; CLAUDE.md\'s Google rule and third-party rule, and fetch-retry.ts\'s header, send every push service, FCM included, through fetchWithRetry', () => {
      const code = read('src/services/web-push/send.ts');
      const problems: string[] = [];
      if (!/\bgenerateRequestDetails\(/.test(code) || !/\bfetchWithRetry\(/.test(code)) problems.push('send.ts does not build with generateRequestDetails and send with fetchWithRetry');
      if (/\bsendNotification\b/.test(code)) problems.push('send.ts names sendNotification (web-push\'s own sender bypasses the retry loop)');
      if (/google-retry|googleFetch/.test(code)) problems.push('send.ts uses the Google policy');
      const pkg = JSON.parse(read('package.json'));
      if (pkg.dependencies?.['web-push'] !== '3.6.7') problems.push(`web-push is ${pkg.dependencies?.['web-push']}`);
      if (pkg.devDependencies?.['@types/web-push'] !== '3.6.4') problems.push(`@types/web-push is ${pkg.devDependencies?.['@types/web-push']}`);
      const lines = read('CLAUDE.md').split('\n');
      const google = lines.find((l) => l.startsWith('- Every call to Google retries')) ?? '';
      const third = lines.find((l) => l.startsWith('- Every call to a third party other than Google')) ?? '';
      for (const word of ['`fcm.googleapis.com`', '`fetchWithRetry`']) if (!google.includes(word)) problems.push(`the Google rule does not name ${word}`);
      for (const word of ['every browser push service, FCM included', '`sendWebPush`', 'src/services/web-push/send.ts']) if (!third.includes(word)) problems.push(`the third-party rule does not name ${word}`);
      const header = read('src/utils/fetch-retry.ts').split('*/')[0];
      if (!/push service/.test(header) || !/FCM/.test(header)) problems.push('fetch-retry.ts\'s header does not name the push services');
      assert(problems.length === 0, problems.join('; '));
    });

    await check('W8 no console line or Sentry field of the run holds an endpoint, a push service token, a key, the private VAPID key, the title or the body', () => {
      const text = show(everyLog) + show(everyCapture);
      const secrets = [FCM, APPLE, 'dQw4w9WgXcQ', 'QGuQyavXutnMH7', S1.p256dh, S1.auth, S2.p256dh, S2.auth, VAPID.privateKey, TITLE, BODY];
      const found = secrets.filter((s) => text.includes(s));
      assert(found.length === 0, `leaked: ${found.map((s) => s.slice(0, 16)).join(', ')}`);
    });

    report();
  }

  main().catch((err) => {
    console.error(err);
    process.exit(1);
  });
  ```

  Notes for the implementer, about this smoke:
  - `everyCapture` comes from signin-world (every capture of the run), `captured` from the current check only (reset by `resetWorld`); W4's once-a-day part relies on not resetting between its three sends.
  - The fake `devices.ts` and `pause.ts` are served to `send.ts` by signin-world's `stubModule` (require cache and load hook), set before `send.ts` loads.
  - If `nodeRequire.resolve('web-push')` fails, Task 1's `npm install` is missing.

  **1b. `scripts/smoke-sentry-scrub.ts`**: before the report loop (`let failed = 0;`, ~line 387), insert:

  ```ts
    // ---- Browser notifications (spec 2026-11-05-web-push §3.4): a device's endpoint and keys, and the push services' paths ----

    await check('#15 browser notifications: endpoint, keys, p256dh and auth dropped from a body (object, JSON, form, cut-off JSON); the push services\' URL paths scrubbed from urls, breadcrumbs, the transaction name and span descriptions, every host the devices accept included; a second scrub changes nothing; another host\'s path and the rest kept', async () => {
      const config = await load('src/services/web-push/config.ts');
      const ENDPOINTS: Record<string, string> = {
        fcm: 'https://fcm.googleapis.com/fcm/send/dQw4w9WgXcQ:APA91bHPRgkF3JUikC4ENAHEeMrd41Zxv3hVZjC9KtT8',
        apple: 'https://web.push.apple.com/QGuQyavXutnMH7Yfc6PB9mxXzqrBm2rWvzGJ7oM8hGfT',
        mozilla: 'https://updates.push.services.mozilla.com/wpush/v2/gAAAAABnYtEXAMPLEpushtoken_abc',
        wns: 'https://wns2-par02p.notify.windows.com/w/?token=BQYAAABexampleWnsToken%2bAbc%3d',
      };
      const WANT: Record<string, string> = {
        fcm: 'https://fcm.googleapis.com/[scrubbed]',
        apple: 'https://web.push.apple.com/[scrubbed]',
        mozilla: 'https://updates.push.services.mozilla.com/[scrubbed]',
        wns: 'https://wns2-par02p.notify.windows.com/[scrubbed]?token=[scrubbed]',
      };
      const P256DH = 'BNcRdreALRFXTkOOUHK1EtK2wtaz5Ry4YfYCA_0QTpQtUbVlUls0VJXg7A8u-Ts1XbjhazAkj7I99e8QcYP7DkM';
      const AUTH = 'tBHItJI5svbpez7KI4CCXg';
      const SECRETS = ['dQw4w9WgXcQ', 'APA91bHPRgkF3J', 'QGuQyavXutnMH7', 'gAAAAABnYtEXAMPLE', 'BQYAAABexampleWnsToken', P256DH, AUTH];
      const pushLeaks = (value: unknown) => SECRETS.filter((s) => show(value).includes(s));
      const run = (data: unknown) => scrub.scrubServerEvent({ request: { data } }).request.data;
      const object = run({ endpoint: ENDPOINTS.fcm, keys: { p256dh: P256DH, auth: AUTH }, locale: 'he' });
      assert(show(object) === show({ locale: 'he' }), `object ${show(object)}`);
      const json = run(JSON.stringify({ Endpoint: ENDPOINTS.apple, keys: { p256dh: P256DH, auth: AUTH }, user_id: 9 }));
      assert(json === JSON.stringify({ user_id: 9 }), `JSON string ${show(json)}`);
      const form = run(`endpoint=${encodeURIComponent(ENDPOINTS.mozilla)}&p256dh=${P256DH}&auth=${AUTH}&locale=he`);
      assert(form === 'endpoint=[scrubbed]&p256dh=[scrubbed]&auth=[scrubbed]&locale=he', `form ${show(form)}`);
      const cut = run(`{"locale":"he","endpoint":"${ENDPOINTS.fcm}","keys":{"auth":"${AUTH}","p256dh":"${P256DH}","note":"a`);
      assert(pushLeaks(cut).length === 0 && String(cut).startsWith('{"locale":"he"'), `cut-off JSON ${show(cut)}`);
      for (const [name, url] of Object.entries(ENDPOINTS)) {
        const crumb = scrub.scrubServerBreadcrumb({ category: 'http', message: `POST ${url}`, data: { url, 'http.method': 'POST', status_code: 410 } });
        assert(crumb.data.url === WANT[name] && crumb.message === `POST ${WANT[name]}` && crumb.data.status_code === 410, `${name} breadcrumb ${show(crumb)}`);
        const tx = scrub.scrubServerTransaction({ type: 'transaction', transaction: `POST ${url}`, spans: [{ description: `POST ${url}`, data: { 'url.full': url } }] });
        assert(pushLeaks(tx).length === 0 && tx.transaction === `POST ${WANT[name]}` && tx.spans[0].description === `POST ${WANT[name]}`, `${name} transaction ${show(tx)}`);
        assert(scrub.scrubUrl(scrub.scrubUrl(url)) === scrub.scrubUrl(url), `${name}: a second scrub changes ${scrub.scrubUrl(url)}`);
      }
      for (const host of config.PUSH_SERVICE_HOSTS as string[]) {
        for (const url of [`https://${host}/tok-a-1`, `https://edge-1.${host}/send/tok-b-2`]) {
          assert(!scrub.scrubUrl(url).includes('tok-'), `${host} is accepted for devices, but its path reaches Sentry: ${scrub.scrubUrl(url)}`);
        }
      }
      for (const kept of ['https://example.test/fcm/send/abc', 'https://fcm.googleapis.com.example.test/fcm/send/abc', 'https://famcal.bot/api/push/devices', 'https://www.googleapis.com/calendar/v3/calendars/primary']) {
        assert(scrub.scrubUrl(kept) === kept, `${kept} was changed: ${scrub.scrubUrl(kept)}`);
      }
    });
  ```

  **1c. `scripts/smoke-pause.ts`.**

  The `Pause` type, line 104:

  ```ts
  type Pause = { isPaused: (user: PauseUser, at?: Date, skipped?: string) => Promise<boolean> };
  ```
  →
  ```ts
  type Pause = {
    isPaused: (user: PauseUser, at?: Date, skipped?: string) => Promise<boolean>;
    nextPauseStart: (user: PauseUser, from: Date, withinMs: number) => Promise<Date | null>;
  };
  ```

  After the check `'a skipped send is logged "[pause] skipped <kind> for <userId>", …'` (ends ~line 643) and before `// ============ The audit ============`, insert:

  ```ts
    // ============ The next window, for a push's lifetime (web-push/send.ts) ============

    await check('nextPauseStart: the start of the first window that begins after `from` and no later than `from` + the time asked (the end counts, `from` itself does not), at the person\'s own place, across a UTC midnight with no second computation; a span over a day is read as a day; none for the switch off (and no work); none for a failure (logged and captured, never thrown)', async () => {
      resetWorld();
      const H = 60 * 60_000;
      const iso = (d: Date | null) => (d ? d.toISOString() : null);
      const user = person(70, 'Asia/Jerusalem', JERUSALEM);
      const cases: Array<[string, Date, number, string | null]> = [
        ['an hour before sunset, an hour asked: the sunset itself', minute(FRI_SUNSET, -60), H, FRI_SUNSET],
        ['61 minutes before sunset, an hour asked: none yet', minute(FRI_SUNSET, -61), H, null],
        ['Thursday 16:00 UTC, a day asked: Friday\'s sunset, in the next UTC day', at('2026-10-15T16:00:00.000Z'), 24 * H, FRI_SUNSET],
        ['at the sunset itself: that window has begun, and none begins in the day after it', at(FRI_SUNSET), 24 * H, null],
        ['a minute after Shabbat ends, a day asked: none', minute(SAT_END, 1), 24 * H, null],
        ['Thursday 09:00 UTC, two days asked: read as one day, so none (the sunset is 30 hours away)', at('2026-10-15T09:00:00.000Z'), 48 * H, null],
      ];
      for (const [name, from, within, want] of cases) assertSame(iso(await pause.nextPauseStart(user, from, within)), want, name);

      // One computation holds `from`'s UTC day and the next: the same day's isPaused asks nothing more
      resetWorld();
      const cached = person(71, 'Asia/Jerusalem', JERUSALEM);
      assertSame(iso(await pause.nextPauseStart(cached, at('2026-10-15T16:00:00.000Z'), 24 * H)), FRI_SUNSET, 'the Thursday answer');
      const reads = redisReads;
      assert(await pause.isPaused(cached, at('2026-10-15T20:00:00.000Z')) === false && redisReads === reads, `the same day's isPaused read ${redisReads - reads} more`);

      // Each person by their own place: New York begins at its own sunset, 22:14 UTC
      const newYork = person(72, 'America/New_York', NEW_YORK);
      assertSame(iso(await pause.nextPauseStart(newYork, at('2026-10-16T21:00:00.000Z'), 2 * H)), '2026-10-16T22:14:00.000Z', 'New York');

      // The switch off: none, and no work
      resetWorld();
      for (const pauseOnShabbat of [false, undefined, null]) {
        assert(await pause.nextPauseStart(person(73, 'Asia/Jerusalem', JERUSALEM, { pauseOnShabbat }), minute(FRI_SUNSET, -30), H) === null, `pauseOnShabbat ${String(pauseOnShabbat)}`);
      }
      assert(redisReads === 0 && dbCalls === 0 && logs.length === 0 && captured.length === 0, `work done with the switch off: ${redisReads} reads, ${dbCalls} db, ${show(logs)}`);

      // A failure: logged and captured, and no window (the push keeps its own lifetime)
      resetWorld();
      const broken = person(74, 'Not/AZone', JERUSALEM);
      assert(await pause.nextPauseStart(broken, at('2026-10-16T14:00:00.000Z'), H) === null, 'a failure is no window');
      assert(logs.some(l => l.startsWith('[pause]') && l.includes('user 74')), `logged: ${show(logs)}`);
      assert(captured.some(c => c.context === 'pause-next'), `captured: ${show(captured)}`);
    });
  ```

  **3a. `src/services/web-push/payload.ts`** (new):

  ```ts
  /**
   * A browser notification's payload (spec docs/superpowers/specs/2026-11-05-web-push-design.md §3.3): one JSON for every
   * browser, in Apple's declarative format ({ web_push: 8030, notification: {...} }), which iOS 18.4+ shows even when the
   * worker was evicted, and which public/sw.js reads everywhere else. At most MAX_PAYLOAD_BYTES of UTF-8 (Firefox for
   * Android takes about 2.7 KB): a long title is cut first, never below half the room, then the body, each with "…".
   * Built at send time; never kept, logged or sent to Sentry. Server only (node:crypto).
   */

  import { createHash } from 'node:crypto';

  export const MAX_PAYLOAD_BYTES = 2048;

  export type PushLang = 'en' | 'he' | 'ru';

  export interface PushMessage {
    title: string;
    body: string;
    /** Where a tap goes: an absolute https://famcal.bot URL (the declarative format's `navigate`). */
    url: string;
    /** A newer notification with the same tag replaces the older one on the device. */
    tag: string;
    lang: PushLang;
    /** No sound or vibration (the partner's Done, as on Telegram). */
    silent?: boolean;
  }

  const ELLIPSIS = '…';
  const utf8 = (text: string) => Buffer.byteLength(text, 'utf8');

  function render(m: PushMessage, title: string, body: string): string {
    return JSON.stringify({
      web_push: 8030,
      notification: { title, body, navigate: m.url, lang: m.lang, dir: m.lang === 'he' ? 'rtl' : 'ltr', tag: m.tag, silent: m.silent === true },
    });
  }

  /** The longest start of `text` (whole characters, then "…") that `fits`; the text itself when it fits whole. */
  function cut(text: string, fits: (candidate: string) => boolean): string {
    if (fits(text)) return text;
    const chars = Array.from(text);
    const withEllipsis = (n: number) => `${chars.slice(0, n).join('').trimEnd()}${ELLIPSIS}`;
    let lo = 0;
    let hi = chars.length - 1;
    while (lo < hi) {
      const mid = Math.ceil((lo + hi) / 2);
      if (fits(withEllipsis(mid))) lo = mid;
      else hi = mid - 1;
    }
    if (lo > 0) return withEllipsis(lo);
    return fits(ELLIPSIS) ? ELLIPSIS : '';
  }

  /** The payload, its title and body cut to fit MAX_PAYLOAD_BYTES. */
  export function buildPushPayload(m: PushMessage): string {
    const fits = (title: string, body: string) => utf8(render(m, title, body)) <= MAX_PAYLOAD_BYTES;
    if (fits(m.title, m.body)) return render(m, m.title, m.body);
    // The title first, as far as the whole body needs, but never below half the room: a long list keeps its title
    const half = Math.floor((MAX_PAYLOAD_BYTES - utf8(render(m, '', ''))) / 2);
    const inJson = (text: string) => utf8(JSON.stringify(text)) - 2;
    const title = cut(m.title, (t) => fits(t, m.body) || inJson(t) <= half);
    const body = cut(m.body, (b) => fits(title, b));
    return render(m, title, body);
  }

  /**
   * The push's Topic (RFC 8030: at most 32 base64url characters): a newer push with the same topic replaces one still
   * waiting at the push service. A SHA-256 of the tag, so the push service sees no FamCal id.
   */
  export function topicFor(tag: string): string {
    return createHash('sha256').update(tag, 'utf8').digest('base64url').slice(0, 32);
  }
  ```

  `compute`, line 128:

  ```ts

  ```
  →
  ```ts

    const windows = shabbatYomTovWindows({ from: new Date(day * DAY_MS), to: new Date(endOfDay + DAY_MS) }, place, community);
  ```

  The doc of `compute` (line 101): "The windows for the UTC `day` (a day number since the epoch) and the next one, at the person's place."

  Replace `pausedAt` (lines 132-147) with:

  ```ts
  /** The cached windows for `at`'s UTC day and the next, computed again on another day, place or community, or after `until`. */
  async function windowsAt(user: PauseUser, at: Date): Promise<Array<[number, number]>> {
    const day = Math.floor(at.getTime() / DAY_MS);
    const community = isCommunity(user.zmanimCommunity) ? user.zmanimCommunity : DEFAULT_COMMUNITY;
    const key = [day, user.location ?? '', user.latitude ?? '', user.longitude ?? '', user.elevationM ?? '', community].join('|');
    let entry = cache.get(user.id);
    if (!entry || entry.key !== key || at.getTime() >= entry.until) {
      entry = { key, ...(await compute(user, at, community, day)) };
      cache.set(user.id, entry);
    }
    return entry.windows;
  }

  /**
   * Whether `at` falls inside a window at the person's place. The switch is the caller's (pause.ts).
   * Throws what the place or the windows throw; the caller reads that as "not paused".
   */
  export async function pausedAt(user: PauseUser, at: Date): Promise<boolean> {
    const t = at.getTime();
    return (await windowsAt(user, at)).some(([start, end]) => t >= start && t < end);
  }

  /**
   * The start of the first window that begins after `from` and no later than `from` + `withinMs`, or null. `withinMs` is
   * read as at most a day: the entry holds `from`'s UTC day and the next, and no push waits longer. The switch is the
   * caller's (pause.ts); throws what the place or the windows throw.
   */
  export async function pauseStartWithin(user: PauseUser, from: Date, withinMs: number): Promise<Date | null> {
    const t = from.getTime();
    const last = t + Math.min(withinMs, DAY_MS);
    const starts = (await windowsAt(user, from)).map(([start]) => start).filter((start) => start > t && start <= last);
    return starts.length > 0 ? new Date(Math.min(...starts)) : null;
  }
  ```

  After `isPaused` (line 59), append:

  ```ts

  /**
   * The start of the person's next Shabbat or Yom Tov window that begins after `from` and no later than `from` +
   * `withinMs` (read as at most a day), or null: the switch is off, no window begins in the span, or it could not be
   * told (logged and captured; the caller keeps its own limit). Never throws.
   */
  export async function nextPauseStart(user: PauseUser, from: Date, withinMs: number): Promise<Date | null> {
    if (user.pauseOnShabbat !== true || !(withinMs > 0)) return null;
    try {
      const { pauseStartWithin } = await import('./pause-windows');
      return await pauseStartWithin(user, from, withinMs);
    } catch (error) {
      console.error(`[pause] could not tell when user ${user.id}'s next Shabbat or Yom Tov begins; the push keeps its own lifetime:`, error);
      captureError(error, 'pause-next', { user_id: user.id });
      return null;
    }
  }
  ```

  **3d. `src/services/web-push/send.ts`** (new). Write `isPaused` in its comments only as `` `isPaused` `` with no "(" after it: smoke-pause's audit fails a file listed as not paused that matches `\bisPaused\(`.

  ```ts
  /**
   * The one sender of FamCal's browser notifications (spec docs/superpowers/specs/2026-11-05-web-push-design.md §3.4,
   * §3.6).
   *
   *   sendWebPush(user, input)            one notification to every device of the account: the devices read at send
   *                                       time (devicesForSend), each request built and encrypted by web-push
   *                                       (generateRequestDetails, never its own sender) and POSTed through
   *                                       fetchWithRetry, the one loop for third parties, FCM included (CLAUDE.md).
   *                                       Never throws; one log line, "[push] user <id>: <kind> sent to N of M devices"
   *   pushTtlSeconds(user, kind, now)     how long a push may wait at the push service: the kind's lifetime, an alert's
   *                                       event start, and for a scheduled kind the next Shabbat or Yom Tov window;
   *                                       never over a day
   *
   * A transport: it does not ask the pause. Each scheduled caller asks `isPaused` before it calls this
   * (scripts/smoke-pause.ts lists this file as not paused), and the lifetime makes a waiting push expire before the
   * next window opens.
   *
   * Answers: any 2xx is sent; 404 and 410 delete the device; 429, 5xx and no answer are retried by the loop, and a
   * give-up makes the result retriable; 401 and 403 (the keys don't match the subscription) are logged each time and
   * reported once a day per device; 400, 413 and any other status are a bug in our request, logged and reported.
   *
   * The VAPID header is made once an hour per push service (Apple asks for no more) and kept on globalThis (Next 16 gives
   * the scheduler and the routes separate module instances). No console line or Sentry field holds an endpoint, a key, a
   * title or a body: the user id, the kind, the device id, counts and the status. Server only.
   */

  import { generateRequestDetails, getVapidHeaders } from 'web-push';
  import { captureError } from '../../lib/error-capture';
  import { fetchWithRetry, HttpStatusError } from '../../utils/fetch-retry';
  import { SafeSignInError, safeErrorSummary } from '../web-signin/safe-error';
  import { nextPauseStart, type PauseUser } from '../zmanim/pause';
  import { vapidKeys, vapidSubject } from './config';
  import { deleteGoneDevice, devicesForSend, type StoredDevice } from './devices';
  import { buildPushPayload, topicFor, type PushMessage } from './payload';

  export type PushKind = 'reminder' | 'alert' | 'briefing' | 'partner-accepted' | 'partner-done' | 'reconnect' | 'test';

  /** How long each kind may wait at the push service for a device that is off, in seconds (spec §3.6). */
  export const PUSH_LIFETIME_S: Record<PushKind, number> = {
    reminder: 3600,
    alert: 86_400,
    briefing: 10_800,
    'partner-accepted': 86_400,
    'partner-done': 86_400,
    reconnect: 86_400,
    test: 600,
  };

  /** RFC 8030 Urgency: high for reminders and alerts, low for the partner's Done, normal for the rest (spec §3.4). */
  export const PUSH_URGENCY: Record<PushKind, 'high' | 'normal' | 'low'> = {
    reminder: 'high',
    alert: 'high',
    briefing: 'normal',
    'partner-accepted': 'normal',
    'partner-done': 'low',
    reconnect: 'normal',
    test: 'normal',
  };

  /** The scheduled kinds: their callers ask the pause, and their lifetime ends before the next window. */
  export const PAUSED_KINDS: readonly PushKind[] = ['reminder', 'alert', 'briefing', 'reconnect'];

  /** No push waits longer than a day (spec §3.6, the Policy's "within a day"). */
  export const MAX_PUSH_TTL_S = 86_400;

  /** The pause's fields of the person (their place and switch); id required. */
  export type PushUser = PauseUser;

  export interface PushSendInput extends PushMessage {
    kind: PushKind;
    /** An alert's event start: the lifetime never passes it. */
    until?: Date;
    /** Only this device (the "Notifications are on" reply goes to the browser that just turned them on). */
    onlyEndpoint?: string;
  }

  /**
   * sent: devices that took it; total: devices tried. retriable: none took it and one answered 429 or 5xx or nothing
   * (the loop gave up), or the devices could not be read: a passing failure, so a reminder's claim is let go.
   */
  export interface PushSendResult {
    sent: number;
    total: number;
    retriable: boolean;
  }

  const HOUR_MS = 3_600_000;
  const DAY_MS = 86_400_000;

  interface KeptVapid {
    authorization: string;
    publicKey: string;
    madeAt: number;
  }
  const shared = globalThis as { __pushVapidByOrigin?: Map<string, KeptVapid>; __pushAuthReportedOn?: Map<string, number> };
  /** The hour's VAPID header per push service origin. */
  const vapidByOrigin: Map<string, KeptVapid> = (shared.__pushVapidByOrigin ??= new Map());
  /** The UTC day a device's 401/403 was last reported to Sentry. */
  const authReportedOn: Map<string, number> = (shared.__pushAuthReportedOn ??= new Map());

  /** Smoke scripts only: forgets the kept VAPID headers and the once-a-day reports. */
  export function __resetPushForTests(): void {
    vapidByOrigin.clear();
    authReportedOn.clear();
  }

  export async function pushTtlSeconds(user: PushUser, kind: PushKind, now: Date, until?: Date): Promise<number> {
    let ttl = Math.min(PUSH_LIFETIME_S[kind], MAX_PUSH_TTL_S);
    if (until) ttl = Math.min(ttl, Math.floor((until.getTime() - now.getTime()) / 1000));
    if (ttl > 0 && PAUSED_KINDS.includes(kind)) {
      const start = await nextPauseStart(user, now, ttl * 1000);
      if (start) ttl = Math.min(ttl, Math.floor((start.getTime() - now.getTime()) / 1000));
    }
    return Math.max(1, ttl);
  }

  /** The hour's VAPID Authorization for this endpoint's push service, made anew after an hour or a key change. */
  function vapidAuthorization(endpoint: string, keys: { publicKey: string; privateKey: string }, nowMs: number): string {
    const origin = new URL(endpoint).origin;
    const kept = vapidByOrigin.get(origin);
    if (kept && kept.publicKey === keys.publicKey && nowMs - kept.madeAt < HOUR_MS) return kept.authorization;
    // The token is good for 12 hours (the library's default, at most 24): an hour's reuse is well inside it
    const { Authorization } = getVapidHeaders(origin, vapidSubject(), keys.publicKey, keys.privateKey, 'aes128gcm');
    vapidByOrigin.set(origin, { authorization: Authorization, publicKey: keys.publicKey, madeAt: nowMs });
    return Authorization;
  }

  type Outcome = 'sent' | 'gone' | 'retriable' | 'refused';

  async function sendToDevice(
    user: PushUser,
    kind: PushKind,
    device: StoredDevice,
    payload: string,
    options: { ttl: number; topic: string; urgency: 'high' | 'normal' | 'low' },
    keys: { publicKey: string; privateKey: string },
    now: Date,
  ): Promise<Outcome> {
    const who = `[push] user ${user.id}: ${kind} to device ${device.id}`;
    let request: { endpoint: string; headers: Record<string, string>; body: Buffer | null };
    try {
      const details = generateRequestDetails(
        { endpoint: device.endpoint, keys: { p256dh: device.p256dh, auth: device.auth } },
        payload,
        // No vapidDetails: the Authorization is the hour's kept one (with vapidDetails the library signs a token per call)
        {
          TTL: options.ttl,
          urgency: options.urgency,
          topic: options.topic,
          contentEncoding: 'aes128gcm',
          headers: { Authorization: vapidAuthorization(device.endpoint, keys, now.getTime()) },
        },
      );
      // fetch sets Content-Length from the body itself; the library gives TTL and the length as numbers
      const headers = Object.fromEntries(
        Object.entries(details.headers)
          .filter(([name]) => name.toLowerCase() !== 'content-length')
          .map(([name, value]) => [name, String(value)]),
      );
      request = { endpoint: details.endpoint, headers, body: details.body };
    } catch (error) {
      // web-push's own messages name the problem (a key's length, the subject), never a key or the endpoint
      const why = error instanceof Error ? error.message : safeErrorSummary(error);
      console.error(`${who} could not be built: ${why}`);
      captureError(new SafeSignInError(`push-send: ${why}`), 'push-send', { user_id: user.id, kind, device_id: device.id, purpose: 'build' });
      return 'refused';
    }
    try {
      const response = await fetchWithRetry(
        request.endpoint,
        { method: 'POST', headers: request.headers, body: request.body ? new Uint8Array(request.body) : undefined },
        {
          label: who,
          // A refusal is told below; a give-up of 429, 5xx or no answer is told here
          onGiveUp: (error, { why }) => {
            if (why !== 'not retryable') console.warn(`${who} not delivered (${why}): ${safeErrorSummary(error)}`);
          },
        },
      );
      // The answer's body is of no use: free the connection
      response.body?.cancel().catch((error: unknown) => console.warn(`${who}: could not drop the answer's body: ${safeErrorSummary(error)}`));
      return 'sent';
    } catch (error) {
      const status = error instanceof HttpStatusError ? error.status : null;
      if (status === 404 || status === 410) {
        console.log(`${who}: the push service says it is gone (${status}); deleting the device`);
        await deleteGoneDevice(device.id);
        return 'gone';
      }
      if (status === null || status === 429 || status >= 500) return 'retriable';
      if (status === 401 || status === 403) {
        console.error(`${who}: refused (${status}): the VAPID keys don't match this device's subscription`);
        const day = Math.floor(now.getTime() / DAY_MS);
        if (authReportedOn.get(device.id) !== day) {
          authReportedOn.set(device.id, day);
          captureError(new SafeSignInError(`push-send: HTTP ${status}`), 'push-send', { user_id: user.id, kind, device_id: device.id, status });
        }
        return 'refused';
      }
      console.error(`${who}: refused (${status}): the request is wrong`);
      captureError(new SafeSignInError(`push-send: HTTP ${status}`), 'push-send', { user_id: user.id, kind, device_id: device.id, status });
      return 'refused';
    }
  }

  /** Sends one notification to every device of the account (see the header). Never throws. */
  export async function sendWebPush(user: PushUser, input: PushSendInput, now: Date = new Date()): Promise<PushSendResult> {
    const { kind } = input;
    const keys = vapidKeys();
    if (!keys) {
      console.log(`[push] user ${user.id}: ${kind} not sent: browser notifications are off (no VAPID keys)`);
      return { sent: 0, total: 0, retriable: false };
    }
    try {
      const all = await devicesForSend(user.id);
      const devices = input.onlyEndpoint === undefined ? all : all.filter((d) => d.endpoint === input.onlyEndpoint);
      if (devices.length === 0) {
        console.log(`[push] user ${user.id}: ${kind} sent to 0 of 0 devices`);
        return { sent: 0, total: 0, retriable: false };
      }
      const ttl = await pushTtlSeconds(user, kind, now, input.until);
      const payload = buildPushPayload(input);
      const options = { ttl, topic: topicFor(input.tag), urgency: PUSH_URGENCY[kind] };
      const outcomes = await Promise.all(devices.map((device) => sendToDevice(user, kind, device, payload, options, keys, now)));
      const sent = outcomes.filter((outcome) => outcome === 'sent').length;
      console.log(`[push] user ${user.id}: ${kind} sent to ${sent} of ${devices.length} devices`);
      return { sent, total: devices.length, retriable: sent === 0 && outcomes.includes('retriable') };
    } catch (error) {
      // The device read (the database) or a bug: a passing failure, told by its kind only
      console.error(`[push] user ${user.id}: ${kind} not sent: ${safeErrorSummary(error)}`);
      captureError(new SafeSignInError(`push-send: ${safeErrorSummary(error)}`), 'push-send', { user_id: user.id, kind });
      return { sent: 0, total: 0, retriable: true };
    }
  }
  ```

  How to confirm the library behaves as assumed, after Task 1's install: read `node_modules/web-push/src/web-push-lib.js` `generateRequestDetails`. With no `vapidDetails` option and no `setVapidDetails` call, `currentVapidDetails` is undefined, the endpoint isn't `android.googleapis.com/gcm/send`, and the GCM key is `''`, so the `Authorization` from `options.headers` is the one sent. `options.headers` may not repeat a top-level option name (`TTL`, `urgency`, `topic`): only `Authorization` goes there. W3 and W5 prove it: the JWT verifies against our key, and it stays the same for the hour.

  ```ts
  export async function pushPropsFor(user: { id: number; telegramId: number | bigint | null | undefined; whatsappPhone?: string | null; whatsappBsuid?: string | null }, session: PageSession): Promise<PushPageProps | null>;
  // src/services/web-push/request.ts (server only)

  export type PushFront = { ok: true; auth: PushSessionAuth } | { ok: false; response: NextResponse };
  export async function pushFront(request: NextRequest, tag: string, opts: { needsPushOn: boolean }): Promise<PushFront>;
  export async function readPushBody(request: NextRequest, tag: string): Promise<unknown>;
  export function endpointOf(body: unknown): string | null;
  // src/lib/page-data.ts

  // DashboardPageProps gains   push: PushPageProps | null
  ```
