- The wire contract Task 5's client codes against. All three requests are `fetch(url, { method, credentials: 'same-origin', headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' }, body })` with `url` = `/api/push/devices?user_id=<the page's userId, the DB id>` (check: `/api/push/devices/check?user_id=...`). `Content-Type: application/json` is required (415 `{ error: 'json_only' }` otherwise); the browser adds `Origin` itself.
  - `POST /api/push/devices`, body `{ endpoint, keys: { p256dh, auth } }` (a `PushSubscription.toJSON()`). 200 `{ ok: true, count }`. 400 `{ ok: false, error: 'bad-input' }`. 403 `{ ok: false, error: 'stale-sign-in' }` (plus `whatsapp: true` for a WhatsApp link's session). 409 `{ ok: false, error: 'has-chat' }`. 503 `{ ok: false, error: 'push-off' }`. 500 `{ ok: false, error: 'failed' }`. Also, from the shared front: 401 `{ error }` (no session, or the wrong `user_id`, or a Telegram initData request), 403 `{ error: 'cross_site' }`, 403 `{ ok: false, error: 'browser-only' }` (cannot happen: the routes pass no initData), 415, and 429 `{ error: 'Too many requests. Please wait a minute.' }` (no `ok` field).
  - `POST /api/push/devices/check`, body `{ endpoint }`. 200 `{ ok: true, mine: boolean }` (another account's endpoint and an unknown one both say `mine: false`, byte for byte). 400, 503, 500 and the shared refusals as above.
  - `DELETE /api/push/devices`, body `{ endpoint }`. 200 `{ ok: true }` whether or not a row was removed (only the caller's own is). 400 `bad-input`. It works while push is off and asks for no recent sign-in.
- Spec points decided here, for the controller: (1) the test notification goes through `sendWebPush`, which sends to every device of the account, so a second device turned on shows "Notifications are on" on the first too (the spec says "that device"; `PushSendInput` has no device filter, and adding one is Task 2's file); (2) `DELETE` answers even while push is off (the spec says the routes answer 503 while off; a device must always be removable, and none can have been added while off); (3) `countDevices` is added here because the page props need a count and `devices.ts` is the only file that may read the table; Task 7 may replace it with `pushDeviceCountOf(user)` once the user selects carry `_count`; (4) a re-subscribe of an endpoint the account already has sends the notification again.

- [ ] **Step 1: Write the failing test**

Create `scripts/smoke-push-routes.ts` with exactly this content. It stands in for `devices.ts` and `send.ts` through `stubModule` (the sign-in world's own mechanism), so it tests the routes and the page props against the contract, not against Tasks 1 and 2's internals; those have their own smokes. (Its stand-in validator accepts any `https://` endpoint with base64url keys of the contract's lengths; Task 1's real one also demands a known push-service host, which is why the routes' 400 is only exercised here with bodies that fail on any rule.)

```ts
// Smoke test for the notification-device routes and the `push` page prop (web push spec 2026-11-05 §3.2; plan 6c-2 Task 3):
// POST/DELETE /api/push/devices, POST /api/push/devices/check, and src/services/web-push/page-props.ts.
//
// What it pins:
//   - subscribe: a new endpoint needs a sign-in from the last hour (403 stale-sign-in, nothing stored); an endpoint the
//     account already has does not; one another account holds moves here (with the hour) and leaves the other account
//     without it; an account with a chat (Telegram, WhatsApp) is refused 409 has-chat; a bad body is 400 and stores
//     nothing; only the three fields are handed on; a stored device is followed, after the answer, by one "Notifications
//     are on" notification in the account's language (kind 'test', the home's absolute URL), and a failed send is captured
//     without touching the answer;
//   - the front of all three routes: another origin or a body that is not JSON is refused first, no cookie is 401, a
//     Telegram initData request is 401 (a Mini App is refused), push off (no VAPID keys) is 503 for subscribe and check
//     while an unsubscribe still works, and the 21st request of an account's minute is 429 under the push limiter keyed by
//     the proved account;
//   - check: true only for an endpoint this account holds; another account's endpoint and an unknown one get the same
//     answer byte for byte; it asks for no recent sign-in;
//   - unsubscribe: removes only the caller's own device, answers the same for another account's endpoint and for none, and
//     asks for no recent sign-in;
//   - no log line, capture or answer holds an endpoint or a key (a store that fails with the endpoint in its message is
//     captured by its kind only);
//   - the `push` prop: null for Telegram's initData and for an account with a chat; for a no-chat session its key (null while
//     push is off, and then no devices are counted), whether the sign-in is within the hour, and its device count; a failed
//     count is captured and reads as none;
//   - wiring: page-data hands both props the session's proved-at, the page-data route passes it, SettingsClient takes
//     the prop, the limiter is 20 a minute, bot.push is in en, he and ru with no emoji.
//
// Real: the three routes, request.ts, page-props.ts, verifyUserAuth, the session cookie, the helper for the hour
// (recent-sign-in.ts), config.ts, bot-messages and the messages files. Faked at the boundaries (scripts/lib/signin-world.ts):
// Prisma and Redis in memory, the rate limiter (this smoke's own, which records and can refuse), error capture, Next's
// after(). Stood in for, because they are other tasks' modules: devices.ts (an in-memory store with the contract's
// signatures and a validator that follows the contract) and send.ts (records the notifications). No network, no DB.
//
// Run with the fake env of scripts/ci-smokes.sh (the plan's $RUN runner):
//   $RUN smoke-push-routes

import fs from 'fs';
import { createRequire } from 'module';
import path from 'path';
import {
  assert, baseUser, body, check, DB, drainAfter, everyCapture, everyLog, initDataFor, installSigninWorld, load, report,
  request, resetWorld, ROOT, sessionCookie, show, stubModule, type Row,
} from './lib/signin-world';

const nodeRequire = createRequire(__filename);
const HOUR = 3_600_000;
const BASE = 'https://famcal.bot';

const DANA = 50; // no chat, English
const NOAM = 51; // no chat, Hebrew
const RUTH = 52; // no chat, Russian
const TAL = 9; // Telegram
const TAL_TG = 111111111;
const WANDA = 60; // WhatsApp phone
const BOAZ = 61; // WhatsApp BSUID

process.env.VAPID_PUBLIC_KEY = 'smoke-public-key-not-a-real-one';
process.env.VAPID_PRIVATE_KEY = 'smoke-private-key-not-a-real-one';

// ---- The stand-ins for Task 1's devices.ts and Task 2's send.ts ----

interface Device { userId: number; endpoint: string; p256dh: string; auth: string }
const devices: Device[] = [];
const addCalls: { userId: number; input: Row }[] = [];
let addFails: Error | null = null;
let countFails = false;
let countCalls = 0;
const sends: { userId: number; input: Row }[] = [];
let sendFails = false;
/** The limiter's calls: [limiter name, identifier], and whether the next ones are refused. */
const limiterCalls: [string, string][] = [];
let limited = false;

const B64URL = /^[A-Za-z0-9_-]+$/;
/** The contract's validator: an https endpoint of at most 1024 characters, keys base64url (p256dh 80-100, auth 16-32). */
function isValidDeviceInput(x: unknown): boolean {
  const o = x as { endpoint?: unknown; keys?: { p256dh?: unknown; auth?: unknown } } | null;
  return typeof o?.endpoint === 'string' && o.endpoint.startsWith('https://') && o.endpoint.length <= 1024
    && typeof o.keys?.p256dh === 'string' && B64URL.test(o.keys.p256dh) && o.keys.p256dh.length >= 80 && o.keys.p256dh.length <= 100
    && typeof o.keys.auth === 'string' && B64URL.test(o.keys.auth) && o.keys.auth.length >= 16 && o.keys.auth.length <= 32;
}

let seq = 0;
const P256DH = 'B'.repeat(87);
const AUTH = 'a'.repeat(22);
const fresh = (): { endpoint: string; keys: { p256dh: string; auth: string } } => ({
  endpoint: `https://push.example.test/send/secret-endpoint-token-${++seq}`,
  keys: { p256dh: `${P256DH.slice(0, 80)}${String(seq).padStart(7, 'k')}`, auth: `${AUTH.slice(0, 16)}${String(seq).padStart(6, 'x')}` },
});
/** Every endpoint and key value this run made, so the end can check that none reached a log line or a capture. */
const secrets = new Set<string>();
const mint = () => {
  const d = fresh();
  secrets.add(d.endpoint).add(d.keys.p256dh).add(d.keys.auth);
  return d;
};

const MESSAGES: Record<string, Row> = Object.fromEntries(
  ['en', 'he', 'ru'].map(l => [l, JSON.parse(fs.readFileSync(path.join(ROOT, `messages/${l}.json`), 'utf8'))]),
);
const EMOJI = /\p{Extended_Pictographic}/u;

function seed(): void {
  resetWorld();
  devices.length = 0;
  addCalls.length = 0;
  sends.length = 0;
  limiterCalls.length = 0;
  addFails = null;
  countFails = false;
  countCalls = 0;
  sendFails = false;
  limited = false;
  process.env.VAPID_PUBLIC_KEY = 'smoke-public-key-not-a-real-one';
  process.env.VAPID_PRIVATE_KEY = 'smoke-private-key-not-a-real-one';
  DB.user = [
    baseUser({ id: DANA, name: 'Dana', language: 'en' }),
    baseUser({ id: NOAM, name: 'Noam', language: 'he' }),
    baseUser({ id: RUTH, name: 'Ruth', language: 'ru' }),
    baseUser({ id: TAL, name: 'Tal', language: 'en', telegramId: BigInt(TAL_TG) }),
    baseUser({ id: WANDA, name: 'Wanda', messagingPlatform: 'whatsapp', whatsappPhone: '+972500000060' }),
    baseUser({ id: BOAZ, name: 'Boaz', messagingPlatform: 'whatsapp', whatsappBsuid: 'IL.1234567890' }),
  ];
}

/** How many notifications went (a function, so the compiler does not narrow the array's length across the awaits). */
const sentCount = (): number => sends.length;
const own = (userId: number): string[] => devices.filter(d => d.userId === userId).map(d => d.endpoint);
const url = (userId: number, tail = '') => `/api/push/devices${tail}?user_id=${userId}`;

async function main() {
  await installSigninWorld();
  // The push limiter, as this smoke's own: records [name, identifier] and refuses on demand (the world's allows everything)
  const limiter = (name: string) => ({ name });
  stubModule('src/lib/rate-limit.ts', {
    commandRateLimiter: limiter('command'), settingsRateLimiter: limiter('settings'), generalRateLimiter: limiter('general'),
    tokenPollRateLimiter: limiter('token-poll'), calendarRateLimiter: limiter('calendar'), reminderActionRateLimiter: limiter('reminder-action'),
    calendarWriteRateLimiter: limiter('calendar-write'), pushDeviceRateLimiter: limiter('push-device'),
    checkRateLimit: async (l: { name: string }, id: string) => {
      limiterCalls.push([l.name, String(id)]);
      return { success: !limited, limit: 20, remaining: limited ? 0 : 19, reset: 0 };
    },
    getRateLimitHeaders: () => ({}),
  });
  stubModule('src/services/web-push/devices.ts', {
    MAX_DEVICES: 10,
    isValidDeviceInput,
    addDevice: async (userId: number, input: Row) => {
      addCalls.push({ userId, input });
      if (addFails) throw addFails;
      const held = devices.find(d => d.endpoint === input.endpoint);
      const moved = held !== undefined && held.userId !== userId;
      if (held) {
        held.userId = userId;
        held.p256dh = input.keys.p256dh;
        held.auth = input.keys.auth;
      } else {
        devices.push({ userId, endpoint: input.endpoint, p256dh: input.keys.p256dh, auth: input.keys.auth });
      }
      return { moved, count: own(userId).length };
    },
    isOwnDevice: async (userId: number, endpoint: string) => devices.some(d => d.userId === userId && d.endpoint === endpoint),
    deleteOwnDevice: async (userId: number, endpoint: string) => {
      const i = devices.findIndex(d => d.userId === userId && d.endpoint === endpoint);
      if (i >= 0) devices.splice(i, 1);
      return i >= 0;
    },
    countDevices: async (userId: number) => {
      countCalls++;
      if (countFails) throw new Error('the count failed (smoke)');
      return own(userId).length;
    },
  });
  stubModule('src/services/web-push/send.ts', {
    sendWebPush: async (user: Row, input: Row) => {
      if (sendFails) throw new Error('the push service is down (smoke)');
      sends.push({ userId: user.id, input });
      return { sent: own(user.id).length, total: own(user.id).length, retriable: false };
    },
  });
  const post = await load('app/api/push/devices/route.ts');
  const checkRoute = await load('app/api/push/devices/check/route.ts');
  const { pushPropsFor } = await load('src/services/web-push/page-props.ts');
  const { NextRequest } = nodeRequire('next/server');

  const cookieOf = (userId: number, method = 'email', ageMs = 60_000) => sessionCookie(userId, method, Date.now() - ageMs);
  const subscribe = async (userId: number, d: Row, o: { cookie?: string; ageMs?: number; method?: string } = {}) =>
    post.POST(request(url(userId), { body: d, cookie: o.cookie ?? await cookieOf(userId, o.method, o.ageMs) }));
  const check_ = async (userId: number, endpoint: unknown, ageMs?: number) =>
    checkRoute.POST(request(url(userId, '/check'), { body: { endpoint }, cookie: await cookieOf(userId, 'email', ageMs) }));
  const unsubscribe = async (userId: number, endpoint: unknown, ageMs?: number) =>
    post.DELETE(request(url(userId), { method: 'DELETE', body: { endpoint }, cookie: await cookieOf(userId, 'email', ageMs) }));

  await check('subscribe: a no-chat account with a fresh session stores the device and answers { ok, count }; only the three fields are handed on, and it is one more device each time', async () => {
    seed();
    const d = mint();
    const res = await subscribe(DANA, { ...d, evil: 'x', keys: { ...d.keys, extra: 1 } });
    assert(res.status === 200 && show(await body(res)) === show({ ok: true, count: 1 }), `${res.status} ${show(await body(res))}`);
    assert(show(addCalls[0]) === show({ userId: DANA, input: { endpoint: d.endpoint, keys: { p256dh: d.keys.p256dh, auth: d.keys.auth } } }), `handed on: ${show(addCalls[0])}`);
    const second = mint();
    const again = await subscribe(DANA, second);
    assert(show(await body(again)) === show({ ok: true, count: 2 }), `second: ${show(await body(again))}`);
    assert(own(DANA).length === 2, 'two devices');
  });

  await check('subscribe: after the answer, one "Notifications are on" notification (kind test, the account\'s language, the home\'s absolute URL); nothing is sent before the answer is made', async () => {
    seed();
    for (const [id, lang, prefix] of [[DANA, 'en', '/en'], [NOAM, 'he', '/he'], [RUTH, 'ru', '/ru']] as const) {
      sends.length = 0;
      const res = await subscribe(id, mint());
      assert(res.status === 200, `${lang}: ${res.status}`);
      assert(sentCount() === 0, `${lang}: a notification went before the after() task ran`);
      await drainAfter();
      assert(sentCount() === 1, `${lang}: ${sentCount()} notifications`);
      const { userId, input } = sends[0];
      const bot = MESSAGES[lang].bot.push;
      assert(userId === id && input.kind === 'test' && input.lang === lang, `${lang}: ${show(sends[0])}`);
      assert(input.title === bot.turnedOn && input.body === bot.turnedOnBody, `${lang}: the words ${show(input)}`);
      assert(input.url === `${BASE}${prefix}/dashboard?user_id=${id}`, `${lang}: the url ${input.url}`);
      assert(typeof input.tag === 'string' && input.tag.length > 0 && input.silent === undefined, `${lang}: the tag ${show(input)}`);
      assert(typeof input.onlyEndpoint === 'string' && input.onlyEndpoint.startsWith('https://'), `${lang}: the reply goes to every device ${show(input)}`);
    }
  });

  await check('subscribe: a notification that fails to send is captured (push-turned-on, a warning) and the answer was already 200', async () => {
    seed();
    sendFails = true;
    const before = everyCapture.length;
    const res = await subscribe(DANA, mint());
    assert(res.status === 200, `${res.status}`);
    await drainAfter();
    const caught = everyCapture.slice(before);
    assert(caught.length === 1 && caught[0].context === 'push-turned-on' && caught[0].extra.user_id === DANA, `captured: ${show(caught)}`);
  });

  await check('subscribe: a new endpoint with a session proved over an hour ago is 403 stale-sign-in and nothing is stored or sent; an endpoint the account already has needs no hour (200, and no second device)', async () => {
    seed();
    const d = mint();
    const stale = await subscribe(DANA, d, { ageMs: 3 * HOUR });
    assert(stale.status === 403 && show(await body(stale)) === show({ ok: false, error: 'stale-sign-in' }), `${stale.status} ${show(await body(stale))}`);
    await drainAfter();
    assert(devices.length === 0 && addCalls.length === 0 && sends.length === 0, 'something was stored or sent');
    await subscribe(DANA, d);
    await drainAfter();
    assert(own(DANA).length === 1, 'the fresh sign-in stored it');
    const re = await subscribe(DANA, d, { ageMs: 3 * HOUR });
    assert(re.status === 200 && show(await body(re)) === show({ ok: true, count: 1 }), `re-subscribe: ${re.status} ${show(await body(re))}`);
    // Just inside the hour passes, as the helper's own edge says (smoke-recent-sign-in)
    const near = await subscribe(NOAM, mint(), { ageMs: HOUR - 60_000 });
    assert(near.status === 200, `59 minutes: ${near.status}`);
  });

  await check('subscribe: an endpoint another account holds moves here with a fresh sign-in (the other account loses it); with a stale one it is refused and the other account keeps it', async () => {
    seed();
    const d = mint();
    await subscribe(NOAM, d);
    const stale = await subscribe(DANA, d, { ageMs: 2 * HOUR });
    assert(stale.status === 403 && own(NOAM).length === 1 && own(DANA).length === 0, `stale: ${stale.status} ${show(devices)}`);
    const lines = everyLog.length;
    const moved = await subscribe(DANA, d);
    assert(moved.status === 200 && own(DANA).length === 1 && own(NOAM).length === 0, `moved: ${moved.status} ${show(devices)}`);
    assert(everyLog.slice(lines).some(l => l === `[push] user ${DANA}: device moved here from another account (1 on this account)`), `the line: ${show(everyLog.slice(lines))}`);
  });

  await check('subscribe: an account with a chat is refused 409 has-chat before anything is read or stored (Telegram, a WhatsApp phone, a WhatsApp BSUID), and the line says why', async () => {
    seed();
    for (const [id, method] of [[TAL, 'tg'], [WANDA, 'wa'], [BOAZ, 'wa']] as const) {
      const lines = everyLog.length;
      const res = await subscribe(id, mint(), { method });
      assert(res.status === 409 && show(await body(res)) === show({ ok: false, error: 'has-chat' }), `${id}: ${res.status} ${show(await body(res))}`);
      assert(everyLog.slice(lines).includes(`[push] user ${id}: subscribe refused: the account has a chat`), `${id}: ${show(everyLog.slice(lines))}`);
    }
    assert(devices.length === 0 && addCalls.length === 0, 'a device was stored');
  });

  await check('subscribe: a body that is not a device (not JSON, empty, an endpoint that is not a string, no keys) is 400 bad-input and stores nothing', async () => {
    seed();
    const cookie = await cookieOf(DANA);
    const raw = new NextRequest(`${BASE}${url(DANA)}`, { method: 'POST', headers: { 'content-type': 'application/json', origin: BASE, cookie }, body: '{not json' });
    const cases: Row[] = [{ body: {}, cookie }, { body: { endpoint: 5, keys: {} }, cookie }, { body: { endpoint: 'https://x.example/a' }, cookie }, { body: { endpoint: 'http://push.example.test/x', keys: { p256dh: P256DH, auth: AUTH } }, cookie }];
    const answers = [await post.POST(raw), ...(await Promise.all(cases.map(c => post.POST(request(url(DANA), c)))))];
    for (const res of answers) assert(res.status === 400 && show(await body(res)) === show({ ok: false, error: 'bad-input' }), `${res.status} ${show(await body(res))}`);
    assert(devices.length === 0 && addCalls.length === 0, 'something was stored');
  });

  await check('the front: another origin or none is 403 cross_site and a body that is not JSON is 415, each before the limiter or the session is asked; no cookie is 401; the wrong account\'s id is 401', async () => {
    seed();
    const cookie = await cookieOf(DANA);
    const d = mint();
    for (const route of [(r: Row) => post.POST(r), (r: Row) => checkRoute.POST(r), (r: Row) => post.DELETE(r)]) {
      const none = await route(request(url(DANA), { body: d, cookie, origin: null }));
      assert(none.status === 403 && (await body(none)).error === 'cross_site', `no origin: ${none.status}`);
      const other = await route(request(url(DANA), { body: d, cookie, origin: 'https://evil.example' }));
      assert(other.status === 403 && (await body(other)).error === 'cross_site', `other origin: ${other.status}`);
      const form = await route(new NextRequest(`${BASE}${url(DANA)}`, { method: 'POST', headers: { 'content-type': 'text/plain', origin: BASE, cookie }, body: JSON.stringify(d) }));
      assert(form.status === 415 && (await body(form)).error === 'json_only', `not JSON: ${form.status}`);
    }
    assert(limiterCalls.length === 0, `the limiter was asked before the origin check: ${show(limiterCalls)}`);
    const anon = await post.POST(request(url(DANA), { body: d }));
    assert(anon.status === 401, `no cookie: ${anon.status}`);
    const wrong = await post.POST(request(url(NOAM), { body: d, cookie }));
    assert(wrong.status === 401, `another account's id: ${wrong.status}`);
    assert(devices.length === 0, 'something was stored');
  });

  await check('the front: a Telegram initData request is refused (401) on all three routes, even from an account that exists, and nothing is stored; initData next to a valid cookie is ignored (the cookie still proves the person)', async () => {
    seed();
    const initData = initDataFor(TAL_TG);
    const d = mint();
    for (const [route, tail, method] of [[post.POST, '', 'POST'], [checkRoute.POST, '/check', 'POST'], [post.DELETE, '', 'DELETE']] as const) {
      const res = await route(request(url(TAL, tail), { method, body: d, initData }));
      assert(res.status === 401, `${method}${tail}: ${res.status}`);
    }
    assert(devices.length === 0, 'something was stored');
    const both = await post.POST(request(url(DANA), { body: mint(), cookie: await cookieOf(DANA), initData }));
    assert(both.status === 200, `a cookie with initData: ${both.status}`);
  });

  await check('push off (no VAPID keys): subscribe and check are 503 push-off, nothing stored; an unsubscribe still removes the device', async () => {
    seed();
    const d = mint();
    await subscribe(DANA, d);
    process.env.VAPID_PRIVATE_KEY = '';
    const sub = await subscribe(DANA, mint());
    assert(sub.status === 503 && show(await body(sub)) === show({ ok: false, error: 'push-off' }), `subscribe: ${sub.status} ${show(await body(sub))}`);
    const chk = await check_(DANA, d.endpoint);
    assert(chk.status === 503 && (await body(chk)).error === 'push-off', `check: ${chk.status}`);
    assert(own(DANA).length === 1, 'a device was added while off');
    const del = await unsubscribe(DANA, d.endpoint);
    assert(del.status === 200 && own(DANA).length === 0, `unsubscribe: ${del.status} ${show(devices)}`);
    process.env.VAPID_PRIVATE_KEY = 'smoke-private-key-not-a-real-one';
  });

  await check('the limiter: all three routes ask the push limiter keyed by the proved account (u<id>), and a spent bucket is 429 before anything is read or stored', async () => {
    seed();
    const d = mint();
    await subscribe(DANA, d);
    await check_(DANA, d.endpoint);
    await unsubscribe(DANA, d.endpoint);
    assert(show(limiterCalls) === show([['push-device', `u${DANA}`], ['push-device', `u${DANA}`], ['push-device', `u${DANA}`]]), `the limiter's calls: ${show(limiterCalls)}`);
    limited = true;
    const e = mint();
    const lines = everyLog.length;
    for (const res of [await subscribe(DANA, e), await check_(DANA, e.endpoint), await unsubscribe(DANA, e.endpoint)]) assert(res.status === 429, `${res.status}`);
    assert(devices.length === 0, 'a limited request stored something');
    assert(show(everyLog.slice(lines)) === show(['[push] push-devices refused: too many requests', '[push] push-devices-check refused: too many requests', '[push] push-devices-delete refused: too many requests']), `the lines: ${show(everyLog.slice(lines))}`);
    const source = fs.readFileSync(path.join(ROOT, 'src/lib/rate-limit.ts'), 'utf8');
    assert(/export const pushDeviceRateLimiter = new Ratelimit\(\{[^}]*slidingWindow\(20, '1 m'\)[^}]*prefix: 'ratelimit:push-device'/s.test(source), 'rate-limit.ts: the limiter is not 20 a minute under its own prefix');
  });

  await check('check: true for this account\'s own endpoint; false for another account\'s and for an unknown one, and those two answers are the same bytes; a session over an hour old is fine', async () => {
    seed();
    const mine = mint();
    const theirs = mint();
    await subscribe(DANA, mine);
    await subscribe(NOAM, theirs);
    const a = await check_(DANA, mine.endpoint, 5 * HOUR);
    assert(a.status === 200 && show(await body(a)) === show({ ok: true, mine: true }), `own: ${a.status} ${show(await body(a))}`);
    const other = await check_(DANA, theirs.endpoint);
    const unknown = await check_(DANA, 'https://push.example.test/send/nobody-has-this');
    assert(other.status === 200 && unknown.status === 200, `${other.status} ${unknown.status}`);
    assert((await other.clone().text()) === (await unknown.clone().text()) && show(await body(other)) === show({ ok: true, mine: false }), `another's ${await other.clone().text()} vs unknown ${await unknown.clone().text()}`);
    for (const bad of [undefined, 5, '', 'x'.repeat(1025)]) {
      const res = await check_(DANA, bad);
      assert(res.status === 400 && (await body(res)).error === 'bad-input', `${show(bad)?.slice(0, 20)}: ${res.status}`);
    }
  });

  await check('unsubscribe: removes only the caller\'s own device; another account\'s endpoint and an unknown one change nothing and answer 200 { ok: true } like a real removal; a stale session may remove; a bad body is 400', async () => {
    seed();
    const mine = mint();
    const theirs = mint();
    await subscribe(DANA, mine);
    await subscribe(NOAM, theirs);
    const lines = everyLog.length;
    const foreign = await unsubscribe(DANA, theirs.endpoint);
    assert(foreign.status === 200 && show(await body(foreign)) === show({ ok: true }) && own(NOAM).length === 1, `another account's: ${foreign.status} ${show(devices.map(d => d.userId))}`);
    const none = await unsubscribe(DANA, 'https://push.example.test/send/none');
    assert(none.status === 200 && show(await body(none)) === show({ ok: true }), `unknown: ${none.status}`);
    const gone = await unsubscribe(DANA, mine.endpoint, 9 * HOUR);
    assert(gone.status === 200 && show(await body(gone)) === show({ ok: true }) && own(DANA).length === 0, `own, stale session: ${gone.status} ${show(devices.map(d => d.userId))}`);
    assert(everyLog.slice(lines).filter(l => l.startsWith('[push]')).join('|') === `[push] user ${DANA}: device not found; nothing removed|[push] user ${DANA}: device not found; nothing removed|[push] user ${DANA}: device removed`, `lines: ${show(everyLog.slice(lines))}`);
    for (const bad of [undefined, 7, '']) {
      const res = await unsubscribe(DANA, bad);
      assert(res.status === 400 && (await body(res)).error === 'bad-input', `${show(bad)}: ${res.status}`);
    }
  });

  await check('a store that fails with the endpoint in its message is 500 failed, captured by its kind only (no endpoint, key or message), and nothing leaks into the answer', async () => {
    seed();
    const d = mint();
    addFails = Object.assign(new Error(`Invalid prisma.pushDevice.upsert() invocation: endpoint: "${d.endpoint}" p256dh: "${d.keys.p256dh}"`), { code: 'P2002' });
    const before = everyCapture.length;
    const res = await subscribe(DANA, d);
    assert(res.status === 500 && show(await body(res)) === show({ ok: false, error: 'failed' }), `${res.status} ${show(await body(res))}`);
    const caught = everyCapture.slice(before);
    assert(caught.length === 1 && caught[0].context === 'push-devices' && caught[0].message === 'push-devices: Error P2002' && caught[0].extra.user_id === DANA, `captured: ${show(caught)}`);
    await drainAfter();
    assert(sends.length === 0, 'a notification went after a failed store');
  });

  await check('pushPropsFor: null for Telegram\'s initData (no session) and for every account with a chat; a no-chat session gets the key, whether it was proved within the hour, and the device count', async () => {
    seed();
    const chatty = [{ id: TAL, telegramId: BigInt(TAL_TG) }, { id: WANDA, telegramId: null, whatsappPhone: '+972500000060' }, { id: BOAZ, telegramId: null, whatsappBsuid: 'IL.1' }];
    for (const user of chatty) assert((await pushPropsFor(user, { provedAt: Date.now() })) === null, `${user.id}: a chat account got props`);
    assert((await pushPropsFor({ id: DANA, telegramId: null }, null)) === null, 'initData got props');
    assert(countCalls === 0, 'devices were counted for a null prop');
    await subscribe(DANA, mint());
    await subscribe(DANA, mint());
    const now = Date.now();
    const props = await pushPropsFor({ id: DANA, telegramId: null }, { provedAt: now - 5 * 60_000 });
    assert(show(props) === show({ key: 'smoke-public-key-not-a-real-one', recentSignIn: true, devices: 2 }), `${show(props)}`);
    const old = await pushPropsFor({ id: DANA, telegramId: null }, { provedAt: now - 2 * HOUR });
    assert(old?.recentSignIn === false && old.devices === 2, `old: ${show(old)}`);
    const none = await pushPropsFor({ id: NOAM, telegramId: null, whatsappPhone: null, whatsappBsuid: null }, { provedAt: now });
    assert(none?.devices === 0 && none.key !== null, `no devices: ${show(none)}`);
  });

  await check('pushPropsFor: while push is off the key is null and no devices are counted (no query); a count that fails is captured (push-page-props) and reads as no devices', async () => {
    seed();
    process.env.VAPID_PUBLIC_KEY = '  ';
    const off = await pushPropsFor({ id: DANA, telegramId: null }, { provedAt: Date.now() });
    assert(show(off) === show({ key: null, recentSignIn: true, devices: 0 }) && countCalls === 0, `off: ${show(off)} counted ${countCalls}`);
    process.env.VAPID_PUBLIC_KEY = 'smoke-public-key-not-a-real-one';
    countFails = true;
    const before = everyCapture.length;
    const failed = await pushPropsFor({ id: DANA, telegramId: null }, { provedAt: Date.now() });
    assert(failed?.devices === 0 && failed.key !== null, `failed: ${show(failed)}`);
    const caught = everyCapture.slice(before);
    assert(caught.length === 1 && caught[0].context === 'push-page-props' && caught[0].extra.user_id === DANA, `captured: ${show(caught)}`);
  });

  await check('wiring: page-data hands the dashboard and Settings props the push prop from the session, the page-data route passes the proved-at of a session (null for initData), SettingsClient takes the prop, and the three routes share one front', () => {
    const read = (rel: string) => fs.readFileSync(path.join(ROOT, rel), 'utf8');
    const pageData = read('src/lib/page-data.ts');
    assert((pageData.match(/pushPropsFor\(user, session\)/g) ?? []).length === 2, 'page-data: pushPropsFor(user, session) is not asked by both pages');
    assert(/return dashboard\(user, urlUserId, locale, session\)/.test(pageData) && /return settings\(user, urlUserId, userLocale, session\)/.test(pageData), 'page-data: the session is not passed on');
    assert((pageData.match(/^\s+push,$/gm) ?? []).length === 2, 'page-data: push is not in both props');
    assert(/push: PushPageProps \| null;/.test(pageData), 'DashboardPageProps has no push');
    const route = read('app/api/page-data/route.ts');
    assert(/auth\.via === 'session' \? \{ provedAt: auth\.session\.provedAt \} : null/.test(route) && /loadPageData\(.*, session\)/.test(route), 'the page-data route does not pass the session');
    assert(/push\?: PushPageProps \| null;/.test(read('app/[locale]/(app)/settings/SettingsClient.tsx')), 'SettingsClient takes no push prop');
    for (const rel of ['app/api/push/devices/route.ts', 'app/api/push/devices/check/route.ts']) {
      const text = read(rel);
      assert(/pushFront\(request, '[a-z-]+', \{ needsPushOn: (true|false) \}\)/.test(text), `${rel}: no pushFront`);
      assert(!/verifyUserAuth|refuseCrossSite/.test(text.replace(/^\s*\/\/.*$/gm, '')), `${rel}: its own auth, not the shared front`);
    }
    const deleteSource = read('app/api/push/devices/route.ts').split('export async function DELETE')[1];
    assert(/needsPushOn: false/.test(deleteSource) && !/refuseStaleSignIn/.test(deleteSource), 'DELETE: asks push on, or the hour');
    assert(/export async function POST/.test(read('app/api/push/devices/route.ts')) && /isOwnDevice\(userId, body\.endpoint\)/.test(read('app/api/push/devices/route.ts')), 'POST: the hour is not tied to a new endpoint');
  });

  await check('copy: bot.push.turnedOn and turnedOnBody are in en, he and ru, differ per language, and hold no emoji', () => {
    const seen = new Set<string>();
    for (const lang of ['en', 'he', 'ru']) {
      const words = MESSAGES[lang].bot.push;
      for (const key of ['turnedOn', 'turnedOnBody']) {
        assert(typeof words?.[key] === 'string' && words[key].length > 0, `${lang}.${key} is missing`);
        assert(!EMOJI.test(words[key]), `${lang}.${key} has an emoji`);
        seen.add(words[key]);
      }
    }
    assert(seen.size === 6, 'two languages share a text');
    assert(/[א-ת]/.test(MESSAGES.he.bot.push.turnedOn) && /[Ѐ-ӿ]/.test(MESSAGES.ru.bot.push.turnedOn), 'he or ru is not in its own letters');
  });

  await check('no endpoint, key or token of this run is in a console line, a capture or an answer\'s text', () => {
    const haystack = `${everyLog.join('\n')}\n${show(everyCapture)}`;
    for (const secret of secrets) assert(!haystack.includes(secret), `a secret reached a log or a capture: ${secret.slice(0, 40)}`);
    assert(secrets.size > 10, 'the run made too few secrets to mean anything');
  });

  report();
}

main().catch(err => {
  process.stdout.write(`${err instanceof Error ? err.stack : String(err)}\n`);
  process.exit(1);
});
```

- [ ] **Step 2: Run it to see it fail**

Run: `$RUN smoke-push-routes`
Expected: it crashes at once with `Cannot find module '.../app/api/push/devices/route.ts'` (the routes do not exist yet; `stubModule` of `src/lib/rate-limit.ts` has already run, which is fine).

- [ ] **Step 3: Implement**

**3a. The limiter.** In `src/lib/rate-limit.ts`, find the line `export interface RateLimitResult {` (line 67) and insert this above it (leave the blank line that is already there after it):

```ts
// push/devices (subscribe, check, unsubscribe): 20 requests per minute per account, on a budget of its own. A person turns
// a device on or off one tap at a time, and the app's open checks the browser's own endpoint once; the budget is for a
// stuck retry, and it keeps a script from walking endpoints through the check route
export const pushDeviceRateLimiter = new Ratelimit({
  redis,
  limiter: Ratelimit.slidingWindow(20, '1 m'),
```

**3c. The page-prop type.** Create `src/lib/web-push-props.ts`:

```ts
/**
 * What the page data hands the Settings card and the home banner for notifications in the browser (web push spec
 * 2026-11-05 §3.2). Types only, so a 'use client' file can import it.
 *
 * `key` is the public VAPID key, read at request time (never NEXT_PUBLIC_), null while push is off: the switch and the
 * banner's action then do not show. `recentSignIn` is whether the session was proved within the hour (turning on asks
 * for it), so the tap needs no request before the browser's prompt. `devices` is how many browsers the account has on.
 * The whole prop is null inside Telegram and for an account with a chat.
 */
export interface PushPageProps {
  key: string | null;
  recentSignIn: boolean;
  devices: number;
}
```

```ts
/** How many devices the account has on: the page props' `devices` (pushPropsFor). */
export async function countDevices(userId: number): Promise<number> {
  return prisma.pushDevice.count({ where: { userId } });
}
```

**3e. The shared front.** Create `src/services/web-push/request.ts`:

```ts
/**
 * The front of the three push-device routes (web push spec 2026-11-05 §3.2): one path for all of them, so none can forget a
 * step. In this order: the request comes from the app's own origin and sends JSON (refuseCrossSite), then the session
 * cookie proves the person (verifyUserAuth with no initData, so a Mini App request has nothing to prove it by) under the
 * push limiter (20 a minute per account), then, for the routes that need push on, 503 while the VAPID keys are not set.
 * Every refusal logs one line with the user id and the reason, never the endpoint or the keys. Server only.
 */

import { NextResponse, type NextRequest } from 'next/server';
import { verifyUserAuth, type AuthResult } from '../../lib/api-auth';
import { pushDeviceRateLimiter } from '../../lib/rate-limit';
import { refuseCrossSite } from '../../lib/same-origin';
import { pushConfigured } from './config';

/** A proved request that rides the web session cookie (the only credential these routes take). */
export type PushSessionAuth = Extract<AuthResult, { success: true; via: 'session' }>;

export type PushFront = { ok: true; auth: PushSessionAuth } | { ok: false; response: NextResponse };

/**
 * `tag` names the route in the logs and the limiter's lines. `needsPushOn`: answer 503 { ok: false, error: 'push-off' }
 * while push is not configured (subscribe and check); an unsubscribe passes false, so a device can always be removed.
 */
export async function pushFront(request: NextRequest, tag: string, opts: { needsPushOn: boolean }): Promise<PushFront> {
  const refused = refuseCrossSite(request);
  if (refused) return { ok: false, response: refused };
  const auth = await verifyUserAuth(request, request.nextUrl.searchParams.get('user_id'), null, pushDeviceRateLimiter, tag);
  if (!auth.success) {
    // verifyUserAuth logs its other refusals itself; the spent minute (429) it answers silently, and the person is proved by then
    if (auth.response.status === 429) console.warn(`[push] ${tag} refused: too many requests`);
    return { ok: false, response: auth.response };
  }
  // With no initData only the cookie can prove the user, so this is a session; the check narrows the type
  if (auth.via !== 'session') {
    console.warn(`[push] user ${auth.user.id}: ${tag} refused: not a browser session`);
    return { ok: false, response: NextResponse.json({ ok: false, error: 'browser-only' }, { status: 403 }) };
  }
  if (opts.needsPushOn && !pushConfigured()) {
    console.warn(`[push] user ${auth.user.id}: ${tag} refused: push is off`);
    return { ok: false, response: NextResponse.json({ ok: false, error: 'push-off' }, { status: 503 }) };
  }
  return { ok: true, auth };
}

/** The body as JSON, or null (and one line saying only that it was not JSON). */
export async function readPushBody(request: NextRequest, tag: string): Promise<unknown> {
  return request.json().catch(() => {
    console.warn(`[push] ${tag}: the body is not JSON`);
    return null;
  });
}

/** devices.ts's own limit for an endpoint: the check and the unsubscribe only compare it with stored rows. */
const MAX_ENDPOINT_CHARS = 1024;

/** The body's `endpoint` when it is a non-empty string of at most 1024 characters, else null. */
export function endpointOf(body: unknown): string | null {
  const endpoint = typeof body === 'object' && body !== null ? (body as { endpoint?: unknown }).endpoint : undefined;
  return typeof endpoint === 'string' && endpoint.length > 0 && endpoint.length <= MAX_ENDPOINT_CHARS ? endpoint : null;
}
```

**3f. The page-prop builder.** Create `src/services/web-push/page-props.ts`:

```ts
/**
 * The `push` prop of the Settings and home pages (web push spec 2026-11-05 §3.2), worked out in one place for both.
 * Server only. Nothing here is logged.
 */

import { captureError } from '../../lib/error-capture';
import { hasChat } from '../../lib/has-chat';
import { recentlyProved } from '../../lib/recent-sign-in';
import type { PushPageProps } from '../../lib/web-push-props';
import { vapidPublicKey } from './config';
import { countDevices } from './devices';

/** How the page's request proved the person: a web session (when it was proved, epoch ms), or null for Telegram initData. */
export type PageSession = { provedAt: number } | null;

/**
 * null for a request proved by Telegram's initData and for an account with a chat (they get their messages there).
 * Otherwise the key (null while push is off, and then no devices are counted), whether the session was proved within the
 * hour, and how many devices the account has on. A failed count is captured and reads as none: the page still loads.
 */
export async function pushPropsFor(
  user: { id: number; telegramId: number | bigint | null | undefined; whatsappPhone?: string | null; whatsappBsuid?: string | null },
  session: PageSession,
): Promise<PushPageProps | null> {
  if (session === null || hasChat(user)) return null;
  const key = vapidPublicKey();
  let devices = 0;
  if (key !== null) {
    try {
      devices = await countDevices(user.id);
    } catch (error) {
      captureError(error, 'push-page-props', { user_id: user.id }, 'warning');
    }
  }
  return { key, recentSignIn: recentlyProved(session.provedAt), devices };
}
```

**3g. The routes.** Create `app/api/push/devices/route.ts`:

```ts
// Notifications in the browser (web push spec 2026-11-05 §3.2): this browser subscribes and unsubscribes.
//
// POST   /api/push/devices?user_id=…  body { endpoint, keys: { p256dh, auth } }
//   → 200 { ok: true, count }: the device is stored (count is how many the account has on now), and a "Notifications are on"
//     notification goes to the account's devices after the answer (kind 'test': a reply to the person's own tap, not paused).
//   → 403 { ok: false, error: 'stale-sign-in' }: a new endpoint needs a sign-in from the last hour (refuseStaleSignIn); an
//     endpoint the account already has does not. An endpoint another account holds moves here: the browser just proved it
//     holds it, with that recent sign-in.
//   → 409 { ok: false, error: 'has-chat' }: Telegram or WhatsApp carries everything for this account.
//   → 400 { ok: false, error: 'bad-input' }, 503 { ok: false, error: 'push-off' } (no VAPID keys), 500 { ok: false, error: 'failed' }.
// DELETE /api/push/devices?user_id=…  body { endpoint }
//   → 200 { ok: true }: this account's own device is gone (another account's endpoint, or none, changes nothing and
//     answers the same). No recent sign-in asked, and it works while push is off.
// Both: src/services/web-push/request.ts first (same origin and JSON, the session cookie, 20 a minute per account).
// POST /api/push/devices/check is the sibling route.
//
// No log line, capture or answer holds the endpoint or the keys: a failure is told by its kind (captureSignInFailure),
// because a Prisma error prints the values of its query.

import { NextRequest, NextResponse, after } from 'next/server';
import { getBotMessage } from '@/src/lib/bot-messages';
import { hasChat } from '@/src/lib/has-chat';
import { captureError } from '@/src/lib/error-capture';
import { refuseStaleSignIn } from '@/src/lib/recent-sign-in';
import { appPageUrl } from '@/src/lib/web-session';
import type { UserConfig } from '@/src/types';
import { normalizeLocale } from '@/src/utils/locale';
import { captureSignInFailure } from '@/src/services/web-signin/safe-error';
import { addDevice, deleteOwnDevice, isOwnDevice, isValidDeviceInput } from '@/src/services/web-push/devices';
import { endpointOf, pushFront, readPushBody } from '@/src/services/web-push/request';
import { sendWebPush } from '@/src/services/web-push/send';

export const dynamic = 'force-dynamic';

/** The "Notifications are on" notification, after the answer. It never throws: a failure is captured. */
async function sendTurnedOn(user: UserConfig, endpoint: string): Promise<void> {
  try {
    const lang = normalizeLocale(user.language);
    await sendWebPush(user, {
      kind: 'test',
      title: await getBotMessage(lang, 'push.turnedOn'),
      body: await getBotMessage(lang, 'push.turnedOnBody'),
      url: appPageUrl(lang, 'dashboard', user.id),
      tag: 'push-turned-on',
      lang,
      onlyEndpoint: endpoint,
    });
  } catch (error) {
    captureError(error, 'push-turned-on', { api_route: '/api/push/devices', user_id: user.id }, 'warning');
  }
}

export async function POST(request: NextRequest) {
  let userId: number | undefined;
  try {
    const front = await pushFront(request, 'push-devices', { needsPushOn: true });
    if (!front.ok) return front.response;
    const { auth } = front;
    userId = auth.user.id;
    if (hasChat(auth.user)) {
      console.warn(`[push] user ${userId}: subscribe refused: the account has a chat`);
      return NextResponse.json({ ok: false, error: 'has-chat' }, { status: 409 });
    }
    const body = await readPushBody(request, 'push-devices');
    if (!isValidDeviceInput(body)) {
      console.warn(`[push] user ${userId}: subscribe refused: bad input`);
      return NextResponse.json({ ok: false, error: 'bad-input' }, { status: 400 });
    }
    // The hour is for a device the account does not have yet: one it already has re-subscribes with no new proof
    if (!(await isOwnDevice(userId, body.endpoint))) {
      const stale = refuseStaleSignIn(auth, 'push-devices');
      if (stale) return stale;
    }
    const { moved, count } = await addDevice(userId, { endpoint: body.endpoint, keys: { p256dh: body.keys.p256dh, auth: body.keys.auth } });
    console.log(`[push] user ${userId}: device ${moved ? 'moved here from another account' : 'added'} (${count} on this account)`);
    after(() => sendTurnedOn(auth.user, body.endpoint));
    return NextResponse.json({ ok: true, count });
  } catch (error) {
    captureSignInFailure(error, 'push-devices', { user_id: userId });
    return NextResponse.json({ ok: false, error: 'failed' }, { status: 500 });
  }
}

export async function DELETE(request: NextRequest) {
  let userId: number | undefined;
  try {
    const front = await pushFront(request, 'push-devices-delete', { needsPushOn: false });
    if (!front.ok) return front.response;
    userId = front.auth.user.id;
    const endpoint = endpointOf(await readPushBody(request, 'push-devices-delete'));
    if (endpoint === null) {
      console.warn(`[push] user ${userId}: unsubscribe refused: bad input`);
      return NextResponse.json({ ok: false, error: 'bad-input' }, { status: 400 });
    }
    const removed = await deleteOwnDevice(userId, endpoint);
    console.log(`[push] user ${userId}: device ${removed ? 'removed' : 'not found; nothing removed'}`);
    return NextResponse.json({ ok: true });
  } catch (error) {
    captureSignInFailure(error, 'push-devices-delete', { user_id: userId });
    return NextResponse.json({ ok: false, error: 'failed' }, { status: 500 });
  }
}
```

Create `app/api/push/devices/check/route.ts`:

```ts
// Notifications in the browser (web push spec 2026-11-05 §3.2): whether this browser's subscription is this account's. The
// app asks on every open, so a subscription a shared browser kept from an old account is unsubscribed, never moved.
//
// POST /api/push/devices/check?user_id=…  body { endpoint }
//   → 200 { ok: true, mine: boolean }: true only for an endpoint this account holds. Another account's endpoint and one
//     nobody holds get the same answer (false), so the route tells nothing about anyone else.
//   → 400 { ok: false, error: 'bad-input' }, 503 { ok: false, error: 'push-off' }, 500 { ok: false, error: 'failed' }.
// Same front as the sibling route (src/services/web-push/request.ts). The endpoint is never logged.

import { NextRequest, NextResponse } from 'next/server';
import { captureSignInFailure } from '@/src/services/web-signin/safe-error';
import { isOwnDevice } from '@/src/services/web-push/devices';
import { endpointOf, pushFront, readPushBody } from '@/src/services/web-push/request';

export const dynamic = 'force-dynamic';

export async function POST(request: NextRequest) {
  let userId: number | undefined;
  try {
    const front = await pushFront(request, 'push-devices-check', { needsPushOn: true });
    if (!front.ok) return front.response;
    userId = front.auth.user.id;
    const endpoint = endpointOf(await readPushBody(request, 'push-devices-check'));
    if (endpoint === null) {
      console.warn(`[push] user ${userId}: check refused: bad input`);
      return NextResponse.json({ ok: false, error: 'bad-input' }, { status: 400 });
    }
    return NextResponse.json({ ok: true, mine: await isOwnDevice(userId, endpoint) });
  } catch (error) {
    captureSignInFailure(error, 'push-devices-check', { user_id: userId });
    return NextResponse.json({ ok: false, error: 'failed' }, { status: 500 });
  }
}
```

Two things in the subscribe route are deliberate: the `has-chat` refusal comes before the body is read and before the hour is asked (a chat account never sees "sign in again"), and the hour (`refuseStaleSignIn`) is asked only for an endpoint the account does not already hold (spec §3.2). The "Notifications are on" send runs in `after()`, so the answer does not wait on a push service, and a failure there is captured (a warning) and never changes the answer. Never log or capture the endpoint, the keys, or an error message from the store: use `captureSignInFailure`, as written.

### Task 4: Service worker, manifest and icons

Written and run in a scratch copy at `d619f289`: the smoke passes 14/14 and each mutation below fails it; the copy's whole suite passes with it. The drafter also ran `next dev` in that copy and fetched the results: `/sw.js` comes back `200` with `Cache-Control: no-cache, no-store, must-revalidate` from `public/` (the `headers()` rule applies to a public file), `/manifest.webmanifest` serves the manifest below, and the pages of all three root layouts (the public site, the app and the Google connect pages) carry `<link rel="manifest" href="/manifest.webmanifest">` and `<link rel="apple-touch-icon" href="/apple-icon.png?..." sizes="180x180">`; only the app's pages carry the two `theme-color` metas. Decisions: `public/sw.js` with a `headers()` rule in `next.config.js` (not a route handler: the worker stays a plain, lintable JavaScript file that the smoke runs as it is, and Next's own PWA guide does the same); the manifest is `app/manifest.ts` and the Home Screen icon is `app/apple-icon.png`, both Next file conventions, so the three root layouts need no link of their own.

**Files:**
- Create: `public/sw.js`
- Create: `app/manifest.ts`
- Create: `public/icon-192.png`, `public/icon-512.png`, `public/icon-maskable-512.png` (binary, made once from `public/icon.png` with macOS `sips`, committed)
- Create: `app/apple-icon.png` (binary, 180x180, the same way)
- Modify: `next.config.js` (a `headers()` function, after `poweredByHeader: false`, line 37)
- Modify: `app/[locale]/(app)/layout.tsx` (the `viewport` export, lines 6 and 18-24: a light and a dark `themeColor`)
- Test: `scripts/smoke-push-worker.ts` (new)

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes (Task 2's payload, `buildPushPayload` in `src/services/web-push/payload.ts`): `{ web_push: 8030, notification: { title, body, navigate, lang, dir, tag, silent } }` as JSON, `navigate` an absolute `https://famcal.bot/...` URL, `dir` `'rtl'` for Hebrew else `'ltr'`. The worker reads exactly those keys and nothing else; it also copes with a payload that is not that (shows "FamCal").
- Produces:
  - The worker is served at `/sw.js`, scope `/`. Task 5's client registers it with `navigator.serviceWorker.register('/sw.js', { scope: '/', updateViaCache: 'none' })`, inside the tap that turns notifications on.
  - A shown notification carries `data: { url }` (the payload's `navigate`, made absolute and kept only if it is on FamCal's own origin, else the home `/dashboard`), icon `/icon-192.png`, `renotify: true` when it has a tag and is not silent.
  - A tap closes the notification, focuses (the focused, else a visible, else the first) open FamCal window and navigates it to that URL, else `clients.openWindow(url)`.
  - `/manifest.webmanifest` (name FamCal, `start_url` `/dashboard`, standalone, scope `/`), the icons `/icon-192.png`, `/icon-512.png`, `/icon-maskable-512.png`, and the Home Screen icon `/apple-icon.png`.
- Spec points decided here: (1) the worker sets `renotify` only for a tagged, non-silent push, because a browser throws a TypeError on `silent` with `renotify` (the smoke's fake enforces that rule); (2) `Cache-Control: no-cache, no-store, must-revalidate` (the spec says `no-cache`; the extra `no-store` is so Cloudflare, if it proxies, never holds the file); (3) `theme_color` and `background_color` are the light background `#ffffff` and the `(app)` layout's viewport adds the dark one `#15151d` for a dark device (a manifest holds one value); (4) the manifest link also appears on the public site's pages, which the spec accepted (its `start_url` is the app); (5) the worker has no `pushsubscriptionchange` handler (the spec lists three jobs, and every app open already compares the browser's subscription with the server's).

- [ ] **Step 1: Write the failing test**

Create `scripts/smoke-push-worker.ts`:

```ts
// Smoke test for the notification service worker and the web app's manifest (web push spec 2026-11-05 §3.3; plan 6c-2 Task 4):
// public/sw.js, app/manifest.ts, the icons, the head links and the headers /sw.js is served with.
//
// What it pins:
//   - the worker runs as a classic script in a fake worker scope (a vm with a fake self, clients and registration) and listens to
//     install, activate, push and notificationclick only: no fetch handler, no cache;
//   - install takes over at once (skipWaiting) and activate claims the open windows (clients.claim);
//   - a push shows one notification from the declarative payload (title, body, lang, dir, tag, FamCal's icon, the URL kept in its
//     data); a tag renotifies unless the push is silent (a browser throws on both, and the fake does too); a payload that cannot
//     be read, or holds the wrong types, still shows "FamCal"; a navigate URL on another origin, or not a URL, opens the home;
//   - a tap closes the notification and, with a FamCal window open, focuses it and navigates it (the focused one first, then a
//     visible one, never another site's window), else opens a new window; a window that cannot be focused or navigated, a
//     list that cannot be read and an open that is refused each end in one quiet console line and never in a throw;
//   - nothing the worker logs holds a notification's words or URL;
//   - the manifest: FamCal, start_url /dashboard, standalone, the app's own light colour, a 192, a 512 and a maskable icon that
//     are really those PNG sizes, and the Home Screen icon is 180 square; the proxy leaves /sw.js, the manifest and the icons
//     alone; /sw.js is served with no-cache and no-store, and only /sw.js is;
//   - the head links: the manifest and the Home Screen icon are file conventions of app/, so all three root layouts link them,
//     and no layout sets metadata.manifest or metadata.icons over them; the (app) layout's viewport gives the dark device its
//     own bar colour from the app's tokens.
//
// Nothing is faked but the worker's scope: the real public/sw.js, app/manifest.ts, proxy.ts's matcher and next.config.js run. No
// network, no DB.
//
// Run with the fake env of scripts/ci-smokes.sh (the plan's $RUN runner):
//   $RUN smoke-push-worker

import fs from 'fs';
import { createRequire } from 'module';
import path from 'path';
import vm from 'vm';

// eslint-disable-next-line @typescript-eslint/no-explicit-any
type Row = Record<string, any>;

const ROOT = path.resolve(__dirname, '..');
const nodeRequire = createRequire(__filename);
const ORIGIN = 'https://famcal.bot';
const SOURCE = fs.readFileSync(path.join(ROOT, 'public/sw.js'), 'utf8');

type Result = { name: string; ok: boolean; reason?: string };
const results: Result[] = [];
async function check(name: string, fn: () => void | Promise<void>): Promise<void> {
  try {
    await fn();
    results.push({ name, ok: true });
  } catch (err) {
    results.push({ name, ok: false, reason: err instanceof Error ? err.message : String(err) });
  }
}
function assert(cond: unknown, message: string): asserts cond {
  if (!cond) throw new Error(message);
}
const show = (value: unknown): string => JSON.stringify(value);

// ---- The worker's fake scope ----

interface FakeWindow {
  url: string;
  focused?: boolean;
  visibilityState?: 'visible' | 'hidden';
  focusFails?: boolean;
  navigateFails?: boolean;
  /** What happened to it, in order: 'focus', 'navigate <url>' */
  calls: string[];
}

interface Scope {
  handlers: Record<string, ((event: Row) => void)[]>;
  shown: { title: string; options: Row }[];
  warnings: string[];
  opened: string[];
  matchAllOptions: Row[];
  windows: FakeWindow[];
  counts: { skipWaiting: number; claim: number };
  fails: { showNotification: boolean; matchAll: boolean; openWindow: boolean };
  /** Runs one event through every handler of its type, as the browser does; resolves when what it waited on does. */
  fire: (type: string, extra?: Row) => Promise<Row>;
}

/** A browser's own rules for showNotification, which a worker can break: renotify needs a tag, and a silent notification cannot renotify. */
function browserShowNotification(options: Row): void {
  if (options.renotify && !options.tag) throw new TypeError('Failed to execute showNotification: renotify requires a tag');
  if (options.silent && options.renotify) throw new TypeError('Failed to execute showNotification: silent and renotify cannot both be true');
}

function boot(): Scope {
  const scope: Scope = {
    handlers: {}, shown: [], warnings: [], opened: [], matchAllOptions: [], windows: [],
    counts: { skipWaiting: 0, claim: 0 }, fails: { showNotification: false, matchAll: false, openWindow: false },
    fire: async (type, extra = {}) => {
      const waits: Promise<unknown>[] = [];
      const event: Row = { waitUntil: (p: Promise<unknown>) => { waits.push(Promise.resolve(p)); }, ...extra };
      for (const handler of scope.handlers[type] ?? []) handler(event);
      await Promise.all(waits);
      return event;
    },
  };
  const client = (w: FakeWindow): Row => ({
    url: w.url, focused: w.focused ?? false, visibilityState: w.visibilityState ?? 'hidden',
    focus: async () => { w.calls.push('focus'); if (w.focusFails) throw new Error('focus refused'); return client(w); },
    navigate: async (url: string) => { w.calls.push(`navigate ${url}`); if (w.navigateFails) throw new TypeError('the client is not controlled'); return client(w); },
  });
  const sandbox: Row = {
    URL, Promise, JSON, Boolean, Error, TypeError,
    console: { warn: (...args: unknown[]) => scope.warnings.push(args.map(String).join(' ')), log: () => undefined, error: () => undefined },
    location: { origin: ORIGIN },
    addEventListener: (type: string, handler: (event: Row) => void) => { (scope.handlers[type] ??= []).push(handler); },
    skipWaiting: async () => { scope.counts.skipWaiting++; },
    registration: {
      showNotification: async (title: string, options: Row) => {
        if (scope.fails.showNotification) throw new Error('showNotification refused');
        browserShowNotification(options);
        scope.shown.push({ title, options: JSON.parse(JSON.stringify(options)) });
      },
    },
    clients: {
      claim: async () => { scope.counts.claim++; },
      matchAll: async (options: Row) => {
        scope.matchAllOptions.push(JSON.parse(JSON.stringify(options)));
        if (scope.fails.matchAll) throw new Error('matchAll refused');
        return scope.windows.map(client);
      },
      openWindow: async (url: string) => {
        if (scope.fails.openWindow) throw new Error('openWindow refused');
        scope.opened.push(url);
        return { url };
      },
    },
  };
  sandbox.self = sandbox; // in a worker, self is the global scope itself
  vm.createContext(sandbox);
  vm.runInContext(SOURCE, sandbox, { filename: 'public/sw.js' });
  return scope;
}

/** A push event carrying this payload as JSON, as the browser hands it to the worker. */
const pushOf = (payload: unknown): Row => ({ data: { json: () => JSON.parse(JSON.stringify(payload)) } });
const declarative = (n: Row): Row => ({ web_push: 8030, notification: n });
const HOME = `${ORIGIN}/dashboard`;
const win = (url: string, o: Partial<FakeWindow> = {}): FakeWindow => ({ url, calls: [], ...o });
const tap = (data: unknown): Row => ({ notification: { data, close() { this.closed = true; }, closed: false } });

// ---- The PNG's size, from its header ----

function pngSize(file: string): { width: number; height: number } {
  const buf = fs.readFileSync(file);
  assert(buf.subarray(1, 4).toString('latin1') === 'PNG', `${file} is not a PNG`);
  return { width: buf.readUInt32BE(16), height: buf.readUInt32BE(20) };
}

async function main() {
  await check('the worker is a classic script that listens to install, activate, push and notificationclick only: no fetch handler, no cache, no module syntax', () => {
    const scope = boot();
    assert(show(Object.keys(scope.handlers).sort()) === show(['activate', 'install', 'notificationclick', 'push']), `handlers: ${show(Object.keys(scope.handlers))}`);
    const code = SOURCE.split('\n').filter(line => !/^\s*\/\//.test(line)).join('\n');
    assert(!/\bfetch\b|\bcaches\b|\bimportScripts\b/.test(code), 'the worker fetches, caches or imports');
    assert(!/^\s*(import|export)\s/m.test(code), 'module syntax in a classic worker');
  });

  await check('install takes over at once (skipWaiting) and activate claims the open windows (clients.claim), each waited on', async () => {
    const scope = boot();
    await scope.fire('install');
    await scope.fire('activate');
    assert(scope.counts.skipWaiting === 1 && scope.counts.claim === 1, `${show(scope.counts)}`);
  });

  await check('a push shows one notification from the declarative payload: title, body, lang, dir, tag, FamCal\'s icon, the URL kept in data; a tag renotifies', async () => {
    const scope = boot();
    await scope.fire('push', pushOf(declarative({ title: 'Dentist', body: 'Starting at 10:00', navigate: `${ORIGIN}/en/dashboard?user_id=50`, lang: 'en', dir: 'ltr', tag: 'nr-abc', silent: false })));
    assert(scope.shown.length === 1, `${scope.shown.length} shown`);
    assert(show(scope.shown[0]) === show({
      title: 'Dentist',
      options: { body: 'Starting at 10:00', icon: '/icon-192.png', lang: 'en', dir: 'ltr', data: { url: `${ORIGIN}/en/dashboard?user_id=50` }, silent: false, tag: 'nr-abc', renotify: true },
    }), `shown: ${show(scope.shown[0])}`);
    await scope.fire('push', pushOf(declarative({ title: 'תזכורת', body: 'לכולם', navigate: `${ORIGIN}/he/dashboard?user_id=51`, lang: 'he', dir: 'rtl', tag: 'nr-def' })));
    assert(scope.shown[1].title === 'תזכורת' && scope.shown[1].options.dir === 'rtl' && scope.shown[1].options.lang === 'he' && scope.shown[1].options.silent === false, `he: ${show(scope.shown[1])}`);
  });

  await check('a silent push does not renotify (the browser throws on both); a push with no tag sets neither tag nor renotify', async () => {
    const scope = boot();
    await scope.fire('push', pushOf(declarative({ title: 'Done by Dana', body: 'Dentist', navigate: HOME, tag: 'nr-x', silent: true })));
    assert(scope.shown[0].options.silent === true && scope.shown[0].options.renotify === false && scope.shown[0].options.tag === 'nr-x', `silent: ${show(scope.shown[0])}`);
    await scope.fire('push', pushOf(declarative({ title: 'Today', body: 'Dentist 09:00', navigate: HOME })));
    assert(!('tag' in scope.shown[1].options) && !('renotify' in scope.shown[1].options), `untagged: ${show(scope.shown[1])}`);
  });

  await check('a push whose payload cannot be read (no data, not JSON, the wrong shape) still shows "FamCal" with the home as its target, and a notification the browser refuses is not swallowed', async () => {
    const scope = boot();
    await scope.fire('push', {});
    await scope.fire('push', { data: { json: () => { throw new SyntaxError('Unexpected token') } } });
    for (const odd of [{}, null, [1], 'text', { notification: 'x' }, { notification: null }]) await scope.fire('push', pushOf(odd));
    assert(scope.shown.length === 8, `${scope.shown.length} shown`);
    for (const s of scope.shown) {
      assert(s.title === 'FamCal' && s.options.body === '' && s.options.data.url === HOME && s.options.dir === 'auto' && !('tag' in s.options), `${show(s)}`);
    }
    assert(scope.warnings.length === 1 && scope.warnings[0] === '[sw] a push with a payload that is not JSON', `warnings: ${show(scope.warnings)}`);
    scope.fails.showNotification = true;
    let thrown = false;
    try { await scope.fire('push', pushOf(declarative({ title: 'x', navigate: HOME }))); } catch { thrown = true; }
    assert(thrown, 'a refused showNotification was swallowed (the browser must see the failure)');
  });

  await check('fields of the wrong type are ignored (a title that is a number, a body that is an object, a dir that is neither); a navigate URL on another origin or that is no URL opens the home, a relative one stays on FamCal', async () => {
    const scope = boot();
    await scope.fire('push', pushOf(declarative({ title: 7, body: { a: 1 }, dir: 'weird', lang: 5, tag: 9, navigate: 3 })));
    assert(show(scope.shown[0]) === show({ title: 'FamCal', options: { body: '', icon: '/icon-192.png', lang: '', dir: 'auto', data: { url: HOME }, silent: false } }), `${show(scope.shown[0])}`);
    const targets: [unknown, string][] = [
      ['https://evil.example/phish', HOME], ['javascript:alert(1)', HOME], ['//evil.example/x', HOME], ['https://famcal.bot.evil.example/', HOME],
      ['', HOME], ['/he/dashboard?user_id=5', `${ORIGIN}/he/dashboard?user_id=5`], [`${ORIGIN}/ru/dashboard?user_id=6`, `${ORIGIN}/ru/dashboard?user_id=6`],
    ];
    for (const [navigate, want] of targets) {
      scope.shown.length = 0;
      await scope.fire('push', pushOf(declarative({ title: 't', navigate })));
      assert(scope.shown[0].options.data.url === want, `${show(navigate)} → ${scope.shown[0].options.data.url}, want ${want}`);
    }
  });

  await check('a tap closes the notification and, with FamCal open, focuses that window and then navigates it to the notification\'s URL; matchAll asks for windows, uncontrolled ones too; no new window opens', async () => {
    const scope = boot();
    const open = win(`${ORIGIN}/en/settings?user_id=50`);
    scope.windows = [open];
    const event = tap({ url: `${ORIGIN}/en/dashboard?user_id=50` });
    await scope.fire('notificationclick', event);
    assert(event.notification.closed === true, 'the notification was not closed');
    assert(show(open.calls) === show(['focus', `navigate ${ORIGIN}/en/dashboard?user_id=50`]), `calls: ${show(open.calls)}`);
    assert(scope.opened.length === 0, 'a window was opened as well');
    assert(show(scope.matchAllOptions) === show([{ type: 'window', includeUncontrolled: true }]), `matchAll: ${show(scope.matchAllOptions)}`);
  });

  await check('a tap chooses the focused FamCal window, else a visible one, else the first; another site\'s window is never used', async () => {
    const url = `${ORIGIN}/en/dashboard?user_id=50`;
    const pick = async (windows: FakeWindow[]): Promise<Scope> => {
      const scope = boot();
      scope.windows = windows;
      await scope.fire('notificationclick', tap({ url }));
      return scope;
    };
    const [a, b, c] = [win(`${ORIGIN}/a`), win(`${ORIGIN}/b`, { visibilityState: 'visible' }), win(`${ORIGIN}/c`, { focused: true, visibilityState: 'visible' })];
    await pick([a, b, c]);
    assert(c.calls.length === 2 && a.calls.length === 0 && b.calls.length === 0, `focused: ${show([a.calls, b.calls, c.calls])}`);
    const [d, e] = [win(`${ORIGIN}/d`), win(`${ORIGIN}/e`, { visibilityState: 'visible' })];
    await pick([d, e]);
    assert(e.calls.length === 2 && d.calls.length === 0, `visible: ${show([d.calls, e.calls])}`);
    const [f, g] = [win(`${ORIGIN}/f`), win(`${ORIGIN}/g`)];
    await pick([f, g]);
    assert(f.calls.length === 2 && g.calls.length === 0, `first: ${show([f.calls, g.calls])}`);
    const foreign = win('https://famcal.bot.evil.example/dashboard', { focused: true, visibilityState: 'visible' });
    const other = win('https://evil.example/', { focused: true });
    const scope = await pick([foreign, other]);
    assert(foreign.calls.length === 0 && other.calls.length === 0 && show(scope.opened) === show([url]), `another site's window: ${show([foreign.calls, other.calls, scope.opened])}`);
  });

  await check('a tap with no FamCal window open opens one at the notification\'s URL; one with no URL in its data opens the home', async () => {
    const scope = boot();
    await scope.fire('notificationclick', tap({ url: `${ORIGIN}/he/dashboard?user_id=51` }));
    await scope.fire('notificationclick', tap(undefined));
    await scope.fire('notificationclick', tap({ url: 'https://evil.example/x' }));
    assert(show(scope.opened) === show([`${ORIGIN}/he/dashboard?user_id=51`, HOME, HOME]), `opened: ${show(scope.opened)}`);
  });

  await check('a window that cannot be navigated or focused, a window list that cannot be read, and an open that is refused each end quietly: the next way is tried, one console line, never a throw', async () => {
    const url = `${ORIGIN}/en/dashboard?user_id=50`;
    const uncontrolled = boot();
    const stuck = win(`${ORIGIN}/x`, { navigateFails: true });
    uncontrolled.windows = [stuck];
    await uncontrolled.fire('notificationclick', tap({ url }));
    assert(show(stuck.calls) === show(['focus', `navigate ${url}`]) && show(uncontrolled.opened) === show([url]), `navigate fails: ${show([stuck.calls, uncontrolled.opened])}`);
    assert(uncontrolled.warnings.length === 1 && uncontrolled.warnings[0] === '[sw] could not focus or navigate the open window; opening a new one', `${show(uncontrolled.warnings)}`);
    const noFocus = boot();
    noFocus.windows = [win(`${ORIGIN}/x`, { focusFails: true })];
    await noFocus.fire('notificationclick', tap({ url }));
    assert(show(noFocus.opened) === show([url]), `focus fails: ${show(noFocus.opened)}`);
    const noList = boot();
    noList.fails.matchAll = true;
    await noList.fire('notificationclick', tap({ url }));
    assert(show(noList.opened) === show([url]) && noList.warnings.length === 1 && noList.warnings[0] === '[sw] could not list the open windows', `matchAll fails: ${show([noList.opened, noList.warnings])}`);
    const noOpen = boot();
    noOpen.fails.openWindow = true;
    await noOpen.fire('notificationclick', tap({ url }));
    assert(show(noOpen.warnings) === show(['[sw] could not open a window']), `openWindow fails: ${show(noOpen.warnings)}`);
  });

  await check('nothing the worker logs holds a notification\'s title, body or URL', async () => {
    const scope = boot();
    const words = ['Secret dentist title', 'Secret place body', 'https://famcal.bot/en/dashboard?user_id=777'];
    await scope.fire('push', { data: { json: () => { throw new SyntaxError(`Unexpected token ${words[0]}`); } } });
    await scope.fire('push', pushOf(declarative({ title: words[0], body: words[1], navigate: words[2], tag: 't' })));
    scope.fails.openWindow = true;
    scope.windows = [win(`${ORIGIN}/x`, { navigateFails: true })];
    await scope.fire('notificationclick', tap({ url: words[2] }));
    assert(scope.warnings.length >= 2, `${scope.warnings.length} lines`);
    for (const line of scope.warnings) for (const word of words) assert(!line.includes(word), `a line holds ${word}: ${line}`);
  });

  // ---- The manifest, the icons and the head ----

  await check('the manifest: FamCal, start_url /dashboard, standalone, the app\'s own light colour, and the 192, 512 and maskable icons are real PNGs of those sizes under public/; the Home Screen icon is 180 square', async () => {
    const { default: manifest } = nodeRequire(path.join(ROOT, 'app/manifest.ts'));
    const { APP_TOKENS } = nodeRequire(path.join(ROOT, 'components/app-ui/tokens.ts'));
    const m: Row = manifest();
    assert(m.name === 'FamCal' && m.short_name === 'FamCal' && m.start_url === '/dashboard' && m.scope === '/' && m.display === 'standalone', `${show(m)}`);
    assert(m.background_color === APP_TOKENS.light.bg && m.theme_color === APP_TOKENS.light.bg, `colours ${m.background_color} ${m.theme_color}`);
    const icons: Row[] = m.icons;
    const byPurpose = (purpose: string) => icons.filter(i => i.purpose === purpose);
    assert(byPurpose('maskable').length === 1 && byPurpose('maskable')[0].sizes === '512x512', 'one maskable 512 icon');
    assert(show(byPurpose('any').map(i => i.sizes).sort()) === show(['192x192', '512x512']), `any icons: ${show(byPurpose('any'))}`);
    for (const icon of icons) {
      assert(icon.type === 'image/png' && String(icon.src).startsWith('/') && !String(icon.src).includes('..'), `${show(icon)}`);
      const size = pngSize(path.join(ROOT, 'public', icon.src));
      assert(`${size.width}x${size.height}` === icon.sizes, `${icon.src} is ${size.width}x${size.height}, the manifest says ${icon.sizes}`);
    }
    const apple = pngSize(path.join(ROOT, 'app/apple-icon.png'));
    assert(apple.width === 180 && apple.height === 180, `apple-icon.png is ${apple.width}x${apple.height}`);
    // The worker shows the 192 icon
    assert(icons.some(i => i.src === '/icon-192.png') && SOURCE.includes("'/icon-192.png'"), 'the worker\'s icon is not the manifest\'s 192');
  });

  await check('the proxy leaves /sw.js, the manifest and the icons alone (a path with a dot), and still routes the pages; /sw.js is served no-cache and no-store, and only /sw.js has that rule', async () => {
    const { config } = await import(path.join(ROOT, 'proxy.ts'));
    const matcher = new RegExp(`^${config.matcher[0]}$`);
    for (const skipped of ['/sw.js', '/manifest.webmanifest', '/icon-192.png', '/icon-512.png', '/icon-maskable-512.png', '/apple-icon.png']) assert(!matcher.test(skipped), `the proxy takes ${skipped}`);
    for (const routed of ['/dashboard', '/he/settings', '/']) assert(matcher.test(routed), `the proxy lost ${routed}`);
    const next = nodeRequire(path.join(ROOT, 'next.config.js'));
    const rules: Row[] = await next.headers();
    assert(rules.length === 1 && rules[0].source === '/sw.js', `header rules: ${show(rules)}`);
    const cacheControl = String(rules[0].headers.find((h: Row) => h.key === 'Cache-Control')?.value);
    assert(/\bno-cache\b/.test(cacheControl) && /\bno-store\b/.test(cacheControl), `Cache-Control: ${cacheControl}`);
  });

  await check('the head: the manifest and the Home Screen icon sit at the top of app/ (so all three root layouts link them), no layout sets metadata.manifest or icons over them, and the (app) layout gives a dark device its own bar colour from the app\'s tokens', () => {
    for (const file of ['app/manifest.ts', 'app/apple-icon.png', 'app/icon.png']) assert(fs.existsSync(path.join(ROOT, file)), `${file} is missing`);
    for (const layout of ['app/[locale]/(app)/layout.tsx', 'app/[locale]/(site)/layout.tsx', 'app/(connect)/layout.tsx']) {
      const text = fs.readFileSync(path.join(ROOT, layout), 'utf8').split('\n').filter(l => !/^\s*(\/\/|\*|\/\*)/.test(l)).join('\n');
      assert(!/\bmanifest\s*:|\bicons\s*:|apple-touch-icon|rel="manifest"/.test(text), `${layout} sets its own manifest or icons`);
    }
    const app = fs.readFileSync(path.join(ROOT, 'app/[locale]/(app)/layout.tsx'), 'utf8');
    assert(/themeColor: \[\s*\{ media: '\(prefers-color-scheme: light\)', color: APP_TOKENS\.light\.bg \},\s*\{ media: '\(prefers-color-scheme: dark\)', color: APP_TOKENS\.dark\.bg \},\s*\]/.test(app), 'the (app) layout has no light and dark themeColor from APP_TOKENS');
  });

  let failed = 0;
  for (const r of results) {
    console.log(`${r.ok ? 'OK' : 'FAIL'}  ${r.name}${r.ok ? '' : ` — ${r.reason}`}`);
    if (!r.ok) failed++;
  }
  console.log(`\n${results.length - failed}/${results.length} passed`);
  process.exit(failed ? 1 : 0);
}

main().catch(err => {
  process.stdout.write(`${err instanceof Error ? err.stack : String(err)}\n`);
  process.exit(1);
});
```
