**3a. Create `src/lib/web-push-client.ts`:**

```ts
// The browser's side of notifications (spec docs/superpowers/specs/2026-11-05-web-push-design.md §2.1, §2.4, §3.2, §3.3;
// plan 6c-2 Task 5): what the Settings card, the home's banner, the sign-outs and Remove a way (Task 6) ask of this browser.
// - pushSupport: 'ios-install' for an iPhone or an iPad in a browser tab (an iPad says it is a Mac, with touch points), where
//   notifications need FamCal added to the Home Screen (iOS 16.4 or later); 'unsupported' with no service workers, no
//   PushManager or no Notification; else 'supported'. The iPhone test comes first: a Safari tab has no PushManager.
// - turnOnPush (only from a tap): the worker's install (/sw.js at scope /, Task 4) and the permission prompt both start before
//   any await, so the prompt keeps the tap (Safari and Firefox ask only inside one); then the subscription (one the browser
//   holds is reused when it was made with FamCal's key) and POST /api/push/devices. A browser that wants a tap of its own for
//   the subscribe (NotAllowedError once the permission is granted) answers 'again'. Any answer but the server's ok undoes the
//   browser's subscription at once, so the browser never holds one the server refused (§2.1).
// - turnOffPush: the browser unsubscribes, then DELETE /api/push/devices with the endpoint. Without a user id (signInAgain)
//   only the browser: the server deletes the device when the push service answers "gone" at the next send.
// - checkPushOnOpen (every app open): reads the registration, never installs the worker; a subscription the server says isn't
//   this account's (an old account's, on a shared browser) is undone. No answer undoes nothing.
// - currentPushEndpoint: this browser's endpoint, for Remove a way (Task 6), which keeps this device.
// The routes take the account as every user route does (?user_id=, the DB id) and a browser's session only (no initData).
// Nothing here throws. A log line holds the tag, the step, a status, a known error code or an error's name: never an endpoint,
// a key or a body. Client-safe: two imports (one of them types only); nothing touches window at import.

import { readJsonBody } from '@/src/lib/read-json';
import type { PushPageProps } from '@/src/lib/web-push-props';

const TAG = '[web-push]';
const DEVICES = '/api/push/devices';
const CHECK = '/api/push/devices/check';
/** The worker Task 4 serves, and its scope. */
const WORKER_URL = '/sw.js';
const SCOPE = '/';
/** The routes' refusals (Task 3): the only error codes a log line may name. */
const KNOWN_ERRORS: readonly string[] = ['stale-sign-in', 'has-chat', 'push-off', 'bad-input'];

/**
 * Page-data's push (Settings and the home, Task 3): FamCal's public VAPID key (null while push is off), whether this session
 * proved its sign-in within the hour, and how many devices of the account have notifications on. Null inside Telegram and
 * for an account with a chat. The one type is Task 3's PushPageProps (src/lib/web-push-props.ts, types only); this is its
 * client-side name, never a second copy of the shape.
 */
export type PushProps = PushPageProps;

export type PushSupport = 'supported' | 'ios-install' | 'unsupported';

export type TurnOnResult = { ok: true; count: number } | { ok: false; reason: 'denied' | 'dismissed' | 'again' | 'stale' | 'refused' | 'failed' };

const errorName = (error: unknown): string => (error instanceof Error ? error.name : typeof error);

/** FamCal opened from the Home Screen (iOS's navigator.standalone, or the display mode the manifest asks for). */
function standalone(): boolean {
  try {
    if ((window.navigator as Navigator & { standalone?: boolean }).standalone === true) return true;
    return typeof window.matchMedia === 'function' && window.matchMedia('(display-mode: standalone)').matches;
  } catch (error) {
    console.warn(`${TAG} the display mode could not be read: ${errorName(error)}`);
    return false;
  }
}

/** Whether this browser can show FamCal's notifications here; read on the client only (the server's answer is 'unsupported'). */
export function pushSupport(): PushSupport {
  if (typeof window === 'undefined') return 'unsupported';
  try {
    const nav = window.navigator;
    if (!nav) return 'unsupported';
    const ua = nav.userAgent ?? '';
    const apple = /iPhone|iPad|iPod/.test(ua) || (/Macintosh/.test(ua) && (nav.maxTouchPoints ?? 0) > 1);
    if (apple && !standalone()) return 'ios-install';
    return 'serviceWorker' in nav && 'PushManager' in window && 'Notification' in window ? 'supported' : 'unsupported';
  } catch (error) {
    console.warn(`${TAG} this browser's support could not be read: ${errorName(error)}`);
    return 'unsupported';
  }
}

/** The person blocked FamCal's notifications in this browser (no prompt can show; the site settings can undo it). */
export function pushBlocked(): boolean {
  try {
    return typeof window !== 'undefined' && 'Notification' in window && window.Notification.permission === 'denied';
  } catch (error) {
    console.warn(`${TAG} the permission could not be read: ${errorName(error)}`);
    return false;
  }
}

/** The key as the bytes subscribe() takes; null (logged) when it isn't base64url. */
function keyBytes(key: string): Uint8Array<ArrayBuffer> | null {
  try {
    const raw = atob(key.replace(/-/g, '+').replace(/_/g, '/') + '='.repeat((4 - (key.length % 4)) % 4));
    const out = new Uint8Array(raw.length);
    for (let i = 0; i < raw.length; i++) out[i] = raw.charCodeAt(i);
    return out;
  } catch (error) {
    console.error(`${TAG} the public key is not base64url: ${errorName(error)}`);
    return null;
  }
}

/** The subscription was made with this key (a browser that doesn't say is taken at its word: the key never changes, §3.9). */
function sameKey(subscription: PushSubscription, bytes: Uint8Array): boolean {
  const have = subscription.options?.applicationServerKey;
  if (!have) return true;
  const view = new Uint8Array(have);
  return view.length === bytes.length && view.every((b, i) => b === bytes[i]);
}

/** The registration once its worker is active (subscribe() needs one); null (logged) when the worker never installs. No timer of its own. */
function activated(registration: ServiceWorkerRegistration): Promise<ServiceWorkerRegistration | null> {
  if (registration.active) return Promise.resolve(registration);
  const worker = registration.installing ?? registration.waiting;
  if (!worker) {
    console.error(`${TAG} the registration has no worker`);
    return Promise.resolve(null);
  }
  return new Promise((resolve) => {
    const settle = () => {
      if (worker.state === 'activated') {
        worker.removeEventListener('statechange', settle);
        resolve(registration);
      } else if (worker.state === 'redundant') {
        worker.removeEventListener('statechange', settle);
        console.error(`${TAG} the worker did not install`);
        resolve(null);
      }
    };
    worker.addEventListener('statechange', settle);
    settle();
  });
}

/** The worker's registration, installed now when there is none (only turnOnPush calls this: an app open never installs it). Never rejects. */
async function registrationForPush(): Promise<ServiceWorkerRegistration | null> {
  try {
    const container = window.navigator.serviceWorker;
    const registration = (await container.getRegistration(SCOPE)) ?? (await container.register(WORKER_URL, { scope: SCOPE }));
    return await activated(registration);
  } catch (error) {
    console.error(`${TAG} the worker could not be installed: ${errorName(error)}`);
    return null;
  }
}

/** The subscription this browser holds for FamCal, or null (none, no worker, or a failure, logged). Never installs the worker. */
async function currentSubscription(): Promise<PushSubscription | null> {
  if (typeof window === 'undefined') return null;
  try {
    const container = window.navigator?.serviceWorker;
    if (!container || !('PushManager' in window)) return null;
    const registration = await container.getRegistration(SCOPE);
    return registration ? await registration.pushManager.getSubscription() : null;
  } catch (error) {
    console.error(`${TAG} this browser's subscription could not be read: ${errorName(error)}`);
    return null;
  }
}

/** Unsubscribes this browser; whether it did (a refusal or a throw is logged). */
async function undo(subscription: PushSubscription): Promise<boolean> {
  try {
    const done = await subscription.unsubscribe();
    if (!done) console.warn(`${TAG} the browser kept its subscription`);
    return done;
  } catch (error) {
    console.error(`${TAG} unsubscribing failed: ${errorName(error)}`);
    return false;
  }
}

/** One request for the account (?user_id=): its status and body, or null when it got no answer (logged). */
async function send(method: 'POST' | 'DELETE', pathname: string, userId: number, body: Record<string, unknown>): Promise<{ status: number; ok: boolean; body: Record<string, unknown> | null } | null> {
  try {
    const res = await fetch(`${pathname}?user_id=${userId}`, {
      method,
      credentials: 'same-origin',
      headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' },
      body: JSON.stringify(body),
    });
    const answer = await readJsonBody(res, `${TAG} ${method} ${pathname}`);
    if (!res.ok) {
      const code = typeof answer?.error === 'string' && KNOWN_ERRORS.includes(answer.error) ? answer.error : null;
      console.warn(`${TAG} ${method} ${pathname} answered ${res.status} (${code ?? 'no known code'})`);
    }
    return { status: res.status, ok: res.ok, body: answer };
  } catch (error) {
    console.error(`${TAG} ${method} ${pathname} got no answer: ${errorName(error)}`);
    return null;
  }
}

/** Turns this browser's notifications on for the account. Call it in the tap itself, synchronously: its first lines ask the browser. */
export async function turnOnPush(userId: number, key: string): Promise<TurnOnResult> {
  if (pushSupport() !== 'supported') {
    console.warn(`${TAG} turn on was asked where this browser can't show notifications`);
    return { ok: false, reason: 'failed' };
  }
  const notification = window.Notification;
  if (notification.permission === 'denied') {
    console.warn(`${TAG} notifications are blocked in this browser`);
    return { ok: false, reason: 'denied' };
  }
  // Both start here, in the tap, before any await: the prompt needs the tap, and the worker installs while it is up
  const registering = registrationForPush();
  let permission: NotificationPermission;
  try {
    permission = notification.permission === 'granted' ? 'granted' : (await notification.requestPermission()) || notification.permission;
  } catch (error) {
    console.error(`${TAG} the permission prompt failed: ${errorName(error)}`);
    permission = notification.permission;
  }
  const registration = await registering;
  if (permission === 'denied') {
    console.warn(`${TAG} the permission was refused`);
    return { ok: false, reason: 'denied' };
  }
  if (permission !== 'granted') {
    console.warn(`${TAG} the browser did not ask, or the prompt was closed (${permission})`);
    return { ok: false, reason: 'dismissed' };
  }
  const bytes = keyBytes(key);
  if (!registration || !bytes) return { ok: false, reason: 'failed' };

  let subscription: PushSubscription;
  try {
    const held = await registration.pushManager.getSubscription();
    if (held && !sameKey(held, bytes)) {
      // Made with another key: the push service would refuse every push to it, so it is made again
      console.warn(`${TAG} the browser's subscription has another key; it is made again`);
      await held.unsubscribe();
    }
    subscription = held && sameKey(held, bytes) ? held : await registration.pushManager.subscribe({ userVisibleOnly: true, applicationServerKey: bytes });
  } catch (error) {
    const name = errorName(error);
    if (name === 'NotAllowedError') {
      console.warn(`${TAG} the browser wants a tap of its own to subscribe (NotAllowedError)`);
      return { ok: false, reason: 'again' };
    }
    console.error(`${TAG} subscribing failed: ${name}`);
    return { ok: false, reason: 'failed' };
  }

  const keys = subscription.toJSON().keys;
  if (typeof keys?.p256dh !== 'string' || typeof keys?.auth !== 'string') {
    console.error(`${TAG} the subscription has no keys`);
    await undo(subscription);
    return { ok: false, reason: 'failed' };
  }
  const answer = await send('POST', DEVICES, userId, { endpoint: subscription.endpoint, keys: { p256dh: keys.p256dh, auth: keys.auth } });
  if (answer?.ok && answer.body?.ok === true && typeof answer.body.count === 'number') return { ok: true, count: answer.body.count };
  // Refused or unanswered: the browser keeps no subscription the server may not hold
  await undo(subscription);
  if (answer && (answer.status === 401 || (answer.status === 403 && answer.body?.error === 'stale-sign-in'))) return { ok: false, reason: 'stale' };
  if (answer?.status === 409 && answer.body?.error === 'has-chat') return { ok: false, reason: 'refused' };
  return { ok: false, reason: 'failed' };
}

/**
 * Turns this browser's notifications off: it unsubscribes, then (with a user id) the device is deleted. True when this browser
 * gets no more notifications: it unsubscribed, or the server deleted its device. A failed DELETE is logged in send; the
 * device then goes at the next send, when the push service answers "gone". Nothing held: true, no request.
 */
export async function turnOffPush(userId: number | null): Promise<boolean> {
  const subscription = await currentSubscription();
  if (!subscription) return true;
  const endpoint = subscription.endpoint;
  const off = await undo(subscription);
  if (userId === null) return off;
  const answer = await send('DELETE', DEVICES, userId, { endpoint });
  return off || (answer?.ok === true && answer.body?.ok === true);
}

/** Every app open: whether this browser gets this account's notifications. Another account's subscription is undone; no answer undoes nothing. */
export async function checkPushOnOpen(userId: number): Promise<'on' | 'off'> {
  if (pushSupport() !== 'supported') return 'off';
  const subscription = await currentSubscription();
  if (!subscription) return 'off';
  const answer = await send('POST', CHECK, userId, { endpoint: subscription.endpoint });
  if (answer?.ok && answer.body?.ok === true && typeof answer.body.mine === 'boolean') {
    if (answer.body.mine) return 'on';
    console.warn(`${TAG} this browser's subscription is not this account's; undone`);
    await undo(subscription);
  }
  return 'off';
}

/** This browser's endpoint for FamCal, or null (Remove a way sends it, so this device stays: Task 6). Never installs the worker. */
export async function currentPushEndpoint(): Promise<string | null> {
  return (await currentSubscription())?.endpoint ?? null;
}
```

If `npx tsc` rejects `Uint8Array<ArrayBuffer>` (TS 5.9.3 here accepts it) or `applicationServerKey: bytes`, keep the runtime as written and type the return `Uint8Array` with `applicationServerKey: bytes as BufferSource`.

**3b. Create `src/lib/use-push-device.ts`:**

```ts
'use client';

// One device's notifications on a screen (spec 2026-11-05-web-push §2.1, §2.4; plan 6c-2 Task 5): Settings' card
// "Notifications on this device" and the home's banner "Get reminders on this device" share it, as the Connect Telegram
// buttons share useTelegramConnect.
// - Every app open checks this browser (checkPushOnOpen, which never installs the worker): a subscription this account has
//   shows as on, another account's (a shared browser) is undone there. It runs for every account in a browser (with no
//   subscription it makes no request), never inside Telegram. The home always calls it; Settings only in its card.
// - The press turns on (turnOnPush runs in the tap itself: the browser's prompt needs the tap), turns off while on, or, when
//   turning on needs a sign-in from the last hour (the page's push.recentSignIn, or the route's stale answer), signs this
//   browser in again (signInAgain) and asks the browser nothing.
// - What came of a press is `line`, worded by pushLineKey (keys of settings); 'again' is the switch's own words.
// - onDevices follows the account's count of devices that are on (Settings' rows a notification can honour come back with one).
// Client-safe; never throws.

import { useEffect, useRef, useState, useSyncExternalStore } from 'react';
import { signInAgain } from './sign-in-again';
import { isInTelegram } from './telegram-ui';
import { checkPushOnOpen, pushBlocked, pushSupport, turnOffPush, turnOnPush, type PushProps, type PushSupport } from './web-push-client';

/**
 * Where this device is: 'checking' until the open's check answers; 'busy' turning on, 'stopping' turning off; 'stale' when
 * turning on needs a sign-in from the last hour first, 'leaving' on the way to /signin.
 */
export type PushPhase = 'checking' | 'off' | 'busy' | 'on' | 'stopping' | 'stale' | 'leaving';
/** What came of a press ('denied' also of the open): worded by pushLineKey. 'again' is the switch's own words, not a line. */
export type PushLine = 'again' | 'dismissed' | 'denied' | 'refused' | 'failed' | 'offFailed' | 'signInFailed';

export interface PushDevice {
  /** null until the client has read it (the server's render and hydration). */
  support: PushSupport | null;
  phase: PushPhase;
  line: PushLine | null;
  /** The tap: turn on (in the tap itself), turn off, or sign in again. Nothing while a press runs or before the open's check. */
  press: () => void;
}

/** The Home Screen steps for iPhone and iPad, keys of settings: the card's and the banner's. */
export const IOS_STEPS = ['push.ios.step1', 'push.ios.step2', 'push.ios.step3'] as const;

/** A line's words (a key of settings) and whether it is a problem (an alert); null for none and for 'again'. */
export function pushLineKey(line: PushLine | null): { key: string; problem: boolean } | null {
  switch (line) {
    case 'dismissed':
      return { key: 'push.dismissed', problem: false };
    case 'denied':
      return { key: 'push.denied', problem: false };
    case 'refused':
      return { key: 'push.refused', problem: true };
    case 'failed':
      return { key: 'push.failed', problem: true };
    case 'offFailed':
      return { key: 'push.offFailed', problem: true };
    case 'signInFailed':
      return { key: 'signIn.failed', problem: true };
    default:
      return null;
  }
}

/** Nothing to subscribe to: the support is read once on the client. */
const subscribeNothing = () => () => {};
/** On the server and while hydrating: not read yet. */
const notRead = () => null;

export function usePushDevice(userId: number, push: PushProps | null | undefined, locale: string, onDevices?: (update: (count: number) => number) => void): PushDevice {
  const support = useSyncExternalStore<PushSupport | null>(subscribeNothing, pushSupport, notRead);
  const [phase, setPhase] = useState<PushPhase>('checking');
  const [line, setLine] = useState<PushLine | null>(null);
  const running = useRef(false);
  const recent = push?.recentSignIn === true;
  const key = push?.key ?? null;

  // The open's check: never inside Telegram, never where push can't work (no request without a subscription)
  useEffect(() => {
    if (support !== 'supported' || isInTelegram()) return;
    let live = true;
    void checkPushOnOpen(userId).then((state) => {
      if (!live) return;
      setPhase(state === 'on' ? 'on' : recent ? 'off' : 'stale');
      setLine(state === 'off' && pushBlocked() ? 'denied' : null);
    });
    return () => {
      live = false;
    };
  }, [support, userId, recent]);

  const press = () => {
    if (running.current || !key || support !== 'supported') return;
    if (phase === 'stale') {
      running.current = true;
      setPhase('leaving');
      setLine(null);
      void signInAgain(locale).then((left) => {
        // Leaving: the page is on its way to /signin and keeps its spinner; a failure lets it be pressed again
        if (left) return;
        running.current = false;
        setPhase('stale');
        setLine('signInFailed');
      });
      return;
    }
    if (phase === 'on') {
      running.current = true;
      setPhase('stopping');
      setLine(null);
      void turnOffPush(userId).then((off) => {
        running.current = false;
        if (!off) {
          setPhase('on');
          setLine('offFailed');
          return;
        }
        setPhase(recent ? 'off' : 'stale');
        onDevices?.((count) => Math.max(0, count - 1));
      });
      return;
    }
    if (phase !== 'off') return;
    running.current = true;
    setPhase('busy');
    setLine(null);
    // Synchronously in the tap: turnOnPush's first lines start the prompt and the install
    void turnOnPush(userId, key).then((result) => {
      running.current = false;
      if (result.ok) {
        setPhase('on');
        onDevices?.(() => result.count);
        return;
      }
      if (result.reason === 'stale') {
        setPhase('stale');
        return;
      }
      setPhase('off');
      setLine(result.reason);
    });
  };

  return { support, phase, line, press };
}
```

**3c. Create `app/[locale]/(app)/settings/PushCard.tsx`:**

```tsx
'use client';

// Settings' "Notifications on this device" card (spec 2026-11-05-web-push §2.1, §2.4; plan 6c-2 Task 5), for an account with
// no chat in a real browser while push is set up (page-data's push.key; Settings renders it only then, right above Sign in on
// the web). It saves itself, as Sign in on the web does: it is not part of the Save bar. Its states (usePushDevice):
// - a browser that can't show notifications (no service worker or no push): one line;
// - an iPhone or an iPad in a browser tab: the three steps to add FamCal to the Home Screen (iOS 16.4 or later), no switch;
// - a sign-in over an hour old: Sign in again (which asks the browser nothing) and the hour's note;
// - checking, off, turning on, on, turning off: the switch; "Tap again to allow" when the browser wants a tap of its own;
// - what came of a press: a notice (not answered and blocked are notes; refused, failed and a failed turn off are alerts).
// onDevices tells the screen the account's count of devices that are on (its rows a notification can honour follow it).
// Strings: settings.push.*. Lucide's spinner only.

import type { ReactNode } from 'react';
import { useLocale, useTranslations } from 'next-intl';
import { Loader2 } from 'lucide-react';
import { FormCard, FormRow, SwitchRow } from '@/components/app-ui/form-kit';
import { Notice } from '@/components/app-ui/Notice';
import { SectionLabel } from '@/components/app-ui/ScreenHeader';
import { IOS_STEPS, pushLineKey, usePushDevice } from '@/src/lib/use-push-device';
import type { PushProps } from '@/src/lib/web-push-client';

export default function PushCard({ userId, push, onDevices }: { userId: number; push: PushProps; onDevices: (update: (count: number) => number) => void }) {
  const t = useTranslations('settings');
  const locale = useLocale();
  const device = usePushDevice(userId, push, locale, onDevices);
  const said = pushLineKey(device.line);
  const working = (text: string) => (
    <>
      <Loader2 size={16} className="ch-spin" aria-hidden="true" /> {text}
    </>
  );

  let card: ReactNode;
  if (device.support === null) {
    card = (
      <FormCard>
        <div className="ch-form-row">
          <p className="ch-form-fixed ch-working" role="status">
            {working(t('push.checking'))}
          </p>
        </div>
      </FormCard>
    );
  } else if (device.support === 'unsupported') {
    card = (
      <FormCard>
        <FormRow label={t('push.unsupported')} />
      </FormCard>
    );
  } else if (device.support === 'ios-install') {
    card = (
      <FormCard>
        <FormRow label={t('push.ios.title')} hint={t('push.ios.body')} />
        <div className="ch-form-row">
          <ol className="ch-sheet-list">
            {IOS_STEPS.map((key) => (
              <li key={key}>{t(key)}</li>
            ))}
          </ol>
        </div>
      </FormCard>
    );
  } else if (device.phase === 'stale' || device.phase === 'leaving') {
    const leaving = device.phase === 'leaving';
    card = (
      <>
        <FormCard>
          <FormRow label={t('push.switchOff')} hint={t('push.offHint')} />
          <button type="button" className="ch-form-row ch-action-row" disabled={leaving} aria-busy={leaving || undefined} onClick={device.press}>
            {leaving ? working(t('push.signInAgain')) : t('push.signInAgain')}
          </button>
        </FormCard>
        <Notice>{t('push.staleNote')}</Notice>
      </>
    );
  } else {
    const on = device.phase === 'on' || device.phase === 'stopping';
    const label =
      device.phase === 'checking'
        ? working(t('push.checking'))
        : device.phase === 'busy'
          ? working(t('push.turningOn'))
          : device.phase === 'stopping'
            ? working(t('push.turningOff'))
            : on
              ? t('push.switchOn')
              : device.line === 'again'
                ? t('push.again')
                : t('push.switchOff');
    card = (
      <FormCard>
        <SwitchRow label={label} hint={on ? t('push.onHint') : t('push.offHint')} checked={on} disabled={device.phase !== 'on' && device.phase !== 'off'} onChange={device.press} />
      </FormCard>
    );
  }

  return (
    <>
      <SectionLabel>{t('push.title')}</SectionLabel>
      {card}
      {said ? <Notice tone={said.problem ? 'error' : 'info'}>{t(said.key)}</Notice> : null}
    </>
  );
}
```

**3d. Replace `app/[locale]/(app)/dashboard/HomeBanners.tsx` with this whole file** (everything that was there is kept; added: the header bullet, the `Bell` import and the two push imports, `BannerFacts.pushBanner`, `homeBanners`' one condition, `pushBannerOf`, `HomeBannersProps.pushDevice`, `PushBanner`, and the `connectTelegram` case's choice):

```tsx
'use client';

// The banners under the home's header (spec §7.2, §5 States), stacked and non-blocking,
// in this order:
// - trial days left, during a trial (→ Subscription);
// - "Set your location for the right times", when the server can't know the user's
//   timezone: no location, and no Google connection to read it from (→ Settings);
// - Choose your calendars: a Google token with no calendars chosen;
// - Reconnect Google: a token cleared while calendars are stored (the page's state, since
//   the range read has no Google half then), or, once a Google answer in this epoch asked
//   for it (it holds until the store drops), a dead token or missing permission (the
//   scopes copy);
// - in a browser, "Open FamCal in Telegram", only for an account with no Telegram but a chat (WhatsApp stays
//   secondary to Telegram); an account that has Telegram already never sees it, and its link
//   starts WhatsApp linking;
// - in a browser, "Connect Telegram" (for reminders and briefings) for an account with no chat at all (6c-1), until it is
//   closed on this device (home-storage.ts). Its press asks for the one-time bot link (telegram-connect-client.ts); a sign-in
//   over an hour old turns it into "Sign in again to connect Telegram"; what came of it is a line under it.
//   While push is set up and this browser can show notifications (6c-2, pushBannerOf), it is "Get reminders on this device"
//   instead: Turn on (the Settings switch's flow, usePushDevice) or, on iPhone and iPad in a browser tab, the three Home Screen
//   steps, with Connect Telegram as its second action and the same close. It hides once this device is on (and while the open's
//   check is out); a browser that can't show notifications keeps Connect Telegram.
// Each banner is one button (or, for Telegram, a link) with a 44 px or taller target; Connect Telegram is two (its own and the
// close), Get reminders on this device three (Turn on, Connect Telegram, the close).
// Google is a connection beside FamCal (spec "Google as a connection" §2): a user who never
// connected sees no Google banner at all, and Connect Google lives in the ⋯ menu only.

import { useTranslations } from 'next-intl';
import { Bell, Loader2, Send, X } from 'lucide-react';
import { DEFAULT_BOT_USERNAME } from '@/src/lib/gate-links';
import type { GoogleState } from '@/src/lib/google-state';
import { connectButtonKey, connectLineKey, useTelegramConnect } from '@/src/lib/telegram-connect-client';
import { IOS_STEPS, pushLineKey, type PushDevice } from '@/src/lib/use-push-device';
import type { PushProps } from '@/src/lib/web-push-client';

export type BannerId = 'trial' | 'location' | 'chooseCalendars' | 'reconnect' | 'reconnectScopes' | 'openInTelegram' | 'connectTelegram';

export interface BannerFacts {
  /** The page's Google state (googleStateOf). */
  googleState: GoogleState;
  /** A Google token is stored. */
  googleConnected: boolean;
  /** Days left in a trial; null or absent: no trial banner. */
  trialDaysLeft?: number | null;
  /** false: no location set; absent: unknown, no location banner. */
  hasLocation?: boolean;
  /** A Google answer in this epoch asked for a reconnect (homeNotices: it holds until the store drops). */
  reconnect: 'token' | 'scopes' | null;
  /** readInitData() is non-empty. */
  inTelegram: boolean;
  /** The account has a Telegram id. false: WhatsApp-only, who gets the Telegram banner in a browser. Absent means unknown: no banner. */
  hasTelegram?: boolean;
  /** false: no chat at all (made on famcal.bot): Connect Telegram in a browser. Absent: has one, as before. */
  hasChat?: boolean;
  /** This device closed the Connect Telegram banner for this account. */
  connectClosed?: boolean;
  /**
   * This device's notifications (6c-2, pushBannerOf): 'offer' turns Connect Telegram into "Get reminders on this device",
   * 'hidden' hides it (this device is on, or the open's check is out). Absent: Connect Telegram as before (push is off, or this
   * browser can't show notifications).
   */
  pushBanner?: 'offer' | 'hidden';
}

/** Which banners show, in order. */
export function homeBanners(f: BannerFacts): BannerId[] {
  const out: BannerId[] = [];
  if (typeof f.trialDaysLeft === 'number' && f.trialDaysLeft > 0) out.push('trial');
  // With a Google token the zone comes from Google; without one, only a location tells it.
  if (f.hasLocation === false && !f.googleConnected) out.push('location');
  if (f.googleState === 'choose') out.push('chooseCalendars');
  // A cleared token has no Google half in the read, so the page's state says it; otherwise a Google answer does.
  if (f.googleState === 'reconnect') out.push('reconnect');
  else if (f.googleState !== 'none' && f.reconnect) out.push(f.reconnect === 'scopes' ? 'reconnectScopes' : 'reconnect');
  if (!f.inTelegram && f.hasTelegram === false && f.hasChat !== false) out.push('openInTelegram');
  if (!f.inTelegram && f.hasChat === false && !f.connectClosed && f.pushBanner !== 'hidden') out.push('connectTelegram');
  return out;
}

/**
 * What this device's notifications make of the Connect Telegram banner (6c-2): nothing without push set up (no key) or where
 * this browser can't show them; hidden until the client has read the browser, while the open's check is out, and while this
 * device is on; else the offer (the iPhone steps as soon as they are known).
 */
export function pushBannerOf(push: PushProps | null | undefined, device: Pick<PushDevice, 'support' | 'phase'>): BannerFacts['pushBanner'] {
  if (!push?.key || device.support === 'unsupported') return undefined;
  if (device.support === 'ios-install') return 'offer';
  if (device.support === null || device.phase === 'checking' || device.phase === 'on' || device.phase === 'stopping') return 'hidden';
  return 'offer';
}

/** The bot's /start connect link: it links a WhatsApp account to Telegram (telegram/commands.ts). */
export const OPEN_IN_TELEGRAM_URL = `https://t.me/${DEFAULT_BOT_USERNAME}?start=connect`;

export interface HomeBannersProps extends BannerFacts {
  /** The page's own user_id, as CalendarHomeProps carries it. */
  userId: number;
  locale: string;
  onNavigate: (href: string) => void;
  /** Closes the Connect Telegram banner (the home remembers it on the device); absent: no close. */
  onCloseConnect?: () => void;
  /** This device's notifications (the home's usePushDevice), for "Get reminders on this device"; absent: Connect Telegram. */
  pushDevice?: PushDevice;
}

type Tone = 'plain' | 'accent' | 'warn';

function BannerIcon({ id }: { id: BannerId }) {
  const common = { className: 'ch-banner-icon', viewBox: '0 0 24 24', fill: 'none', stroke: 'currentColor', strokeWidth: 2, strokeLinecap: 'round' as const, strokeLinejoin: 'round' as const, 'aria-hidden': true, focusable: false };
  switch (id) {
    case 'trial':
      return (
        <svg {...common}>
          <path d="M12 3l2.7 5.5 6 .9-4.3 4.2 1 6L12 16.8 6.6 19.6l1-6L3.3 9.4l6-.9z" />
        </svg>
      );
    case 'location':
      return (
        <svg {...common}>
          <path d="M12 21s-7-6.2-7-11.5a7 7 0 0 1 14 0C19 14.8 12 21 12 21z" />
          <circle cx="12" cy="9.5" r="2.5" />
        </svg>
      );
    case 'reconnect':
    case 'reconnectScopes':
      return (
        <svg {...common}>
          <circle cx="12" cy="12" r="9" />
          <path d="M12 7.5v5.5M12 16.5v.01" />
        </svg>
      );
    case 'openInTelegram':
      return (
        <svg {...common} className="ch-banner-icon ch-flip">
          <path d="M22 2L11 13" />
          <path d="M22 2l-7 20-4-9-9-4 20-7z" />
        </svg>
      );
    default:
      return (
        <svg {...common}>
          <rect x="3" y="5" width="18" height="16" rx="3" />
          <path d="M3 10h18M8 3v4M16 3v4" />
        </svg>
      );
  }
}

function Chevron() {
  return (
    <svg className="ch-banner-chevron ch-flip" viewBox="0 0 24 24" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" strokeWidth="2" strokeLinecap="round" strokeLinejoin="round" aria-hidden="true" focusable="false">
      <path d="M9 6l6 6-6 6" />
    </svg>
  );
}

function BannerContent({ id, title, body, action }: { id: BannerId; title: string; body?: string; action?: string }) {
  return (
    <>
      <BannerIcon id={id} />
      <span className="ch-banner-text">
        <span className="ch-banner-title">{title}</span>
        {body ? <span className="ch-banner-body">{body}</span> : null}
      </span>
      {action ? <span className="ch-banner-action">{action}</span> : <Chevron />}
    </>
  );
}

/**
 * Connect Telegram, for an account with no chat (6c-1): the banner's own button (the bot's one-time link, or Sign in again
 * after a stale sign-in) and its close, side by side in one banner; what came of a press is a line under it.
 */
function ConnectTelegramBanner({ userId, locale, onClose }: { userId: number; locale: string; onClose?: () => void }) {
  const t = useTranslations('calendarHome');
  const c = useTranslations('telegramConnect');
  const { phase, press } = useTelegramConnect(userId, locale);
  const working = phase === 'busy' || phase === 'leaving';
  const line = connectLineKey(phase);
  return (
    <>
      <div className="ch-banner ch-banner-telegram ch-banner-split" data-banner="connectTelegram">
        <button type="button" className="ch-banner-main ch-tap" disabled={working} aria-busy={working || undefined} onClick={press}>
          <Send className="ch-banner-icon ch-flip" aria-hidden="true" focusable="false" />
          <span className="ch-banner-text">
            <span className="ch-banner-title">{c(connectButtonKey(phase))}</span>
            <span className="ch-banner-body">{t('banner.connectTelegramBody')}</span>
          </span>
          {working ? <Loader2 className="ch-banner-chevron ch-spin" aria-hidden="true" focusable="false" /> : <Chevron />}
        </button>
        {onClose ? (
          <button type="button" className="ch-banner-close ch-tap" aria-label={t('banner.close')} onClick={onClose}>
            <X aria-hidden="true" focusable="false" />
          </button>
        ) : null}
      </div>
      {line ? (
        <p className={line.problem ? 'ch-banner-line is-problem' : 'ch-banner-line'} role={line.problem ? 'alert' : 'status'}>
          {c(line.key)}
        </p>
      ) : null}
    </>
  );
}

/**
 * Get reminders on this device (6c-2 §2.1): the icon, the words and the close on top; the iPhone steps under them in a browser
 * tab; then Turn on (or Sign in again, or "Tap again to allow") and Connect Telegram. What came of either press is a line under it.
 */
function PushBanner({ userId, locale, device, onClose }: { userId: number; locale: string; device: PushDevice; onClose?: () => void }) {
  const t = useTranslations('calendarHome');
  const s = useTranslations('settings');
  const c = useTranslations('telegramConnect');
  const connect = useTelegramConnect(userId, locale);
  const connecting = connect.phase === 'busy' || connect.phase === 'leaving';
  const connectLine = connectLineKey(connect.phase);
  const ios = device.support === 'ios-install';
  const stale = device.phase === 'stale' || device.phase === 'leaving';
  const working = device.phase === 'busy' || device.phase === 'leaving' || device.phase === 'stopping';
  const said = pushLineKey(device.line);
  const turnOnLabel = device.phase === 'busy' ? s('push.turningOn') : stale ? s('push.signInAgain') : device.line === 'again' ? s('push.again') : t('banner.pushAction');
  const spin = <Loader2 size={16} className="ch-spin" aria-hidden="true" focusable="false" />;
  return (
    <>
      <div className="ch-banner ch-banner-telegram ch-banner-push" data-banner="connectTelegram" data-push="offer">
        <div className="ch-banner-push-head">
          <Bell className="ch-banner-icon" aria-hidden="true" focusable="false" />
          <span className="ch-banner-text">
            <span className="ch-banner-title">{t('banner.pushTitle')}</span>
            <span className="ch-banner-body">{ios ? s('push.ios.body') : t('banner.pushBody')}</span>
          </span>
          {onClose ? (
            <button type="button" className="ch-banner-close ch-tap" aria-label={t('banner.close')} onClick={onClose}>
              <X aria-hidden="true" focusable="false" />
            </button>
          ) : null}
        </div>
        {ios ? (
          <ol className="ch-banner-steps">
            {IOS_STEPS.map((key) => (
              <li key={key}>{s(key)}</li>
            ))}
          </ol>
        ) : null}
        <div className="ch-banner-push-actions">
          {ios ? null : (
            <button type="button" className="ch-btn ch-btn-primary" disabled={working} aria-busy={working || undefined} onClick={device.press}>
              {working ? spin : null}
              {turnOnLabel}
            </button>
          )}
          <button type="button" className="ch-btn" disabled={connecting} aria-busy={connecting || undefined} onClick={connect.press}>
            {connecting ? spin : null}
            {c(connectButtonKey(connect.phase))}
          </button>
        </div>
      </div>
      {said ? (
        <p className={said.problem ? 'ch-banner-line is-problem' : 'ch-banner-line'} role={said.problem ? 'alert' : 'status'}>
          {s(said.key)}
        </p>
      ) : null}
      {stale ? (
        <p className="ch-banner-line" role="status">
          {s('push.staleNote')}
        </p>
      ) : null}
      {connectLine ? (
        <p className={connectLine.problem ? 'ch-banner-line is-problem' : 'ch-banner-line'} role={connectLine.problem ? 'alert' : 'status'}>
          {c(connectLine.key)}
        </p>
      ) : null}
    </>
  );
}

export function HomeBanners({ userId, locale, onNavigate, onCloseConnect, pushDevice, ...facts }: HomeBannersProps) {
  const t = useTranslations('calendarHome');
  const ids = homeBanners(facts);
  if (ids.length === 0) return null;

  const button = (id: BannerId, tone: Tone, href: string, title: string, body?: string, action?: string) => (
    <button key={id} type="button" className={`ch-banner ch-tap ch-banner-${tone}`} data-banner={id} onClick={() => onNavigate(href)}>
      <BannerContent id={id} title={title} body={body} action={action} />
    </button>
  );
  const page = (name: string) => `/${locale}/${name}?user_id=${userId}`;
  const refreshToken = `/refresh-token?user_id=${userId}`;

  return (
    <div className="ch-banners">
      {ids.map((id) => {
        switch (id) {
          case 'trial':
            return button(id, 'accent', page('subscription'), t('banner.trial', { count: facts.trialDaysLeft ?? 0 }), undefined, t('banner.trialAction'));
          case 'location':
            return button(id, 'plain', page('settings'), t('banner.location'), undefined, t('banner.locationAction'));
          case 'chooseCalendars':
            return button(id, 'accent', page('select-calendars'), t('banner.chooseCalendars'), t('banner.chooseCalendarsBody'));
          case 'reconnect':
            return button(id, 'warn', refreshToken, t('banner.reconnect'), t('banner.reconnectBody'), t('banner.reconnectAction'));
          case 'reconnectScopes':
            return button(id, 'warn', refreshToken, t('banner.reconnectScopes'), t('banner.reconnectScopesBody'), t('banner.reconnectAction'));
          case 'openInTelegram':
            return (
              <a key={id} className="ch-banner ch-tap ch-banner-telegram" data-banner={id} href={OPEN_IN_TELEGRAM_URL} target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">
                <BannerContent id={id} title={t('banner.openInTelegram')} body={t('banner.openInTelegramBody')} />
              </a>
            );
          case 'connectTelegram':
            return facts.pushBanner === 'offer' && pushDevice ? (
              <PushBanner key={id} userId={userId} locale={locale} device={pushDevice} onClose={onCloseConnect} />
            ) : (
              <ConnectTelegramBanner key={id} userId={userId} locale={locale} onClose={onCloseConnect} />
            );
        }
      })}
    </div>
  );
}
```
Diff it against the old file (`git diff`) before going on: only the listed parts may differ.

**3j. `app/[locale]/(app)/settings/signin-methods-client.ts`:** add to the imports (after `import { readJsonBody } from '@/src/lib/read-json';`) `import { turnOffPush } from '@/src/lib/web-push-client';`. Old (:149-157)
```ts
export async function signOutHere(userId: number): Promise<{ kind: 'ok' } | { kind: 'error'; code: RequestError }> {
  const r = await post('/api/auth/signout', userId, {});
  return r?.ok && r.body?.ok === true ? { kind: 'ok' } : { kind: 'error', code: r ? statusError(r.status) : 'failed' };
}

export async function signOutAll(userId: number): Promise<{ kind: 'ok' } | { kind: 'error'; code: RequestError }> {
  const r = await post('/api/auth/signout-all', userId, {});
```
new
```ts
/**
 * Sign out on this device. This browser's notifications end with its sign-in (6c-2 §2.4): it unsubscribes and its device is
 * deleted first (turnOffPush; a failure is logged there and never stops the sign-out, whose route stays query-free).
 */
export async function signOutHere(userId: number): Promise<{ kind: 'ok' } | { kind: 'error'; code: RequestError }> {
  await turnOffPush(userId);
  const r = await post('/api/auth/signout', userId, {});
  return r?.ok && r.body?.ok === true ? { kind: 'ok' } : { kind: 'error', code: r ? statusError(r.status) : 'failed' };
}

/** Sign out on all devices: this browser unsubscribes first, as for signOutHere (the route deletes every other device: Task 6). */
export async function signOutAll(userId: number): Promise<{ kind: 'ok' } | { kind: 'error'; code: RequestError }> {
  await turnOffPush(userId);
  const r = await post('/api/auth/signout-all', userId, {});
```

### Task 6: Ending devices (the session writers, Remove keeping its own device, Connect Telegram and the Join)

Spec §2.4, §3.7. The four writers that move `User.sessionsValidAfter` delete the account's browser devices in the same
write; Remove keeps the device it was pressed on; Connect Telegram and a merge into an account with a chat delete the
devices; a merge's W takes its devices with it (ON DELETE CASCADE at `tx.user.delete`, account-linking.ts:642).

Rules for this task:
- `afterDevicesChanged([ids])` runs after the commit and ONLY when the delete removed at least one row. A Telegram or
  WhatsApp account never has a device (the subscribe route answers 409 has-chat), so for it nothing new runs after the
  write: its reminder-cache entry is not rewritten and its behaviour stays byte for byte.
- No log line holds an endpoint or a key: the user id and the count only.
- `src/lib/user-moderation.ts` loads `devices.ts` with `await import(...)`, as it already loads `identities.ts`
  (user-moderation.ts:407): `user-service.ts:5` imports user-moderation, and devices.ts imports user-service, so a
  static import would make a cycle.

**Files:**
- (No change to `src/services/web-push/devices.ts`: Task 1 exports `isValidEndpoint`, the one endpoint rule.)
- Modify: `app/api/auth/signout-all/route.ts` (the whole POST body, lines 35-44, and the header comment)
- Modify: `src/services/web-signin/identities.ts` (`removeSignInMethod`, lines 247-284; imports)
- Modify: `app/api/signin-methods/remove/route.ts` (the endpoint field, lines 45-50; header comment lines 3-13)
- Modify: `app/[locale]/(app)/settings/signin-methods-client.ts` (`removeMethod`, lines 126-131; imports)
- Modify: `src/lib/user-moderation.ts` (`suspendUser` lines 31-48; `banIdentifier` lines 381-449)
- Modify: `src/services/telegram-connect.ts` (`afterConnect`, lines 250-289; imports)
- Modify: `src/services/account-linking.ts` (before `tx.user.delete` at line 642; imports lines 23 and 35)
- Modify: `scripts/lib/signin-world.ts` (CASCADE and UNIQUE learn `pushDevice`, if Task 1 did not)
- Modify: `scripts/smoke-signin-guards.ts` (its fake model gains `deleteMany`)
- Test: `scripts/smoke-push-session-end.ts` (new)
- Test: `scripts/smoke-account-merge.ts` (extended: FK + #72, #73, #74)
- Test: `scripts/smoke-telegram-connect.ts` (extended: two checks)
- Test: `scripts/smoke-signin-ui.ts` (extended: U5c)

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes (Task 1, `src/services/web-push/devices.ts`):
  `deleteDevicesOf(userIds: number | number[], opts?: { keep?: string | null; tx?: Prisma.TransactionClient }): Promise<number>`,
  `afterDevicesChanged(userIds: number[]): Promise<void>` (never throws).
- Consumes (Task 5, `src/lib/web-push-client.ts`): `currentPushEndpoint(): Promise<string | null>` (never throws).
- Produces:
  - (Consumed, not produced: `isValidEndpoint(x: unknown): x is string` from Task 1's `devices.ts`, a push service
    URL of at most 1024 characters, the same rule `isValidDeviceInput` reads.)
  - `removeSignInMethod(userId: number, which: RemovableWay, keepDevice: string | null = null): Promise<'removed' | 'last-way' | 'none'>`
    (the third parameter is new and optional; existing callers are unchanged).
  - `POST /api/signin-methods/remove` body `{ which, endpoint? }`: `endpoint` absent or null = keep no device; a string
    that is not a valid endpoint, or any other type, answers `400 { ok: false, error: 'invalid' }` and removes nothing.

1c. Create `scripts/smoke-push-session-end.ts`:

```ts
// Smoke test for ending browser devices with the sessions (6c-2 spec §2.4, §3.7; plan Task 6).
//
// What it pins:
//   - sign out on all devices, removing a sign-in way, a suspension and a ban delete the account's browser devices in
//     the same write that moves sessionsValidAfter (a failing delete rolls the whole write back), and then re-sync the
//     account's event-alerts cache entry;
//   - Remove keeps the device it was pressed on (the endpoint the browser sends), and only that one of the account's;
//     a bad endpoint is refused with nothing removed; a removal that changes nothing keeps every device;
//   - a Telegram account (no device) is written as before: its cache entry is not rewritten;
//   - every writer of sessionsValidAfter in src/ and app/ deletes devices (a fence for a new writer);
//   - no log line or Sentry capture holds an endpoint.
//
// Real: the signout-all and remove routes, identities, user-moderation, devices, the reminder cache, api-auth. Faked at
// the boundaries (scripts/lib/signin-world.ts): Prisma and Redis in memory, error capture, the chats, fetch. No network,
// no DB.
//
// Run with the fake env of scripts/ci-smokes.sh: $RUN smoke-push-session-end

import fs from 'fs';
import path from 'path';
import {
  assert, baseUser, body, check, DAY, DB, everyCapture, everyLog, fakePrisma, identityRow, initDataFor, installSigninWorld, load, redisStore,
  report, request, resetWorld, ROOT, sessionCookie, show, type Row,
} from './lib/signin-world';

const ADMIN = 1;
const ADMIN_TG = 111111111;
const W = 50; // made on famcal.bot: a Google way and an email way, no chat, three devices
const P = 51; // made on famcal.bot: an email way only, one device
const T = 60; // Telegram, no device
const T_TG = 600000060;

const EP = (n: string) => `https://fcm.googleapis.com/fcm/send/smoke-${n}`;
const device = (id: string, userId: number, n: string): Row => ({
  id, userId, endpoint: EP(n), p256dh: 'B'.repeat(87), auth: 'A'.repeat(22), createdAt: new Date(Date.now() - DAY),
});
/** The ids of an account's devices now, sorted. */
const devicesOf = (userId: number): string[] => (DB.pushDevice ?? []).filter(d => d.userId === userId).map(d => d.id).sort();
const user = (id: number): Row => DB.user.find(u => u.id === id) as Row;
const read = (rel: string) => fs.readFileSync(path.join(ROOT, rel), 'utf8');

async function main() {
  await installSigninWorld();
  const { REDIS_KEYS } = await load('src/config/redis-keys.ts');
  const signoutAll = await load('app/api/auth/signout-all/route.ts');
  const removeRoute = await load('app/api/signin-methods/remove/route.ts');
  const moderation = await load('src/lib/user-moderation.ts');

  const CACHE = REDIS_KEYS.REMINDER_USERS as string;
  /** An event-alerts cache entry as the hourly rebuild writes it (reminder-cache.ts CachedReminderUser). */
  const entry = (id: number, name: string, telegramId: string): Row => ({
    id, telegramId, googleToken: '', calendarAssignments: null, defaultReminderMinutes: null, language: 'en', name,
    pickupRemindersEnabled: true, calendarSource: 'NATIVE', pairingId: null, location: 'Tel Aviv',
  });
  const cached = (): Row[] => JSON.parse(redisStore.get(CACHE) ?? '[]') as Row[];

  function seed(): void {
    resetWorld();
    DB.user = [
      baseUser({ id: ADMIN, name: 'Admin', telegramId: BigInt(ADMIN_TG), isAdmin: true }),
      baseUser({ id: W, name: 'Wren', remindersEnabled: true }),
      baseUser({ id: P, name: 'Pia', remindersEnabled: true }),
      baseUser({ id: T, name: 'Tom', telegramId: BigInt(T_TG), remindersEnabled: true }),
    ];
    DB.signInIdentity = [
      identityRow({ userId: W, kind: 'google', subject: 'g-wren-0050', address: 'wren@gmail.com' }),
      identityRow({ userId: W, kind: 'email', subject: 'wren@example.com', address: 'wren@example.com' }),
      identityRow({ userId: P, kind: 'email', subject: 'pia@example.com', address: 'pia@example.com' }),
    ];
    DB.blockedIdentifier = [];
    DB.pushDevice = [device('d-w1', W, 'w1'), device('d-w2', W, 'w2'), device('d-w3', W, 'w3'), device('d-p1', P, 'p1')];
    // As if the alerts cache had admitted W (Task 9): the writer's re-sync must take it out once W has no device.
    // T's entry is a sentinel: a re-sync would rewrite its name to Tom's.
    redisStore.set(CACHE, JSON.stringify([entry(W, 'wren-entry', ''), entry(T, 'sentinel', String(T_TG))]));
  }
  const cookieOf = (userId: number) => sessionCookie(userId, 'email');
  const remove = async (userId: number, b: Row) =>
    removeRoute.POST(request(`/api/signin-methods/remove?user_id=${userId}`, { body: b, cookie: await cookieOf(userId) }));
  /** Runs `fn` with every pushDevice.deleteMany failing, as a dropped connection would. */
  const withFailingDelete = async (fn: () => Promise<unknown>): Promise<void> => {
    const real = fakePrisma.pushDevice.deleteMany;
    fakePrisma.pushDevice.deleteMany = async () => { throw Object.assign(new Error('connection reset (smoke)'), { code: 'P1001' }); };
    try {
      await fn();
    } finally {
      fakePrisma.pushDevice.deleteMany = real;
    }
  };

  await check('E1 sign out on all devices deletes every device of the account in the same write, another account\'s stay, and the account\'s alert entry is re-synced', async () => {
    seed();
    const res = await signoutAll.POST(request(`/api/auth/signout-all?user_id=${W}`, { body: {}, cookie: await cookieOf(W) }));
    assert(res.status === 200 && (await body(res)).ok === true, `status ${res.status} ${show(await body(res))}`);
    assert(user(W).sessionsValidAfter instanceof Date, 'sessionsValidAfter did not move');
    assert(show(devicesOf(W)) === '[]' && show(devicesOf(P)) === show(['d-p1']), `devices ${show(DB.pushDevice)}`);
    assert(!cached().some(e => e.id === W), `W's alert entry stayed: ${show(cached())}`);
    assert(cached().find(e => e.id === T)?.name === 'sentinel', 'another account\'s entry was rewritten');
  });

  await check('E2 a Telegram account signs out on all devices as before: sessionsValidAfter moves, no device of anyone goes, and its alert entry is not rewritten', async () => {
    seed();
    const res = await signoutAll.POST(request('/api/auth/signout-all', { body: { user_id: T_TG, initData: initDataFor(T_TG) } }));
    assert(res.status === 200, `status ${res.status}`);
    assert(user(T).sessionsValidAfter instanceof Date, 'not signed out');
    assert(DB.pushDevice.length === 4, `devices ${show(DB.pushDevice.map(d => d.id))}`);
    assert(cached().find(e => e.id === T)?.name === 'sentinel', `T's entry was rewritten: ${show(cached())}`);
  });

  await check('E3 the sign-out is one write: when the device delete fails, sessionsValidAfter stays as it was and the answer is 500', async () => {
    seed();
    let status = 0;
    await withFailingDelete(async () => {
      const res = await signoutAll.POST(request(`/api/auth/signout-all?user_id=${W}`, { body: {}, cookie: await cookieOf(W) }));
      status = res.status;
    });
    assert(status === 500, `status ${status}`);
    assert(user(W).sessionsValidAfter === null && devicesOf(W).length === 3, `written anyway: ${show(user(W).sessionsValidAfter)} ${show(devicesOf(W))}`);
  });

  await check('E4 Remove keeps the device it was pressed on (the endpoint the browser sent) and deletes the account\'s others, in the same write; the alert entry is re-synced', async () => {
    seed();
    const res = await remove(W, { which: 'google', endpoint: EP('w2') });
    const answer = await body(res);
    assert(res.status === 200 && answer.ok === true, `status ${res.status} ${show(answer)}`);
    assert(!DB.signInIdentity.some(i => i.userId === W && i.kind === 'google') && user(W).sessionsValidAfter instanceof Date, 'the way was not removed');
    assert(show(devicesOf(W)) === show(['d-w2']) && show(devicesOf(P)) === show(['d-p1']), `devices ${show(DB.pushDevice.map(d => d.id))}`);
    assert(!cached().some(e => e.id === W), `W's alert entry stayed: ${show(cached())}`);
  });

  await check('E5 Remove with no endpoint (no subscription in this browser) deletes every device of the account; naming another account\'s device keeps none of this account\'s and touches nothing of the other\'s', async () => {
    seed();
    let res = await remove(W, { which: 'google' });
    assert(res.status === 200 && devicesOf(W).length === 0 && devicesOf(P).length === 1, `no endpoint: ${res.status} ${show(DB.pushDevice.map(d => d.id))}`);
    seed();
    res = await remove(W, { which: 'email', endpoint: EP('p1') });
    assert(res.status === 200 && devicesOf(W).length === 0 && show(devicesOf(P)) === show(['d-p1']), `another account's endpoint: ${res.status} ${show(DB.pushDevice.map(d => d.id))}`);
  });

  await check('E6 Remove refuses an endpoint that is not one (not https, not a URL, too long, not a string): 400 invalid, nothing removed, no session ended, no device deleted', async () => {
    for (const endpoint of ['http://push.example/ep', 'not a url', `https://push.example/${'a'.repeat(1100)}`, 42, { url: EP('w1') }]) {
      seed();
      const res = await remove(W, { which: 'google', endpoint });
      const answer = await body(res);
      assert(res.status === 400 && answer.error === 'invalid', `${show(endpoint).slice(0, 40)}: ${res.status} ${show(answer)}`);
      assert(DB.signInIdentity.some(i => i.userId === W && i.kind === 'google') && user(W).sessionsValidAfter === null && devicesOf(W).length === 3, `${show(endpoint).slice(0, 40)}: something was written`);
    }
  });

  await check('E7 a removal that changes nothing keeps every device: the last way (409), and a calendar switch already off (none)', async () => {
    seed();
    const last = await remove(P, { which: 'email' });
    assert(last.status === 409 && show(devicesOf(P)) === show(['d-p1']), `last way: ${last.status} ${show(devicesOf(P))}`);
    user(W).googleCalendarSignIn = false;
    const none = await remove(W, { which: 'calendar-google' });
    assert(none.status === 200 && devicesOf(W).length === 3 && user(W).sessionsValidAfter === null, `none: ${none.status} ${show(devicesOf(W))}`);
  });

  await check('E8 a suspension deletes the account\'s devices in its write and re-syncs its entry; a failing delete leaves the account unsuspended; a Telegram account\'s entry is not rewritten', async () => {
    seed();
    await moderation.suspendUser(W, ADMIN, 'smoke');
    assert(user(W).suspendedAt instanceof Date && user(W).sessionsValidAfter instanceof Date, 'not suspended');
    assert(devicesOf(W).length === 0 && devicesOf(P).length === 1, `devices ${show(DB.pushDevice.map(d => d.id))}`);
    assert(!cached().some(e => e.id === W), `W's entry stayed: ${show(cached())}`);
    seed();
    let threw = false;
    await withFailingDelete(async () => {
      try { await moderation.suspendUser(W, ADMIN, 'smoke'); } catch { threw = true; }
    });
    assert(threw && user(W).suspendedAt === null && user(W).sessionsValidAfter === null && devicesOf(W).length === 3, `a failed delete: threw ${threw}, ${show(user(W).suspendedAt)}`);
    seed();
    await moderation.suspendUser(T, ADMIN, null);
    assert(cached().find(e => e.id === T)?.name === 'sentinel', `T's entry was rewritten: ${show(cached())}`);
  });

  await check('E9 a ban deletes the devices of every account it signs out, in the same write: by account, and a bare ban of an address another account holds; other accounts keep theirs', async () => {
    seed();
    await moderation.banIdentifier({ userId: W }, ADMIN, null);
    assert(user(W).sessionsValidAfter instanceof Date && devicesOf(W).length === 0 && show(devicesOf(P)) === show(['d-p1']), `by account: ${show(DB.pushDevice.map(d => d.id))}`);
    assert(!cached().some(e => e.id === W) && cached().find(e => e.id === T)?.name === 'sentinel', `cache ${show(cached())}`);
    seed();
    await moderation.banIdentifier({ email: 'pia@example.com' }, ADMIN, null);
    assert(user(P).sessionsValidAfter instanceof Date && devicesOf(P).length === 0 && devicesOf(W).length === 3, `bare ban: ${show(DB.pushDevice.map(d => d.id))}`);
  });

  await check('E10 [fence] every write of sessionsValidAfter in src/ and app/ deletes devices: the writers are signout-all, identities and user-moderation (suspend and ban), and each calls deleteDevicesOf', () => {
    const files: string[] = [];
    for (const dir of ['src', 'app']) {
      for (const rel of fs.readdirSync(path.join(ROOT, dir), { recursive: true }) as string[]) {
        if (!/\.tsx?$/.test(rel)) continue;
        const file = path.join(dir, rel);
        if (/sessionsValidAfter:\s*(?:new Date\(|now\b)/.test(read(file))) files.push(file.split(path.sep).join('/'));
      }
    }
    const want = ['app/api/auth/signout-all/route.ts', 'src/lib/user-moderation.ts', 'src/services/web-signin/identities.ts'];
    assert(show(files.sort()) === show(want), `writers of sessionsValidAfter: ${show(files.sort())}; a new one must delete the account's devices in its write and be added here`);
    for (const file of want) assert(read(file).includes('deleteDevicesOf('), `${file} does not delete devices`);
    assert((read('src/lib/user-moderation.ts').match(/deleteDevicesOf\(/g) ?? []).length >= 2, 'user-moderation: the suspension and the ban each delete devices');
  });

  await check('E11 no log line or Sentry capture of the run holds an endpoint', () => {
    const leaks = everyLog.filter(l => l.includes('fcm.googleapis.com') || l.includes('smoke-w') || l.includes('smoke-p'));
    assert(leaks.length === 0, `log leaks ${show(leaks)}`);
    assert(!everyCapture.some(c => show(c).includes('fcm.googleapis.com')), 'a capture holds an endpoint');
  });

  report();
}

main().catch(err => {
  console.error(err);
  process.exit(1);
});
```

```ts
  // ---- Browser notifications (6c-2 spec §2.4): devices end with the sessions that could see them ----

  const pushDevice = (id: string, userId: number): Row => ({
    id, userId, endpoint: `https://fcm.googleapis.com/fcm/send/smoke-${id}`, p256dh: 'B'.repeat(87), auth: 'A'.repeat(22), createdAt: days(-2),
  });
  const devicesLeft = () => table('pushDevice').map((r) => `${r.userId}:${r.id}`).sort();

  await check('#72 a W with no chat joins a T with a chat (6c-2 §2.4): W\'s devices go, and a stray device of T\'s goes too, in the merge\'s transaction; another person\'s stays; no row holds W', async () => {
    const { mergeAccounts } = await load('src/services/account-linking.ts');

    Object.assign(userRow(W), { whatsappPhone: null, messagingPlatform: 'telegram' });
    DB.pushDevice = [pushDevice('w-1', W), pushDevice('w-2', W), pushDevice('t-stray', T), pushDevice('p-1', P)];
    await mergeAccounts(W, T);
    assert(!userRow(W), 'W still exists');
    eq(devicesLeft(), [`${P}:p-1`], 'the devices left');
    eq(referencesTo(W), [], 'rows that still hold W');
  });

  await check('#73 a W with no chat joins a T with no chat: W\'s devices go with W (they cascade at its delete; its sessions end with it), and T keeps its own', async () => {

    Object.assign(userRow(T), { telegramId: null });

    await mergeAccounts(W, T);
    assert(!userRow(W), 'W still exists');
    eq(devicesLeft(), [`${T}:t-1`, `${T}:t-2`], 'the devices left');
    eq(referencesTo(W), [], 'rows that still hold W');
  });

  await check('#74 [guard] hardDeleteUser takes the person\'s browser devices with the account (they cascade); another person\'s stay', async () => {
    seed();
    DB.pushDevice = [pushDevice('t-1', T), pushDevice('p-1', P)];
    const { hardDeleteUser } = await load('src/lib/user-moderation.ts');
```

```ts
    const which: unknown = body?.which;

      console.warn(`[signin-methods] user ${userId}: refused a removal of an unknown way`);

      return NextResponse.json({ ok: false, error: 'invalid' }, { status: 400 });
```

```ts
    const keep = auth.via === 'session' && typeof endpoint === 'string' ? endpoint : null;
    const outcome = await removeSignInMethod(userId, which, keep);
```

```ts
/**
 * Remove a way. Every other browser's session ends with it, and with them their notification devices; this browser's
 * subscription goes along (6c-2 spec §2.4), so its device stays. None in this browser: the body is { which } alone.
 */
export async function removeMethod(userId: number, which: RemoveWhich): Promise<{ kind: 'ok'; methods: SignInMethodsView } | { kind: 'error'; code: RemoveError }> {
  const endpoint = await currentPushEndpoint();
  const r = await post('/api/signin-methods/remove', userId, endpoint ? { which, endpoint } : { which });
```

3f. `src/lib/user-moderation.ts`, `suspendUser`. Old (lines 31-48):

```ts
/**
 * Suspends the account and ends its web sessions (web sign-in spec §2.13): sessionsValidAfter moves to the suspension's
 * moment, so a cookie from before it stays refused after an unsuspend too (unsuspendUser leaves it).
 */
export async function suspendUser(userId: number, adminId: number, reason: string | null): Promise<void> {
  const now = new Date();
  await prisma.user.update({
    where: { id: userId },
    data: {
      suspendedAt: now,
      suspendedBy: adminId,
      suspendedReason: reason,
      sessionsValidAfter: now,
    },
  });

  await invalidateFeatureAccessCache(userId);
  console.log(`[Moderation] Admin ${adminId} suspended user ${userId}`);
}
```

New:

```ts
/**
 * Suspends the account and ends its web sessions (web sign-in spec §2.13): sessionsValidAfter moves to the suspension's
 * moment, so a cookie from before it stays refused after an unsuspend too (unsuspendUser leaves it). Its browser
 * devices are deleted in the same write (6c-2 spec §3.7); an unsuspend brings none back (the person turns them on again).
 * devices.ts is loaded here, not at the top: user-service imports this file and devices.ts imports user-service.
 */
export async function suspendUser(userId: number, adminId: number, reason: string | null): Promise<void> {
  const { afterDevicesChanged, deleteDevicesOf } = await import('../services/web-push/devices');
  const now = new Date();
  const devices = await prisma.$transaction(async (tx) => {
    await tx.user.update({
      where: { id: userId },
      data: {
        suspendedAt: now,
        suspendedBy: adminId,
        suspendedReason: reason,
        sessionsValidAfter: now,
      },
    });
    return deleteDevicesOf(userId, { tx });
  });

  await invalidateFeatureAccessCache(userId);
  // An account with a chat has no device: nothing more runs for it
  if (devices > 0) await afterDevicesChanged([userId]);
  console.log(`[Moderation] Admin ${adminId} suspended user ${userId}${devices > 0 ? `; ${devices} browser device(s) deleted` : ''}`);
}
```

New:

```ts
 * - Every account that holds one of them is signed out on the web (web sign-in spec §2.13): sessionsValidAfter moves to

```


Old (lines 440-447):

```ts
  if (owners.length > 0) {
    ops.push(prisma.user.updateMany({ where: { OR: owners }, data: { sessionsValidAfter: new Date() } }).then(({ count }) => {
```

New:

```ts
  // The accounts signed out, and how many of their devices went: the alert entries to re-sync after the writes
  let signedOut: number[] = [];
  let devicesGone = 0;
  if (owners.length > 0 && devicesModule) {
    const { deleteDevicesOf } = devicesModule;
    // One write per ban: the accounts' sessions end and their browser devices go with them
    ops.push(prisma.$transaction(async (tx) => {
      const ids = (await tx.user.findMany({ where: { OR: owners }, select: { id: true } })).map(u => u.id);
      const { count } = await tx.user.updateMany({ where: { id: { in: ids } }, data: { sessionsValidAfter: new Date() } });
      const devices = ids.length > 0 ? await deleteDevicesOf(ids, { tx }) : 0;
      return { ids, count, devices };
    }).then(({ ids, count, devices }) => {
      signedOut = ids;
      devicesGone = devices;
      console.log(`[Moderation] Ban by admin ${adminId}: ${count} account(s) signed out on the web${devices > 0 ? `, ${devices} browser device(s) deleted` : ''}`);
    }));
  }

  await Promise.all(ops);
  if (devicesGone > 0 && devicesModule) await devicesModule.afterDevicesChanged(signedOut);
```

```ts
    const { invalidateFeatureAccessCache } = await import('./subscription-service');
```

```ts
  });
```

Why after the commit and not inside `attachTelegram`'s write: an interactive transaction there would change the
P2002 race (`smoke-telegram-connect.ts`, "race at the write: the unique telegramId refuses"), and it is not needed: the
moment the Telegram id is written, `canReachByPush` is false for the account (it has a chat), so no push can go to its
devices while the step runs. The step's own `reminder-cache` re-sync (line 282) already re-syncs the entry, so no
`afterDevicesChanged` call here. A failing delete is logged and captured by `step` (`purpose: 'push-devices'`), and
the connect, the welcome and the notice still run.

3i. `src/services/account-linking.ts`. Imports: after line 23 (`import { syncReminderCacheEntry } from './reminder-cache';`):

```ts
import { deleteDevicesOf } from './web-push/devices';
```

and line 35, old `import { hasWhatsApp } from '../lib/has-chat';`, new:

```ts
import { hasChat, hasWhatsApp } from '../lib/has-chat';
```

Before W goes, old (lines 638-642):

```ts
  // W goes before T takes its WhatsApp ids: they are unique. A WhatsApp W was found by the
  // id the link came from, so W's ids are the ones to take, and T is on both chats now; a BSUID-only W has
  // no phone to give. A W with no chat gives none, and T keeps its own ids and messagingPlatform.
  await tx.user.delete({ where: { id: w.id } });
```

New:

```ts
  // Browser notifications (6c-2 spec §2.4). W's devices belonged to W's sessions, which end with W, so they go with it
  // (ON DELETE CASCADE at the delete below); the person turns notifications on again, signed in to T. A T with a chat
  // gets everything there, so its own devices go too, in this transaction (an account with a chat can't add one; this
  // clears one a race left), and W's are counted with them.
  if (hasChat(t) || hasWhatsApp(w)) {
    const gone = await deleteDevicesOf([w.id, t.id], { tx });
    if (gone > 0) console.log(`[AccountLink] User ${t.id} has a chat: ${gone} browser device(s) of W ${w.id} and T deleted`);
  }

  // W goes before T takes its WhatsApp ids: they are unique. A WhatsApp W was found by the
  // id the link came from, so W's ids are the ones to take, and T is on both chats now; a BSUID-only W has
  // no phone to give. A W with no chat gives none, and T keeps its own ids and messagingPlatform.
  await tx.user.delete({ where: { id: w.id } });
```

No `afterDevicesChanged` here: `afterMerge` (line 667) already removes W's alert entry (`clearDeletedUserCaches` ->
`removeUserFromCache`) and re-syncs T's (`syncReminderCacheEntry(t)`). `web-signin/join.ts` needs no change: `runJoin`
merges through `mergeAccounts` (join.ts:125), so a Join gets this rule. The cascade at `tx.user.delete` is the
migration's FK (`PushDevice.userId ... onDelete: Cascade`, Task 1); `smoke-account-merge` #73 and #74 pin it in its FK
table.

### Task 7: One reach rule (`canReachByPush`) and every Telegram gate of reminders and alerts

Spec §2.3, §3.5. Every gate that asked "is this person on Telegram?" for reminders and alerts now asks one rule:
reached on Telegram, or (no chat at all) by a device with browser notifications on. The device count comes in the
reads already there (`PUSH_DEVICE_COUNT`, a relation count Prisma makes in the same SQL statement), never a query of
its own. Telegram and WhatsApp accounts read exactly as before: for them the count changes nothing (a WhatsApp-only
account stays unreachable, as today).

What each gate's callers get (verified with grep at d619f289):
- `getReminderSupport` (support.ts:90). Callers: `page-data.ts:202` (home hints) and `calendar-write/item-info.ts:126`
  and `calendar-write/write.ts:173` take the web request's user, which comes from `getUserById` (api-auth.ts:93), which
  now carries the count: a no-chat account with a device gets reminders (hint, editor kind, create). No edit there.
  `forward-event/handler.ts:124`, `webhook-handlers.ts:610,1138`, `voice/handler.ts:380` are chat users: unchanged.
  `native-reminders/pass.ts:465` reads its own `USER_SELECT` (pass.ts:40), which has no count until Task 8: a push
  creator stays unsupported in the pass until then. The reason `no_telegram` is renamed `no_channel`; nothing in
  `src/`, `app/` or `components/` reads the reason string (grep), two smokes do and are updated.
- `canReceiveReminderPings` (support.ts:116). Callers `page-data.ts:237` (the reminder-time row) and
  `app/api/settings/route.ts:69` (saving it) take the request's user: the row comes back for a push account.
- `isPingable` (ownership.ts:106). Callers `actions.ts:131,162` read the `reachable` select, which gains the count
  here. `pass.ts:481` reads `USER_SELECT` (no count): unchanged until Task 8 (which must also stop
  `isBlocked(u.telegramId!)` for a push recipient).
- `getActivePartnerSummary` (ownership.ts:48) gains `whatsappPhone`, `whatsappBsuid`, `pushDeviceCount`. Its gate
  readers: `resolve.ts:136` (`targetFor`, voice/typed/app create), `event-store.ts:1156` (an edit to "for Dana"/"both"),
  `item-info.ts:320` (`partnerCanRemind`, the editor's For row, used at :138 and :409), `support.ts:123`. Its other
  callers (`actions.ts:121`, `event-store.ts:2001`, `google-sync/view.ts:89`, `resolve.ts:90,615,677`, `pass.ts:452`)
  get three more fields and change nothing.
- `alertsOnFor` (item-info.ts:97), called at item-info.ts:145 with the request's user.
- `notifyDone`'s select (actions.ts:464) gains the fields Task 11 reads; its logic is unchanged here.
- The admin's Reminders switch (`FeatureAccess.tsx:45`) reads a new `remindable` flag from `GET /api/admin/user-overrides?user_id=`.
- Page-data hints: no edit in `page-data.ts`. They read `getReminderSupport(user)` and `canReceiveReminderPings(user)`
  with the session's user from `getUserById`. The `push.devices` prop is Task 3's `pushPropsFor`
  (`src/services/web-push/page-props.ts`), which counts with `countDevices` (one count, only for a no-chat browser
  session while push is on). Leave it as it is in this task: `smoke-push-routes` pins its count calls, and folding it
  into `user.pushDeviceCount` is a follow-up the controller may choose (integration.md).

1a. `scripts/lib/signin-world.ts`: the fake learns `_count` (a select or an include of `PUSH_DEVICE_COUNT`). After the
`RELATIONS` constant (line 128), add:

```ts
/** The relation counts a `_count` select or include reads (PUSH_DEVICE_COUNT): per model and relation, the table and its foreign key. */
const COUNTS: Record<string, Record<string, { table: string; fk: string }>> = {
  user: { pushDevices: { table: 'pushDevice', fk: 'userId' } },
};
function countsOf(name: string, row: Row, arg: unknown): Row {
  const wanted = isPlain(arg) && isPlain(arg.select) ? Object.keys(arg.select).filter(k => (arg.select as Row)[k]) : Object.keys(COUNTS[name] ?? {});
  return Object.fromEntries(wanted.map(k => {
    const relation = COUNTS[name]?.[k];
    if (!relation) throw new Error(`the fake Prisma does not count ${name}.${k}`);
    return [k, (DB[relation.table] ?? []).filter(r => same(r[relation.fk], row.id)).length];
  }));
}
```

In `shape` (lines 131-148), old:

```ts
  if (args.select) {
    const columns = Object.fromEntries(Object.entries(args.select).filter(([k]) => !(k in relations)));
    const out = pick(row, columns) as Row;
    for (const key of Object.keys(relations)) if (args.select[key]) out[key] = related(key, args.select[key]);
    return out;

  for (const key of Object.keys(relations)) if (args.include?.[key]) out[key] = related(key, args.include[key]);
```

New:

```ts
  if (args.select) {
    const columns = Object.fromEntries(Object.entries(args.select).filter(([k]) => !(k in relations) && k !== '_count'));
    const out = pick(row, columns) as Row;
    for (const key of Object.keys(relations)) if (args.select[key]) out[key] = related(key, args.select[key]);
    if (args.select._count) out._count = countsOf(name, row, args.select._count);
    return out;
  }
  const out = { ...row };
  for (const key of Object.keys(relations)) if (args.include?.[key]) out[key] = related(key, args.include[key]);
  if (args.include?._count) out._count = countsOf(name, row, args.include._count);
```
