1b. Create `scripts/smoke-push-reach.ts`:

```ts
// Smoke test for the one reach rule of reminders and alerts (6c-2 spec §2.3, §3.5; plan Task 7): canReachByPush and
// reachableForReminders, the device count riding in the reads already there (getUserById, the partner summary, the
// admin profile), isPingable, and a fence that no reminder gate reads a bare Telegram id any more.
//
// Real: reach.ts, user-service, types, ownership, the admin user-overrides route. Faked at the boundaries
// (scripts/lib/signin-world.ts): Prisma (with _count) and Redis in memory, error capture, the chats, fetch. No network,
// no DB. The VAPID keys are a pair made here (web-push's generateVAPIDKeys), never real ones.
//
// Run with the fake env of scripts/ci-smokes.sh: $RUN smoke-push-reach

import fs from 'fs';
import path from 'path';
import webpush from 'web-push';
import {
  assert, baseUser, body, check, DAY, DB, initDataFor, installSigninWorld, load, report, request, resetWorld, ROOT, show, type Row,
} from './lib/signin-world';

const VAPID = webpush.generateVAPIDKeys();
const pushOn = () => { process.env.VAPID_PUBLIC_KEY = VAPID.publicKey; process.env.VAPID_PRIVATE_KEY = VAPID.privateKey; };
const pushOff = () => { delete process.env.VAPID_PUBLIC_KEY; delete process.env.VAPID_PRIVATE_KEY; };

const ADMIN = 1;
const ADMIN_TG = 111111111;
const W = 50; // no chat, two devices
const V = 51; // no chat, no device
const T = 60; // Telegram
const T_TG = 600000060;
const A = 70; // WhatsApp only, with a device row (a race; WhatsApp alone reaches nobody here)

const read = (rel: string) => fs.readFileSync(path.join(ROOT, rel), 'utf8');
const device = (id: string, userId: number): Row => ({
  id, userId, endpoint: `https://fcm.googleapis.com/fcm/send/smoke-${id}`, p256dh: 'B'.repeat(87), auth: 'A'.repeat(22), createdAt: new Date(Date.now() - DAY),
});

function seed(): void {
  resetWorld();
  pushOn();
  DB.user = [
    baseUser({ id: ADMIN, name: 'Admin', telegramId: BigInt(ADMIN_TG), isAdmin: true }),
    baseUser({ id: W, name: 'Wren', pairingId: 'pair-tw' }),
    baseUser({ id: V, name: 'Vera' }),
    baseUser({ id: T, name: 'Tom', telegramId: BigInt(T_TG), pairingId: 'pair-tw' }),
    baseUser({ id: A, name: 'Ava', whatsappPhone: '+972500000070', messagingPlatform: 'whatsapp' }),
  ];
  DB.pairing = [{ id: 'pair-tw', status: 'ACTIVE', inviterUserId: T, inviteeUserId: W, acceptedAt: new Date(), unlinkedAt: null, unlinkedByUserId: null, createdAt: new Date() }];
  DB.pushDevice = [device('w-1', W), device('w-2', W), device('a-1', A)];
  const now = new Date();
  DB.subscription = [W, V, T, A].map((userId, i) => ({
    id: i + 1, userId, plan: 'FREE', status: 'TRIALING', trialStartedAt: new Date(now.getTime() - DAY), trialEndsAt: new Date(now.getTime() + 13 * DAY),
    currentPeriodStart: null, currentPeriodEnd: null, cancelAtPeriodEnd: false, compedBy: null, compedAt: null, compReason: null, telegramPaymentChargeId: null,
    createdAt: now, updatedAt: now,
  }));
  DB.usageCounter = [W, V, T, A].map((userId, i) => ({
    id: i + 1, userId, textSummariesUsed: 0, voiceSummariesUsed: 0, voiceEventsCreated: 0, remindersTriggered: 0, cycleStartDate: now, updatedAt: now,
  }));
}

async function main() {
  await installSigninWorld();
  const reach = await load('src/services/web-push/reach.ts');
  const { getUserById } = await load('src/services/user-service.ts');
  const { convertPrismaUserToConfig } = await load('src/types.ts');
  const { getActivePartnerSummary, isPingable } = await load('src/services/native-reminders/ownership.ts');
  const overridesRoute = await load('app/api/admin/user-overrides/route.ts');

  await check('R1 canReachByPush: no chat and a device, with browser notifications configured; never with Telegram or WhatsApp, with no device, or with push off; a cached entry\'s empty Telegram id is none', () => {
    seed();
    const cases: [string, Row, boolean][] = [
      ['no chat, 1 device', { telegramId: null, pushDeviceCount: 1 }, true],
      ['no chat (undefined ids), 2 devices', { telegramId: undefined, whatsappPhone: undefined, whatsappBsuid: undefined, pushDeviceCount: 2 }, true],
      ['a cached entry: telegramId \'\'', { telegramId: '', pushDeviceCount: 1 }, true],
      ['no chat, no device', { telegramId: null, pushDeviceCount: 0 }, false],
      ['Telegram and a device', { telegramId: BigInt(5), pushDeviceCount: 1 }, false],
      ['Telegram as a string', { telegramId: '5', pushDeviceCount: 1 }, false],
      ['WhatsApp phone and a device', { telegramId: null, whatsappPhone: '+972500000070', pushDeviceCount: 1 }, false],
      ['WhatsApp BSUID and a device', { telegramId: null, whatsappBsuid: 'IL.1', pushDeviceCount: 1 }, false],
    ];
    for (const [label, user, want] of cases) assert(reach.canReachByPush(user) === want, `${label}: ${!want}`);
    pushOff();
    assert(reach.canReachByPush({ telegramId: null, pushDeviceCount: 3 }) === false, 'push off: no VAPID keys');
    process.env.VAPID_PUBLIC_KEY = VAPID.publicKey;
    assert(reach.canReachByPush({ telegramId: null, pushDeviceCount: 3 }) === false, 'push off: only the public key');
    pushOn();
  });

  await check('R2 reachableForReminders: Telegram as before (any id type, \'\' is none), or a push device with no chat; WhatsApp alone and no channel are not; pushDeviceCountOf reads a row\'s _count and nothing else', () => {
    seed();
    const yes: Row[] = [{ telegramId: 5 }, { telegramId: BigInt(5) }, { telegramId: '5' }, { telegramId: 5, pushDeviceCount: 0 }, { telegramId: null, pushDeviceCount: 1 }];
    const no: Row[] = [{ telegramId: null }, { telegramId: '' }, {}, { telegramId: null, whatsappPhone: '+972500000070', pushDeviceCount: 1 }, { telegramId: null, pushDeviceCount: 0 }];
    for (const u of yes) assert(reach.reachableForReminders(u) === true, `should reach ${show(u)}`);
    for (const u of no) assert(reach.reachableForReminders(u) === false, `should not reach ${show(u)}`);
    pushOff();
    assert(reach.reachableForReminders({ telegramId: null, pushDeviceCount: 1 }) === false && reach.reachableForReminders({ telegramId: 5 }) === true, 'push off');
    pushOn();
    const counts: [unknown, number][] = [[{ _count: { pushDevices: 3 } }, 3], [{ _count: { pushDevices: 0 } }, 0], [{ _count: {} }, 0], [{}, 0], [null, 0], [undefined, 0], [{ _count: { pushDevices: -1 } }, 0]];
    for (const [row, n] of counts) assert(reach.pushDeviceCountOf(row) === n, `pushDeviceCountOf(${show(row)})`);
    assert(show(reach.PUSH_DEVICE_COUNT) === show({ _count: { select: { pushDevices: true } } }), 'PUSH_DEVICE_COUNT');
  });

  await check('R3 getUserById carries the device count in its one read (PUSH_DEVICE_COUNT), and the config holds no _count; a row read without the count leaves pushDeviceCount unset', async () => {
    seed();
    const w = await getUserById(W);
    const v = await getUserById(V);
    assert(w.pushDeviceCount === 2 && !('_count' in w), `W ${show({ count: w.pushDeviceCount, keys: Object.keys(w).filter(k => k.startsWith('_')) })}`);
    assert(v.pushDeviceCount === 0, `V ${show(v.pushDeviceCount)}`);
    const plain = convertPrismaUserToConfig(DB.user.find(u => u.id === T));
    assert(!('pushDeviceCount' in plain) && !('_count' in plain), `a row without the count: ${show(Object.keys(plain).filter(k => /count/i.test(k)))}`);
  });

  await check('R4 the partner summary carries the partner\'s WhatsApp ids and device count, with no _count', async () => {
    seed();
    const p = await getActivePartnerSummary(T, 'pair-tw');
    assert(p?.id === W && p.pushDeviceCount === 2 && p.whatsappPhone === null && p.whatsappBsuid === null && !('_count' in p), `summary ${show(p)}`);
    const q = await getActivePartnerSummary(W, 'pair-tw');
    assert(q?.id === T && q.pushDeviceCount === 0 && String(q.telegramId) === String(T_TG), `summary ${show(q)}`);
  });

  await check('R5 isPingable: Telegram as before (suspended or unreachable is not); a push device with no chat is; no device, WhatsApp alone, or push off is not', () => {
    seed();
    assert(isPingable({ telegramId: BigInt(5) }) && !isPingable({ telegramId: BigInt(5), suspendedAt: new Date() }) && !isPingable({ telegramId: BigInt(5), unreachableSince: new Date() }), 'Telegram');
    assert(isPingable({ telegramId: null, pushDeviceCount: 1 }) && !isPingable({ telegramId: null, pushDeviceCount: 1, suspendedAt: new Date() }), 'push');
    assert(!isPingable({ telegramId: null, pushDeviceCount: 0 }) && !isPingable({ telegramId: null }) && !isPingable({ telegramId: null, whatsappPhone: '+972500000070', pushDeviceCount: 1 }), 'no channel');
    pushOff();
    assert(!isPingable({ telegramId: null, pushDeviceCount: 1 }), 'push off');
    pushOn();
  });

  await check('R6 the admin profile says whom reminders can reach (remindable): Telegram, or no chat with a device; not with none, not WhatsApp alone', async () => {
    seed();
    const get = async (id: number) => {
      const res = await overridesRoute.GET(request(`/api/admin/user-overrides?user_id=${id}`, { initData: initDataFor(ADMIN_TG) }));
      const answer = await body(res);
      assert(res.status === 200, `${id}: ${res.status} ${show(answer)}`);
      return answer.user as Row;
    };
    const got = { w: (await get(W)).remindable, v: (await get(V)).remindable, t: (await get(T)).remindable, a: (await get(A)).remindable };
    assert(show(got) === show({ w: true, v: false, t: true, a: false }), `remindable ${show(got)}`);
    assert(!show(await get(W)).includes('fcm.googleapis.com'), 'the profile carries an endpoint');
  });

  await check('R7 [fence] no reminder or alert gate reads a bare Telegram id: each goes through reachableForReminders, and each read that feeds one carries PUSH_DEVICE_COUNT', () => {
    const support = read('src/services/native-reminders/support.ts');
    assert(!/function hasTelegram\(/.test(support) && (support.match(/reachableForReminders\(/g) ?? []).length >= 2 && /'no_channel'/.test(support) && !/'no_telegram'/.test(support), 'support.ts');
    const ownership = read('src/services/native-reminders/ownership.ts');
    assert(/isPingable = \(user: Pingable\): boolean =>\s*reachableForReminders\(user\)/.test(ownership) && /\.\.\.PUSH_DEVICE_COUNT/.test(ownership), 'ownership.ts');
    assert(!/partner\.telegramId === null/.test(read('src/services/native-reminders/resolve.ts')), 'resolve.ts targetFor');
    assert(!/partner\.telegramId === null/.test(read('src/services/native-calendar/event-store.ts')), 'event-store.ts');
    const info = read('src/services/calendar-write/item-info.ts');
    assert(!/user\.telegramId === null \|\|/.test(info) && !/ctx\.partner\.telegramId !== null/.test(info) && (info.match(/reachableForReminders\(/g) ?? []).length >= 2, 'item-info.ts');
    assert((read('src/services/native-reminders/actions.ts').match(/\.\.\.PUSH_DEVICE_COUNT/g) ?? []).length >= 2, 'actions.ts: the reachable read and notifyDone\'s');
    assert(/include: PUSH_DEVICE_COUNT/.test(read('src/services/user-service.ts')), 'user-service.ts getUserById');
    const access = read('app/[locale]/(app)/admin-panel/views/profile/FeatureAccess.tsx');
    assert(!/person\.telegramId !== null/.test(access) && /shown: person\.remindable/.test(access), 'FeatureAccess.tsx');
  });

  pushOff();
  report();
}

main().catch(err => {
  console.error(err);
  process.exit(1);
});
```

```ts
const pick = (row: Row | undefined, select?: Record<string, unknown>): Row | null =>
```

(`no_telegram`; a push user is refused). `$RUN smoke-calendar-editor-api`: the 6c-2 check fails (`canRemind: false`
for a partner with a device). `$RUN smoke-reminder-actions`: `a device on` completes (`completed: true`).

- [ ] **Step 3: Implement**

3a. Create `src/services/web-push/reach.ts`:

```ts
/**
 * Who FamCal's reminders and alerts can reach (6c-2 spec §2.3, §3.5): the one rule every Telegram gate of reminders and
 * alerts reads. A person is reached on Telegram, or, when the account has no chat at all (no Telegram and no WhatsApp,
 * hasChat), by a browser notification on a device where they turned them on, while browser notifications are set up
 * (both VAPID keys, pushConfigured). WhatsApp alone reaches nobody here, as before (reminders are Telegram's there).
 *
 * The device count rides in the reads that are already there: spread PUSH_DEVICE_COUNT into a select or an include
 * (Prisma counts the relation in the same SQL statement) and read it with pushDeviceCountOf. Never a query of its own.
 * No import beyond the config and has-chat, so ownership.ts and support.ts can take it without a cycle.
 */

import { hasChat } from '../../lib/has-chat';
import { pushConfigured } from './config';

/** Spread into a user select or include: `_count.pushDevices`, in the same statement. */
export const PUSH_DEVICE_COUNT = { _count: { select: { pushDevices: true } } } as const;

/** The device count of a row read with PUSH_DEVICE_COUNT; 0 for a row read without it. */
export function pushDeviceCountOf(row: { _count?: { pushDevices?: number } } | null | undefined): number {
  const n = row?._count?.pushDevices;
  return typeof n === 'number' && Number.isFinite(n) && n > 0 ? n : 0;
}

/** A Telegram id that is there: not null, not undefined, and not '' (the alerts cache writes '' for none). */
export function hasTelegramId(telegramId: bigint | number | string | null | undefined): boolean {
  return telegramId !== null && telegramId !== undefined && String(telegramId) !== '';
}

export interface PushReachUser {
  telegramId: bigint | number | string | null | undefined;
  whatsappPhone?: string | null;
  whatsappBsuid?: string | null;
  /** Devices with notifications on (pushDeviceCountOf, or UserConfig.pushDeviceCount). */
  pushDeviceCount: number;
}

/** Whether a browser notification can reach this person: push configured, no chat, at least one device. */
export function canReachByPush(user: PushReachUser): boolean {
  if (user.pushDeviceCount <= 0 || !pushConfigured()) return false;
  // hasChat is the one rule for "has a chat"; a cached entry's '' Telegram id counts as none
  return !hasChat({ telegramId: hasTelegramId(user.telegramId) ? 1 : null, whatsappPhone: user.whatsappPhone, whatsappBsuid: user.whatsappBsuid });
}

/** What the reminder gates hand over: any of the reach columns, the count optional (unread reads as none). */
export interface ReachUser {
  telegramId?: bigint | number | string | null;
  whatsappPhone?: string | null;
  whatsappBsuid?: string | null;
  pushDeviceCount?: number;
}

/** Whether reminders and alerts can reach this person: on Telegram, or by push (canReachByPush). */
export function reachableForReminders(user: ReachUser): boolean {
  return hasTelegramId(user.telegramId) || canReachByPush({
    telegramId: user.telegramId,
    whatsappPhone: user.whatsappPhone,
    whatsappBsuid: user.whatsappBsuid,
    pushDeviceCount: user.pushDeviceCount ?? 0,
  });
}
```

New:

```ts
export function convertPrismaUserToConfig(user: PrismaUser & { _count?: { pushDevices?: number } }): UserConfig {
  // A row read with PUSH_DEVICE_COUNT carries its devices' count; the config holds the number, never the _count object
  const { _count, ...row } = user;
  return {
    ...row,
    ...(_count !== undefined ? { pushDeviceCount: pushDeviceCountOf({ _count }) } : {}),
```

```ts
/**
 * Get user by DB primary key (platform-agnostic)
 * Used by magic link auth for WhatsApp users
 */
export async function getUserById(id: number): Promise<UserConfig | null> {
  const user = await prisma.user.findUnique({
    where: { id }
  });
```

New:

```ts
/**
 * Get user by DB primary key (platform-agnostic). The web session's read (verifyUserAuth), so it carries the
 * account's browser device count in the same statement (PUSH_DEVICE_COUNT): the reminder gates of a web request read
 * it with no query of their own (6c-2 spec §3.5).
 */
export async function getUserById(id: number): Promise<UserConfig | null> {
  const user = await prisma.user.findUnique({
    where: { id },
    include: PUSH_DEVICE_COUNT,
  });
```

Lines 77-94, old:

```ts
function hasTelegram(telegramId: bigint | number | string | null | undefined): boolean {
  return telegramId !== null && telegramId !== undefined && String(telegramId) !== '';
}

/**
 * Whether this user gets reminders and, if not, the first reason why. Reminders live in
 * FamCal, which everyone has ("Google as a connection" spec §4), so a Google user gets
 * them too; the Telegram and plan gates stay. The checks run cheapest first, so a
 * WhatsApp-only user costs no Redis read. checkFeatureAccess
 * caches its answer in Redis for 24h; a caller that needs the answer more than once per
 * message keeps the result. An error in the feature check fails closed: without
 * reminders the user gets today's behaviour.
 */
export async function getReminderSupport(user: {
  id: number;
  telegramId?: bigint | number | string | null;
}, options: { quiet?: boolean } = {}): Promise<ReminderSupport> {
  if (!hasTelegram(user.telegramId)) return { supported: false, reason: 'no_telegram' };
```

New:

```ts
/**
 * Whether this user gets reminders and, if not, the first reason why. Reminders live in
 * FamCal, which everyone has ("Google as a connection" spec §4), so a Google user gets
 * them too; the reach and plan gates stay. Reach is reachableForReminders: Telegram, or no chat and a
 * device with browser notifications on (its count comes with the user's read: UserConfig.pushDeviceCount,
 * PartnerSummary.pushDeviceCount). The checks run cheapest first, so a
 * WhatsApp-only user costs no Redis read. checkFeatureAccess
 * caches its answer in Redis for 24h; a caller that needs the answer more than once per
 * message keeps the result. An error in the feature check fails closed: without
 * reminders the user gets today's behaviour.
 */
export async function getReminderSupport(user: { id: number } & ReachUser, options: { quiet?: boolean } = {}): Promise<ReminderSupport> {
  if (!reachableForReminders(user)) return { supported: false, reason: 'no_channel' };
```

Lines 109-121, old:

```ts
 * Whether this user can be pinged by FamCal reminders, so the time those pings ring at
 * (User.reminderTime) means something to them: their own plan allows reminders, or they
 * have Telegram and their active partner's plan does (the pass pings a partner-owned
 * reminder at the recipient's time, gated on the creator's plan only). The one rule for
 * showing the setting and for accepting it. An error finding the partner fails closed.
 */
export async function canReceiveReminderPings(
  user: { id: number; telegramId?: bigint | number | string | null; pairingId?: string | null },
  options: { quiet?: boolean } = {},
): Promise<boolean> {
  if ((await getReminderSupport(user, options)).supported) return true;
  if (!hasTelegram(user.telegramId) || !user.pairingId) return false;
```

New:

```ts
 * Whether this user can be pinged by FamCal reminders, so the time those pings ring at
 * (User.reminderTime) means something to them: their own plan allows reminders, or reminders can reach
 * them (reachableForReminders) and their active partner's plan does (the pass pings a partner-owned
 * reminder at the recipient's time, gated on the creator's plan only). The one rule for
 * showing the setting and for accepting it. An error finding the partner fails closed.
 */
export async function canReceiveReminderPings(
  user: { id: number; pairingId?: string | null } & ReachUser,
  options: { quiet?: boolean } = {},
): Promise<boolean> {
  if ((await getReminderSupport(user, options)).supported) return true;
  if (!reachableForReminders(user) || !user.pairingId) return false;
```

```ts
/** The columns that decide whether Telegram can reach a user (the pass also asks the block list). */
export interface Pingable {
  telegramId?: bigint | number | string | null;
  suspendedAt?: Date | null;
  unreachableSince?: Date | null;
}

/** Whether a user can be pinged and told things on Telegram now: on Telegram, not suspended, not unreachable. */
```

New:

```ts
/** The columns that decide whether reminders can reach a user (the pass also asks the block list). */
export interface Pingable {
  telegramId?: bigint | number | string | null;
  whatsappPhone?: string | null;
  whatsappBsuid?: string | null;
  /** Devices with browser notifications on (PUSH_DEVICE_COUNT, read with the row); unread is none. */
  pushDeviceCount?: number;
  suspendedAt?: Date | null;
  unreachableSince?: Date | null;
}

/**
 * Whether a user can be pinged and told things now: reminders reach them (on Telegram, or with no chat by a browser
 * device: reachableForReminders), not suspended, not unreachable on Telegram.
 */
export const isPingable = (user: Pingable): boolean => reachableForReminders(user) && !user.suspendedAt && !user.unreachableSince;
```

3f. `src/services/native-reminders/resolve.ts`. Import (with the file's imports, near line 29):
`import { reachableForReminders } from '../web-push/reach';`. Old (lines 127-137):

```ts
/**
 * Who "remind Dana…" or "remind us…" is for: the active partner (and the creator, for both),
 * only when they can be pinged (on Telegram). Otherwise the creator alone.
 */
function targetFor(
  event: ParsedEvent,
  partner: PartnerSummary | null
): { forUserId: number | null; forBoth: boolean; notice?: 'partner_not_on_telegram' } {
  if (!wantsPartner(event) || !partner) return { forUserId: null, forBoth: false };
  if (partner.telegramId === null) return { forUserId: null, forBoth: false, notice: 'partner_not_on_telegram' };
```

New:

```ts
/**
 * Who "remind Dana…" or "remind us…" is for: the active partner (and the creator, for both),
 * only when reminders can reach them (on Telegram, or with no chat by a browser device: reachableForReminders).
 * Otherwise the creator alone, with the card's notice (its key keeps its old name).
 */
function targetFor(
  event: ParsedEvent,
  partner: PartnerSummary | null
): { forUserId: number | null; forBoth: boolean; notice?: 'partner_not_on_telegram' } {
  if (!wantsPartner(event) || !partner) return { forUserId: null, forBoth: false };
  if (!reachableForReminders(partner)) return { forUserId: null, forBoth: false, notice: 'partner_not_on_telegram' };
```

```ts
 * Whether an alert can reach the user (editor upgrades spec §2): the admin switch, their own
 * alerts setting (Settings), a plan with reminders, and a Telegram chat. The same four
 * conditions the pass applies, each read through its own function. A hint for the Alert row,
 * not a gate (a stored alert waits until alerts are on), so a plan without reminders logs
 * nothing; an error in the plan check fails closed.
 */
export async function alertsOnFor(user: Pick<UserConfig, 'id' | 'telegramId' | 'remindersEnabled'>): Promise<boolean> {
  if (user.telegramId === null || !user.remindersEnabled) return false;
```

New:

```ts
 * Whether an alert can reach the user (editor upgrades spec §2): the admin switch, their own
 * alerts setting (Settings), a plan with reminders, and a way to reach them (a Telegram chat, or with no chat a
 * browser device: reachableForReminders, 6c-2). The same four
 * conditions the pass applies, each read through its own function. A hint for the Alert row,
 * not a gate (a stored alert waits until alerts are on), so a plan without reminders logs
 * nothing; an error in the plan check fails closed.
 */
export async function alertsOnFor(user: Pick<UserConfig, 'id' | 'telegramId' | 'remindersEnabled' | 'whatsappPhone' | 'whatsappBsuid' | 'pushDeviceCount'>): Promise<boolean> {
  if (!reachableForReminders(user) || !user.remindersEnabled) return false;
```

### Task 8: Reminder pings by push (`native-reminders/pass.ts`)

Spec §3.5 "Reminders", §2.2 (Reminder row), §3.6. Line numbers are at main d619f289; if a line moved, find it by the quoted text.

**Files:**
- Modify: `src/services/native-reminders/pass.ts` (imports :10-27, `USER_SELECT`/`PassUser` :40-57, `pingOccurrence` :209-272, `passForRecipient` :323-410, `runNativeReminderPass` :449-495)
- Modify: `messages/en.json`, `messages/he.json`, `messages/ru.json` (`bot.push.reminder`, `bot.push.partnerIsDone`)
- Test: `scripts/smoke-native-reminders.ts` (extended)

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes (Task 2): `sendWebPush(user: PushUser, input: PushSendInput, now?: Date): Promise<PushSendResult>` from `src/services/web-push/send.ts`, with `PushSendResult = { sent: number; total: number; retriable: boolean }`.
- Consumes (Task 7): `PUSH_DEVICE_COUNT`, `pushDeviceCountOf(row)`, `canReachByPush(user: PushReachUser)` from `src/services/web-push/reach.ts`. Also Task 7's `isPingable` (`ownership.ts:106`) and `getReminderSupport` (`support.ts:90`) reading the fields this task adds to the pass's user (`whatsappPhone`, `whatsappBsuid`, `pushDeviceCount`): `isPingable` must count a no-chat user with `pushDeviceCount > 0` (and push on) as pingable, so a push partner makes a reminder "for both of you". The smoke's check 2 proves that dependency.
- Consumes (existing): `appPageUrl(locale, 'dashboard', userId)` (`src/lib/web-session.ts:74`), `getBotMessage` (`src/lib/bot-messages.ts:75`), `hasChat` (`src/lib/has-chat.ts:8`), `normalizeLocale` (`src/utils/locale.ts:12`).
- Produces: copy keys `bot.push.reminder`, `bot.push.partnerIsDone` (`{name}`), in en/he/ru. No new exports.

Behaviour (what the code below does):
- A recipient with a Telegram id: exactly as today.
- A recipient with no chat at all (`hasChat` false) and `canReachByPush` true: the ping is `sendWebPush` with `kind: 'reminder'`, `title` the occurrence's title, `body` the line Telegram adds without its emoji ("for both of you" / "from: <creator>", then "<partner> is done" on its own line), else `bot.push.reminder`; `url` the home in the recipient's language; `tag: nr-<stateId>`; `lang` the recipient's. `pingChatId`/`pingMessageId` stay null.
- A recipient with no chat, not suspended or unreachable, and no device (or push off): every eligible occurrence is claimed without a send, as the pause does at `pass.ts:390-394`, with the line `[native reminder ping] skipped user <id>: no chat and no device`.
- A push no device took: when `retriable` (a device answered 429/5xx/no answer) and `claim.pingAttempts < MAX_PING_ATTEMPTS`, `releaseClaim` and `'retry'` (the next tick tries again); otherwise the claim is kept and `'failed'` (captured once as `native-reminders-send` when it was retriable and the tries ran out; a non-retriable one was already reported by the sender: 400/401/403 to Sentry, 404/410 deleted the device).
- A WhatsApp-only recipient (no Telegram, a WhatsApp id): exactly as today (silent skip, not claimed).
- The block list is by Telegram id: a recipient with no Telegram id is not looked up there (a ban suspends the account, which the pass already reads).

1b. After the fake Telegram block (after `const networkError = ...;`, line 327), add:

```ts
// ---- Fake web push (src/services/web-push/send.ts) ----

type Pushed = { at: number; userId: number; kind: string; title: string; body: string; url: string; tag: string; lang: string; taken: boolean };
/** Every sendWebPush call, taken by a device or not. */
let pushes: Pushed[] = [];
/** What the next sendWebPush calls answer, in order; after them every call reaches its one device. */
let pushAnswers: { sent: number; total: number; retriable: boolean }[] = [];
const fakeSendWebPush = async (user: { id: number }, input: Omit<Pushed, 'at' | 'userId' | 'taken'>) => {
  const answer = pushAnswers.shift() ?? { sent: 1, total: 1, retriable: false };
  pushes.push({ at: clock, userId: user.id, ...input, taken: answer.sent > 0 });
  return answer;
};
```

1f. The checks: insert this block just before `  let failed = 0;` at the end of `main()` (line ~1923), so `both`, `each`, `markDone`, `pauseRaziel`, `SAT`, `copy` and `fromLine` are in scope:

```ts
  // ---- Browser notifications (web push spec §3.5): a recipient with no chat ----

  /** User `id` with no chat and `devices` devices with notifications on (the pass reads the count as _count.pushDevices). */
  const webOnly = (id: number, devices: number) => Object.assign(db.users.find((u) => u.id === id)!, { telegramId: null, _count: { pushDevices: devices } });
  const pushCopy = { en: en.bot.push, he: he.bot.push };
  const pushView = (p: Pushed) => ({ userId: p.userId, kind: p.kind, title: p.title, body: p.body, tag: p.tag, lang: p.lang });

  await check('push: a reminder for an account with no chat and a device on is a notification: its title, "Reminder", the home in their language, tagged with the occurrence; no Telegram message, no chat or message id on the ping row', async () => {
    reset();
    webOnly(1, 1);
    const r = reminder({ startsAt: today('10:00') });
    const result = await pass(today('10:00'));
    assertSame(result, { sent: 1, skipped: 0, failed: 0, nextDue: null });
    assert(sent.length === 0, `Telegram: ${sentLines()}`);
    const row = stateOf(r.id, today('10:00'))!;
    assertSame(pushes.map(pushView), [{ userId: 1, kind: 'reminder', title: 'Call the plumber', body: pushCopy.he.reminder, tag: `nr-${row.id}`, lang: 'he' }]);
    assert(pushes[0].url.endsWith('/he/dashboard?user_id=1'), `url ${pushes[0].url}`);
    assertTime(row.lastPingedAt as Date, today('10:00'), 'lastPingedAt');
    assert(row.pingChatId === null && row.pingMessageId === null && row.pingAttempts === 1, show(row));
    assert(tracked.some((t) => t.userId === 1 && t.action === 'native_reminder_pinged' && t.meta.platform === 'push'), show(tracked));
    await pass(today('10:01'));
    assert(pushes.length === 1, `pushed again: ${show(pushes.map(pushView))}`);
  });

  await check('push: the body is the line Telegram adds: "for both of you" to a partner on push (who counts as reachable), "from: <creator>" on one set for them, "<partner> is done" where each marks their own', async () => {
    reset();
    webOnly(2, 1);
    both({ startsAt: today('10:00') });
    await pass(today('10:00'));
    assert(sent.length === 1 && sent[0].chatId === '1001' && sent[0].text === `⏰ <b>Call the plumber</b>\n${copy.he.ping.forBoth}`, `his Telegram ping: ${sentLines()}`);
    assertSame(pushes.map((p) => [p.userId, p.body, p.lang]), [[2, copy.en.ping.forBoth, 'en']]);

    reset();
    webOnly(2, 1);
    reminder({ startsAt: today('10:00'), forUserId: 2 });
    await pass(today('10:00'));
    assertSame(pushes.map((p) => [p.userId, p.body]), [[2, fromLine('en', 'רזיאל')]]);

    reset();
    webOnly(2, 1);
    const r = each({ startsAt: today('10:00') });
    markDone(r.id, today('10:00'), 1, today('09:58')); // Raziel is done before the time
    await pass(today('10:00'));
    assertSame(pushes.map((p) => [p.userId, p.body]), [[2, `${copy.en.ping.forBoth}\n${pushCopy.en.partnerIsDone.replace('{name}', 'רזיאל')}`]]);
    assert(pushes.every((p) => !/[☀-➿\u{1F300}-\u{1FAFF}]/u.test(p.body)), `an emoji in a body: ${show(pushes.map(pushView))}`);
  });

  await check('push: no chat and no device: each due occurrence is claimed without a send and logged once; a device turned on later that day does not fire the morning\'s date-only reminder', async () => {
    reset();
    webOnly(1, 0);
    const r = dateOnly('2026-10-15');
    const result = await pass(today('09:00'));
    assert(sent.length === 0 && pushes.length === 0 && result.sent === 0 && result.skipped === 1, `${sentLines()} ${show(result)}`);
    assertSame(skipLines, ['[native reminder ping] skipped user 1: no chat and no device']);
    const row = stateOf(r.id, startOfLocalDay('2026-10-15', IL))!;
    assert(row.doneAt === null && row.pingMessageId === null, `open, with no message: ${show(row)}`);
    assertTime(row.lastPingedAt as Date, today('09:00'), 'claimed at the pass');
    webOnly(1, 1);
    await pass(today('15:00'));
    assert(pushes.length === 0 && skipLines.length === 1, `fired late: ${show(pushes.map(pushView))} ${show(skipLines)}`);
  });

  await check('push: an account on WhatsApp alone is left as before: no notification, no claim, no line', async () => {
    reset();
    Object.assign(db.users[0], { telegramId: null, whatsappPhone: '+972500000001', _count: { pushDevices: 1 } });
    reminder({ startsAt: today('10:00') });
    const result = await pass(today('10:00'));
    assert(pushes.length === 0 && sent.length === 0 && skipLines.length === 0 && db.pings.length === 0, `${show(pushes.map(pushView))} ${show(skipLines)} ${show(db.pings)}`);
    assert(result.skipped === 1, show(result));
  });

  await check('push: no device took it: given back for the next tick only when a device answered 429 or 5xx, three tries in all (then captured); a refusal keeps the claim', async () => {
    reset();
    webOnly(1, 1);
    const r = reminder({ startsAt: today('10:00') });
    pushAnswers = [{ sent: 0, total: 1, retriable: true }];
    const first = await pass(today('10:00'));
    assertSame([first.sent, first.failed], [0, 1]);
    assertTime(first.nextDue, today('10:01'), 'retried at the next tick');
    assert(stateOf(r.id, today('10:00'))!.lastPingedAt === null, 'the claim was given back');
    const second = await pass(today('10:01'));
    assert(second.sent === 1 && pushes.length === 2 && pushes[1].taken, show(pushes.map(pushView)));

    reset();
    webOnly(1, 1);
    reminder({ startsAt: today('10:00') });
    pushAnswers = [1, 2, 3].map(() => ({ sent: 0, total: 1, retriable: true }));
    for (const minute of ['10:00', '10:01', '10:02', '10:03']) await pass(today(minute));
    assert(pushes.length === 3 && !pushes.some((p) => p.taken), `tries: ${pushes.length}`);
    assert(captured.some((c) => c.context === 'native-reminders-send'), show(captured));

    reset();
    webOnly(1, 1);
    const refused = reminder({ startsAt: today('10:00') });
    pushAnswers = [{ sent: 0, total: 2, retriable: false }];
    const once = await pass(today('10:00'));
    await pass(today('10:01'));
    assert(once.failed === 1 && pushes.length === 1, `tried again: ${pushes.length}`);
    assertTime(stateOf(refused.id, today('10:00'))!.lastPingedAt as Date, today('10:00'), 'the claim stays');
    assert(!captured.some((c) => c.context === 'native-reminders-send'), `a refusal is the sender's to report: ${show(captured)}`);
  });

  await check('push: in Shabbat a push recipient is claimed without a send, as a chat one is', async () => {
    reset();
    pauseRaziel();
    webOnly(1, 1);
    const r = reminder({ startsAt: at(`${SAT} 10:00`) });
    await pass(at(`${SAT} 10:00`));
    assert(pushes.length === 0 && skipLines.length === 0, show(pushes.map(pushView)));
    assertSame(pauseLines, ['[pause] skipped native reminder ping for 1']);
    assertTime(stateOf(r.id, at(`${SAT} 10:00`))!.lastPingedAt as Date, at(`${SAT} 10:00`), 'claimed');
  });

  await check('push: an account on Telegram is pinged on Telegram even with a device row (Connect Telegram deletes them; the chat wins)', async () => {
    reset();
    Object.assign(db.users[0], { _count: { pushDevices: 1 } });
    reminder({ startsAt: today('10:00') });
    await pass(today('10:00'));
    assert(sent.length === 1 && sent[0].chatId === '1001' && pushes.length === 0, `${sentLines()} ${show(pushes.map(pushView))}`);
  });
```

3e. Add `pushPing` right after `pingOccurrence` (before the `settleLateOwnDone` doc comment, line ~274):

```ts
/**
 * The ping of a recipient with no chat, as a notification on each of their devices (web push spec §3.5): the reminder's
 * title, and as its text the line Telegram adds under it (for both of you, or from the creator; then the partner who is
 * done), else "Reminder". No buttons and no message to edit later, so pingChatId and pingMessageId stay null; the tag
 * nr-<stateId> makes a newer ping of the same occurrence (after a snooze) replace this one on the device. A push no
 * device took is a passing failure only when a device answered 429 or 5xx (or not at all): the claim is given back for a
 * later pass, up to MAX_PING_ATTEMPTS, as a chat's 5xx is. Anything else keeps the claim: the sender has already deleted
 * a gone device or reported a refusal. No line here holds the title.
 */
async function pushPing(
  series: NativeEvent,
  candidate: Candidate,
  claim: Claim,
  creator: PassUser,
  recipient: PassUser,
  forBoth: boolean,
  doneBy: PassUser | undefined
): Promise<SendOutcome> {
  const lang = normalizeLocale(recipient.language);
  const lines = [
    forBoth
      ? await getBotMessage(lang, 'nativeReminders.ping.forBoth')
      : creator.id !== recipient.id
        ? await getBotMessage(lang, 'nativeReminders.ping.from', { name: creator.name })
        : null,
    doneBy ? await getBotMessage(lang, 'push.partnerIsDone', { name: doneBy.name }) : null,
  ].filter((line): line is string => !!line);
  const result = await sendWebPush(recipient, {
    kind: 'reminder',
    title: candidate.occ.title,
    body: lines.length > 0 ? lines.join('\n') : await getBotMessage(lang, 'push.reminder'),
    url: appPageUrl(lang, 'dashboard', recipient.id),
    tag: `nr-${claim.stateId}`,
    lang,
  });
  if (result.sent === 0) {
    if (result.retriable && claim.pingAttempts < MAX_PING_ATTEMPTS) {
      console.warn(`[NativeReminders] Push ping to user ${recipient.id} for series ${series.id}, state ${claim.stateId} reached no device (attempt ${claim.pingAttempts}), retrying`);
      await releaseClaim(claim);
      return 'retry';
    }
    console.error(`[NativeReminders] Push ping to user ${recipient.id} for series ${series.id}, state ${claim.stateId} reached none of ${result.total} devices (attempt ${claim.pingAttempts}); not retried`);
    if (result.retriable) {
      captureError(new Error('Push ping reached no device after its tries'), 'native-reminders-send', { user_id: recipient.id, series_id: series.id, state_id: claim.stateId, attempts: claim.pingAttempts }, 'warning');
    }
    return 'failed';
  }
  trackActivityAsync(recipient.id, 'native_reminder_pinged', {
    for_partner: creator.id !== recipient.id,
    ...(forBoth ? { for_both: true } : {}),
    snoozed: candidate.snoozed,
    all_day: candidate.occ.allDay,
    platform: 'push',
  });
  return 'sent';
}
```

(`ActivityMetadata` has `platform?: string`, `src/services/analytics-service.ts:94`. `creator.name` and `doneBy.name` are `String` in the schema, never null.)

```ts
  // How this recipient hears it: their Telegram chat or, for an account with no chat at all, a notification on each of
  // their devices (canReachByPush, the one rule). A WhatsApp-only one has neither, as before.
  const telegramId = recipient.telegramId;

  const open = !recipient.suspendedAt && !recipient.unreachableSince;
  if (open && telegramId == null && !hasChat(recipient) && !byPush) {
    // No chat and no device: claimed without a send, as a paused one is below, so turning notifications on later

  if (!reachable) {
    result.skipped += eligible.length;
    return 'idle';
  }
```

### Task 9: Event alerts by push (`reminder-cache.ts`, `handlers.ts`, `reminders.ts`)

Spec §3.5 "Event alerts", §2.2 (Event alert row), §3.6, §3.8. Line numbers are at main d619f289.

**Files:**
- Modify: `src/services/reminder-cache.ts` (`CachedReminderUser` :39-69, new `alertChannelOf`, `syncReminderCacheEntry` :156-188)
- (No change to `src/services/web-push/devices.ts`: Task 3's `countDevices(userId)` is the count this task reads.)
- Modify: `src/cron/handlers.ts` (`rebuildReminderCache` :49-102, the per-user build :244-272, the error line :281)
- Modify: `src/services/reminders.ts` (imports, `formatReminderMessage` :340-353, `sendReminder` :408-469, new `alertWhen`, `recordAlertSent`, `sendAlertByPush`)
- Test: `scripts/smoke-reminders.ts` (extended)

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes (Task 2): `sendWebPush(user, input, now?)` with `input.kind: 'alert'` and `input.until` (the TTL never passes it).
- Consumes (Task 7): `PUSH_DEVICE_COUNT`, `pushDeviceCountOf`, `canReachByPush` (`src/services/web-push/reach.ts`).
- Consumes (Task 3): `countDevices(userId: number): Promise<number>` (`src/services/web-push/devices.ts`, the only reader of the table).
- Consumes (Task 1): `afterDevicesChanged(userIds)` calls `syncReminderCacheEntry(user)` for each account; with this task that call counts the devices itself, so a device added or deleted adds or removes the account's entry.
- Produces:
  - `src/services/reminder-cache.ts`:
    ```ts
    export interface AlertAdmissionUser { remindersEnabled?: boolean | null; telegramId: bigint | number | string | null | undefined; whatsappPhone?: string | null; whatsappBsuid?: string | null; suspendedAt?: Date | null; unreachableSince?: Date | null }
    export function alertChannelOf(user: AlertAdmissionUser, pushDeviceCount: number): 'telegram' | 'push' | null;
    // CachedReminderUser gains: pushDevice?: boolean   (true only for a push entry; telegramId is '' there)
    // syncReminderCacheEntry's user gains 'whatsappPhone' | 'whatsappBsuid' in its Pick (UserConfig has both, optional)
    ```

Behaviour:
- The cache admits an account with alerts on, not suspended, not unreachable, and a Telegram id, or no chat at all and `canReachByPush` (one rule, `alertChannelOf`, read by the hourly rebuild and by every entry sync). The entry of a push account has `telegramId: ''` and `pushDevice: true`; never the devices.
- `syncReminderCacheEntry` counts the devices (one query, Task 3's `countDevices`) only for an account the cache would hold by push once it has a device; never for a chat account. A failed count leaves the entry as it was (logged and captured).
- The 5-minute pass builds the user with `telegramId: null` for a push entry (no `parseInt('')`), and `sendReminder` sends an account with no chat a notification: `title` the event's title, `body` Telegram's time line (`bot.eventAlertWhen.*`, the same function) and the place on a second line, `kind: 'alert'`, `until` the moment the alert is about (`reminder.eventTime`: the start, or the end for a pickup), `tag: alert-<eventId>-<type>`, `url` the home. It is marked sent (`markReminderSent`), tracked and counted only when a device took it. No log line holds the title or the place.
- The pause (`isPaused(user, now, 'event alert')` in `processUserReminders`, reminders.ts:518) already runs before `sendReminder`, so it holds push alerts too. The downgrade notice still needs a Telegram id (reminders.ts:487), so a push account gets none (spec §2.4).
- Neon: an idle pass makes no query for a push entry (the entry is all the pass reads; `sendWebPush` reads the devices only when an alert is due).

Notes for the implementer: the fake `user.findMany` (smoke-reminders.ts:92-96) applies only the `null` keys of `where` and ignores `OR`, so the JS admission (`alertChannelOf`) is what these checks exercise; its `select` pick returns `row._count` for the `_count` key. `REDIS_KEYS.reminder(userId, eventId, type, date)` is `src/config/redis-keys.ts:8`. A Google event's `eventId` is its raw id (`calendar-read/google-map.ts:249`), so the tag is `alert-k60-start`.

```ts
/** The fields alertChannelOf reads: a database row, a UserConfig and the hourly rebuild's select all fit. */
export interface AlertAdmissionUser {
  remindersEnabled?: boolean | null;
  telegramId: bigint | number | string | null | undefined;
  whatsappPhone?: string | null;
  whatsappBsuid?: string | null;
  suspendedAt?: Date | null;
  unreachableSince?: Date | null;
}

/**
 * How the 5-minute alert pass reaches this account, or null when the cache does not hold it: the one admission rule, read
 * by the hourly rebuild (handlers.ts rebuildReminderCache) and every entry sync. Alerts on, not suspended, not unreachable
 * (Telegram refuses that chat every 5 minutes), and a Telegram chat, or no chat at all and a device with notifications on
 * (canReachByPush, web push spec §3.5). A WhatsApp-only account is not held: event alerts go to Telegram only.
 */
export function alertChannelOf(user: AlertAdmissionUser, pushDeviceCount: number): 'telegram' | 'push' | null {
  if (!user.remindersEnabled || user.suspendedAt || user.unreachableSince) return null;
  if (user.telegramId != null) return 'telegram';
  return canReachByPush({ ...user, pushDeviceCount }) ? 'push' : null;
}

/**
```

```ts
 * Telegram chat, and no device with notifications on for an account with no chat), suspended or unreachable is not
 * (Telegram refuses an unreachable chat every 5 minutes, and a save must not put them back). `user` is the row as
 * convertPrismaUserToConfig returns it (the token already decrypted). The devices are counted here (one query) only for
 * an account the cache would hold by notification once it has one, never for a chat account; a failed count leaves the
 * entry as it was, for the hourly rebuild to put right.

export async function syncReminderCacheEntry(
  user: Pick<UserConfig, 'id' | 'telegramId' | 'whatsappPhone' | 'whatsappBsuid' | 'remindersEnabled' | 'suspendedAt' | 'unreachableSince' | 'googleRefreshToken' | 'calendarAssignments' | 'calendarSource' | 'pairingId' | 'defaultReminderMinutes' | 'language' | 'name' | 'pickupRemindersEnabled' | 'location' | 'pauseOnShabbat' | 'zmanimCommunity' | 'latitude' | 'longitude' | 'elevationM'>,

      captureError(error, 'reminder-cache', { user_id: user.id }, 'warning');
      return;
    }
  }
```

```ts
/**
 * The alert's time line, "Starting at 10:00" or "Pickup tomorrow at 08:00" (bot.eventAlertWhen, eventWhen), in the user's
 * timezone: the chat's message and the notification read the same line.
 */
async function alertWhen(reminder: DueReminder, language: string, timezone?: string, pingAt: Date = new Date()): Promise<string> {
  const tz = timezone || 'UTC';
  const timeStr = reminder.eventTime.toLocaleTimeString('en-US', {
    hour: '2-digit',
    minute: '2-digit',
    hour12: false,
    timeZone: tz,
  });
  return eventWhen(reminder.type, reminder.eventTime, pingAt, timeStr, language, tz);
}
```

```ts
/** What a delivered alert leaves, by chat or by notification: the sent mark under the key getDueReminders checks, the activity, and the plan's count. */
async function recordAlertSent(user: UserConfig, reminder: DueReminder, meta: { platform?: string } = {}): Promise<void> {
  if (reminder.event.eventId) {
    await markReminderSent(user.id, reminder.event.eventId, reminder.type, reminder.sentKeyDate);
  }
  trackActivityAsync(user.id, 'reminder_sent', {
    reminder_type: reminder.type,
    minutes_before: getReminderMinutes(reminder.event, user)?.minutes,
    ...meta,
  });
  incrementUsage(user.id, 'reminders').catch(err => {
    console.error('[Subscription] Failed to increment reminders:', err);
    captureError(err, 'reminders-increment-usage', { user_id: user.id }, 'warning');
  });
}

/**
 * An alert as a notification, for an account with no chat (the alert cache holds one only with a device on:
 * reminder-cache.ts alertChannelOf). The title is the event's, the text Telegram's time line with the place under it,
 * and it waits at the push service no longer than the moment it is about (`until`). Marked sent only when a device took
 * it, as the chat's alert is only once Telegram took it. No line here holds the title or the place.
 */
async function sendAlertByPush(user: UserConfig, reminder: DueReminder, timezone?: string): Promise<boolean> {
  try {
    const lang = normalizeLocale(user.language);
    const when = await alertWhen(reminder, lang, timezone);
    const result = await sendWebPush(user, {
      kind: 'alert',
      title: reminder.event.summary,
      body: reminder.event.location ? `${when}\n${reminder.event.location}` : when,
      url: appPageUrl(lang, 'dashboard', user.id),
      tag: `alert-${reminder.event.eventId ?? reminder.eventTime.getTime()}-${reminder.type}`,
      lang,
      until: reminder.eventTime,
    });
    if (result.sent === 0) {
      console.warn(`[Reminders] ${reminder.type} alert to user ${user.id} not delivered by push (0 of ${result.total} devices${result.retriable ? ', a passing failure' : ''})`);
      return false;
    }
    await recordAlertSent(user, reminder, { platform: 'push' });
    console.log(`[Reminders] Sent ${reminder.type} reminder to user ${user.id} by push`);
    return true;
  } catch (error) {
    console.error(`[Reminders] Error sending a push alert to user ${user.id}:`, error);
    captureError(error, 'reminders-send', { user_id: user.id });
    return false;
  }
}

/**
 * Send a reminder to a user
 */
export async function sendReminder(
  user: UserConfig,
  reminder: DueReminder,
  timezone?: string
): Promise<boolean> {
  // An account with no chat is in the alert cache only with a device on (reminder-cache.ts alertChannelOf): a notification.
  if (!hasChat(user)) return sendAlertByPush(user, reminder, timezone);
  const platform = user.messagingPlatform || 'telegram';
```

```ts
    trackActivityAsync(user.id, 'reminder_sent', {
      reminder_type: reminder.type,
      minutes_before: getReminderMinutes(reminder.event, user)?.minutes,

    incrementUsage(user.id, 'reminders').catch(err => {
      console.error('[Subscription] Failed to increment reminders:', err);
      captureError(err, 'reminders-increment-usage', { user_id: user.id }, 'warning');
```

### Task 10: Briefings by push (`telegram/summary.ts`)

**Files:**
- Create: `src/services/web-push/briefing-line.ts` (the short line: all-day first, then timed by start, up to 3 names, "and N more")
- Modify: `src/services/telegram/summary.ts` (`readSummaryEvents` pulled out of `prepareSummaryForUser`; `sendBriefingPush`; the batch's `BatchPlatform` 'push'; `processUser` :600-607 stops skipping a no-chat account that has a device)
- Modify: `src/services/user-service.ts` (`getAllUsers` reads each user's device count; `UserConfig.pushDeviceCount` and `convertPrismaUserToConfig` are Task 7's)
- Modify: `messages/en.json`, `messages/he.json`, `messages/ru.json` (`bot.push.briefingToday`, `briefingTomorrow`, `briefingMore`)
- Test: `scripts/smoke-push-briefing.ts` (new)

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes (earlier tasks): `sendWebPush(user: PushUser, input: PushSendInput, now?): Promise<{ sent; total; retriable }>` (Task 2, `web-push/send.ts`; `PushSendInput` = `{ kind, title, body, url, tag, lang, silent?, until? }`); `canReachByPush`, `PUSH_DEVICE_COUNT`, `pushDeviceCountOf` (Task 7, `web-push/reach.ts`), and Task 7's `UserConfig.pushDeviceCount?: number` (set by `convertPrismaUserToConfig` for a row read with `PUSH_DEVICE_COUNT`, unset otherwise: read it as 0). Already in `summary.ts` and unchanged: the pause check (`paused(user)` → `isPaused(user, now, 'daily summary' | 'tomorrow summary')`), the hour/day/dedup filter (`filterUsersForSummary`), `markSummarySent`.
- Produces:
  ```ts
  // src/services/web-push/briefing-line.ts
  export const BRIEFING_NAMES_SHOWN = 3;
  export const BRIEFING_NAME_MAX_CHARS = 40;
  export interface BriefingItem { name: string; time: string | null }   // time 'HH:mm' on the person's clock, null when the item has no time on this day
  export function briefingItems(events: CalendarEvent[], timezone: string): BriefingItem[];
  export async function buildBriefingBody(events: CalendarEvent[], timezone: string, language: string | null | undefined): Promise<{ body: string; items: number } | null>; // null: nothing on the day
  ```
  Nothing else is exported. The tap address is the existing `appPageUrl(language, 'dashboard', user.id)` (`src/lib/web-session.ts:74`), the one Tasks 3, 8, 9 and 11 use too.

**How it behaves (the spec's §3.5 "Briefings" with the owner's rulings):**
- Only for an account for which `deliveryPlatformFor(user)` is null (no Telegram, no WhatsApp) and `canReachByPush` is true (push configured, a device on). Every other account is byte-for-byte as before; an account with no device keeps today's line `[Summary] Skipping user <id>: no Telegram and no WhatsApp to deliver to`.
- The pause, the hour, the days and the dedup key are the ones the chat summary already uses (`paused(user)` runs first in `processUser`; the dedup mark is written by `deliverToUser`'s tail).
- The line comes from the same day read as the AI summary: `readSummaryEvents` (new, pulled out of `prepareSummaryForUser` so both read once, one way). No Gemini call, no `incrementUsage`, no voice. A carried-over open reminder (`reminder.openSince`) belongs to an earlier day and is not listed.
- The person's "text summaries" switch (`textSummaryEnabled === false`) is honoured: nothing is read or sent, one log line, marked sent as a chat's is.
- A day with nothing on it: no send, one log line `[push-briefing] skipped user <id>: nothing on today|tomorrow`, marked sent.
- Counting: `result.processed` counts a briefing only when a device took it. The dedup mark is withheld only when no device took it AND a push service asked to try again (`retriable`), as a failed chat send leaves none; devices that are all gone (404/410) are marked (nothing to retry).
- Google: a failed read is thrown out of `sendBriefingPush`, so the batch's existing Google retry (30 s later, then FamCal-only) handles it for a push account exactly as for a chat. A dead token is logged only (`reportFailure` pushes no reconnect notice; the copy-to-Google message and the home's banner tell the person). When Google's half is missing from the read the line is FamCal's items and one log line says so.
- `sendWebPush` is loaded with `import()` inside `sendBriefingPush`, so every smoke that loads `summary.ts` without pushing never loads the sender. `scripts/smoke-pause.ts` already lists `summary.ts` as `PAUSED`; `briefing-line.ts` never calls `sendWebPush(`.
- Copy: `bot.push.briefingToday|briefingTomorrow` are the title; `briefingMore` is an ICU plural rendered with next-intl's `createTranslator` (as `zman-view.ts` does), not by `getBotMessage`. Items are the events' names as they are in the calendar (user content): they are joined outside ICU, never formatted by it.

It runs the real `summary.ts`, the digest reader, the new line, bot messages, summary scheduling and the pause.

```ts
// Smoke test for the briefing as a browser notification (web push spec §3.5, plan Task 10): an account with no chat and a
// device gets the morning and evening briefing as one short line, from the day the summary already reads.
//
// Covered:
//   - the line (src/services/web-push/briefing-line.ts): all-day items first, then the timed ones by start, up to three
//     names, "and N more" in its language's plural form (en, he, ru), times on the person's own clock in 24 hours (a
//     winter and a summer day, a zone that is not the server's), a long name cut, no title in the log;
//   - the batch (sendSummaryToAll in src/services/telegram/summary.ts): a no-chat account with a device gets one push
//     (kind briefing, "Today" or "Tomorrow" in its language, the day tag, the home address), with no model call and no
//     usage counted, and is marked sent; a Telegram account in the same batch is unchanged; a no-chat account with no
//     device, or with push off, is skipped with today's line; text summaries off, an empty day and the Shabbat and Yom
//     Tov pause send nothing; a push no device took and a push service asked to retry is not marked; a Google read that
//     fails is retried once, as for a chat, and the push goes after it.
//
// The real summary.ts, the digest reader, the briefing line, bot messages, summary scheduling and the pause run. Faked:
// the reader's readRange, the clock, Redis (in memory), the database, the users, the AI (it must not be called), Telegram,
// the voice note, the quota, error capture, the admin notifier and sendWebPush (every call recorded). No network, no DB.
//
// Run under both clocks; the output must not depend on the server's timezone:
//   TZ=UTC npx tsx scripts/smoke-push-briefing.ts
//   TZ=Asia/Jerusalem npx tsx scripts/smoke-push-briefing.ts

import Module from 'node:module';
import path from 'node:path';
import { fileURLToPath } from 'node:url';
import { fromZonedTime } from 'date-fns-tz';
import type { CalendarAssignment, CalendarEvent, UserConfig } from '../src/types';
import type { RangeRead, ReadItem, ReadRangeOptions } from '../src/services/calendar-read/types';
import type { AICompletionResult } from '../src/services/ai-provider';

type Result = { name: string; ok: boolean; reason?: string };
const results: Result[] = [];

async function check(name: string, fn: () => void | Promise<void>) {
  try {
    await fn();
    results.push({ name, ok: true });
  } catch (err) {
    results.push({ name, ok: false, reason: err instanceof Error ? err.message : String(err) });
  }
}

function assert(cond: unknown, message: string): asserts cond {
  if (!cond) throw new Error(message);
}

function same(got: unknown, want: unknown, what: string) {
  const g = JSON.stringify(got);
  const w = JSON.stringify(want);
  assert(g === w, `${what}\n     got  ${g}\n     want ${w}`);
}

process.env.NEXT_PUBLIC_APP_URL = 'https://famcal.test';
// Push is configured (the keys are never used to sign here: sendWebPush is faked)
process.env.VAPID_PUBLIC_KEY = 'smoke-public-key';
process.env.VAPID_PRIVATE_KEY = 'smoke-private-key';
/** Any request that slips past the fakes is refused and counted. */
const networkAttempts: string[] = [];
globalThis.fetch = (async (input: unknown) => {
  networkAttempts.push(String(input));
  throw new Error(`network is blocked in this smoke test: ${String(input)}`);
}) as typeof fetch;

// ---- Clock: every argument-less new Date() and Date.now() reads `clock` ----

const IL = 'Asia/Jerusalem';
const il = (local: string) => fromZonedTime(local, IL).getTime();

let clock = 0;
const RealDate = Date;
class ClockDate extends RealDate {
  constructor(...args: unknown[]) {
    if (args.length === 0) super(clock);
    else super(...(args as [number, number, number, number, number, number, number]));
  }
  static now() {
    return clock;
  }
}
globalThis.Date = ClockDate as unknown as DateConstructor;

// ---- What happened ----

/** Every console line of the run so far (cleared by reset). */
let logs: string[] = [];
let marks: string[] = [];
let telegramSent: { chatId: number; text: string }[] = [];
let aiCalls = 0;
let usageCounted: string[] = [];
let captured: { context: string; message: string }[] = [];
let reads: string[] = [];
type PushCall = { userId: number; input: Record<string, unknown> };
let pushes: PushCall[] = [];
/** What the next sendWebPush answers. */
let pushResult = { sent: 1, total: 1, retriable: false };

// ---- Users ----

const cal = (calendarId: string, name: string): CalendarAssignment => ({ calendarId, labels: ['yours'], name, color: '#039BE5' });

function person(id: number, name: string, over: Record<string, unknown>): UserConfig {
  return {
    id, telegramId: null, name, englishName: name, gender: 'female', language: 'en', culture: 'default', calendarSource: 'NATIVE', googleRefreshToken: '',
    location: '', weatherEnabled: false, textSummaryEnabled: true, voiceSummaryEnabled: true, messagingPlatform: 'telegram',
    isAdmin: false, suspendedAt: null, unreachableSince: null, globalRules: [], includeLookaheadInTomorrow: false,
    preferredMorningHour: 7, preferredEveningHour: 19, dailySummaryDays: [0, 1, 2, 3, 4, 5, 6], tomorrowSummaryDays: [0, 1, 2, 3, 4, 5, 6],
    pushDeviceCount: 0,
    ...over,
  } as unknown as UserConfig;
}

/** An account made on famcal.bot with one browser on. */
const MAYA = person(31, 'Maya', { pushDeviceCount: 1 });
const MAYA_HE = person(32, 'Michal', { pushDeviceCount: 2, language: 'he' });
/** No chat and no device: nothing to send to. */
const NODEV = person(33, 'Nadav', {});
/** A Telegram account in the same batch: the summary is as it always was. */
const TAMAR = person(34, 'Tamar', { telegramId: 3400 });
/** Text summaries off. */
const OFFER = person(35, 'Omer', { pushDeviceCount: 1, textSummaryEnabled: false });
/** The Shabbat and Yom Tov pause is on for her, in Jerusalem (Sephardi): Shabbat runs from Friday 17:50 to Saturday 18:39, 2026-10-16/17. */
const PIA = person(36, 'Pia', { pushDeviceCount: 1, pauseOnShabbat: true, zmanimCommunity: 'sephardi', latitude: 31.778, longitude: 35.235, elevationM: 754 });
/** Nothing on her day. */
const EMPTY = person(37, 'Elad', { pushDeviceCount: 1 });
/** A FamCal account that connected Google: FamCal and Google in one read. */
const GAIA = person(38, 'Gaia', { pushDeviceCount: 1, googleRefreshToken: 'refresh-token', calendarAssignments: [cal('gaia@gmail.com', 'Gaia G')] });
/** A read that breaks. */
const BREAKER = person(39, 'Boaz', { pushDeviceCount: 1 });
/** A place and the weather on, but no calendar: a chat gets the weather-only message, a browser briefing has none. */
const NOCAL = person(40, 'Noy', { pushDeviceCount: 1, location: 'Tel Aviv', weatherEnabled: true });
const EVERYONE = [MAYA, MAYA_HE, NODEV, TAMAR, OFFER, PIA, EMPTY, GAIA, BREAKER, NOCAL];
const nameOfChat = (chatId: unknown) => EVERYONE.find(u => u.telegramId === Number(chatId))?.name ?? `chat ${String(chatId)}`;

let batch: UserConfig[] = [];

// ---- The reader ----

type Outcome = 'ok' | 'fail' | 'boom';
let plans = new Map<number, Outcome[]>();

function item(user: UserConfig, event: CalendarEvent): ReadItem {
  return {
    source: 'famcal', key: `famcal|${event.summary}`, event: { ...event, provider: 'native' },
    calendar: { id: event.calendarId, source: 'famcal', name: event.calendarName, color: null, mine: true, inLegend: true },
    title: event.summary, kind: 'event', seriesId: null, originalStart: null, recurring: false, rrule: null, hebrewRule: null, hebrewAnchor: null,
  };
}

const at = (day: string, time: string) => new RealDate(il(`${day} ${time}:00`)).toISOString();
function timed(user: UserConfig, day: string, summary: string, from: string, to: string): CalendarEvent {
  return { summary, start: at(day, from), end: at(day, to), calendarId: 'native:cal', calendarName: user.name };
}
function allDay(user: UserConfig, day: string, summary: string): CalendarEvent {
  const next = new RealDate(`${day}T00:00:00Z`);
  next.setUTCDate(next.getUTCDate() + 1);
  return { summary, start: day, end: next.toISOString().slice(0, 10), calendarId: 'native:cal', calendarName: user.name };
}

/** The day's events of a user; the default is two timed ones. */
const eventsOf = new Map<number, (user: UserConfig, day: string) => CalendarEvent[]>();
const defaultEvents = (user: UserConfig, day: string) => [timed(user, day, 'Pickup', '16:00', '16:30'), timed(user, day, 'Dentist', '09:00', '10:00')];

async function readRange(user: UserConfig, fromKey: string, toKey: string, opts: ReadRangeOptions): Promise<RangeRead> {
  const famcalOnly = !!opts.sources && opts.sources.length === 1 && opts.sources[0] === 'famcal';
  reads.push(`${user.name} ${fromKey}${famcalOnly ? ' famcal' : ''}`);
  const window = { from: new RealDate(il(`${fromKey} 00:00:00`)), to: new RealDate(il(`${toKey} 00:00:00`)) };
  const items = (eventsOf.get(user.id) ?? defaultEvents)(user, fromKey).map(e => item(user, e));
  const famcal = { status: 'ok' as const, ctx: { viewerId: user.id, partner: null }, calendars: [], partner: null };
  if (famcalOnly) return { window, items, famcal };
  const outcome = plans.get(user.id)?.shift() ?? 'ok';
  if (outcome === 'boom') throw new Error(`the read for ${user.name} broke`);
  if (user.googleRefreshToken) {
    const google: RangeRead['google'] = outcome === 'fail'
      ? { status: 'error', reason: 'transient', cause: new Error('rateLimitExceeded') }
      : { status: 'ok', calendars: [], goneIds: [], partial: null, stale: false };
    return { window, items, google, famcal };
  }
  return { window, items, famcal };
}

// ---- Redis, in memory: the dedup marks ----

const store = new Map<string, string>();
const fakeRedis = {
  get: async (key: string) => store.get(key) ?? null,
  set: async (key: string, value: unknown) => {
    if (key.startsWith('summary:dedup:')) {
      const [, , type, userId, date] = key.split(':');
      marks.push(`${EVERYONE.find(u => u.id === Number(userId))?.name} ${type} ${date}`);
    }
    store.set(key, String(value));
    return 'OK';
  },
  del: async (key: string) => (store.delete(key) ? 1 : 0),
};

// ---- Telegram ----

const telegram = {
  sendMessage: async (chatId: number | string, text: string) => {
    telegramSent.push({ chatId: Number(chatId), text });
    return 1;
  },
  updateMessage: async () => {},
  deleteMessage: async () => {},
  sendTypingIndicator: async () => undefined,
};

/** The AI: a push briefing must never reach it, a Telegram summary answers with the user's name. */
async function generateAICompletion(prompt: string): Promise<AICompletionResult> {
  aiCalls++;
  const named = EVERYONE.filter(u => prompt.includes(u.name));
  return { text: `Summary for ${named.length === 1 ? named[0].name : '?'}`, usage: { inputTokens: 1, outputTokens: 1 }, stopReason: 'end_turn', model: 'fake-model', durationMs: 0 };
}

// ---- Stubs: static imports go through Module._load, import() through the ES module hooks ----

const SRC = path.resolve(__dirname, '../src');
const stubs: Record<string, object> = {
  [`${SRC}/services/calendar-read/index.ts`]: {
    readRange,
    sourcesFor: (u: UserConfig) => (u.googleRefreshToken ? ['famcal', 'google'] : ['famcal']),

  },

  },
  [`${SRC}/services/ai-provider.ts`]: {
```
