```ts
    },
  },
  [`${SRC}/services/telegram/bot.ts`]: { getMessagingService: () => telegram },
  [`${SRC}/services/messaging/index.ts`]: {
    MessagingPlatform: { TELEGRAM: 'telegram', WHATSAPP: 'whatsapp' },
    MessageFormat: { HTML: 'html', MARKDOWN: 'markdown', PLAIN: 'plain' },
    getMessagingService: () => {
      throw new Error('no WhatsApp user in this smoke');
    },
  },
  [`${SRC}/services/telegram/voice.ts`]: { sendVoiceMessage: async () => {}, sendWeeklyVoiceMessage: async () => {} },
  [`${SRC}/services/telegram/commands.ts`]: { sendSetupNudgeIfNeeded: async () => false },
  [`${SRC}/services/analytics-service.ts`]: { trackActivityAsync: () => {}, addBreadcrumb: () => {} },
  [`${SRC}/services/subscription-service.ts`]: {
    checkFeatureAccess: async () => ({ allowed: true }),
    incrementUsage: async (userId: number, what: string) => {
      usageCounted.push(`${userId}:${what}`);
    },
  },
  [`${SRC}/lib/timezone.ts`]: { resolveUserTimezone: async () => IL },
  [`${SRC}/utils/redis.ts`]: { redis: fakeRedis },
  [`${SRC}/utils/prisma.ts`]: { prisma: {} },
  [`${SRC}/utils/encryption.ts`]: { safeDecrypt: (value: string) => value, readStoredToken: (value: string) => value },
  [`${SRC}/lib/error-capture.ts`]: {
    captureError: (error: unknown, context: string) => {
      captured.push({ context, message: error instanceof Error ? error.message : String(error) });
    },
    captureWarning: (message: string, context: string) => {
      captured.push({ context, message });
    },
    captureInfo: () => {},
  },
  [`${SRC}/utils/error-notifier.ts`]: { notifyAdminWarning: async () => {}, notifyAdminError: async () => {} },
  // The sender is its own task's (send.ts): here it only records what summary.ts hands it
  [`${SRC}/services/web-push/send.ts`]: {
    sendWebPush: async (user: { id: number }, input: Record<string, unknown>) => {
      pushes.push({ userId: user.id, input });
      return pushResult;
    },
  },
};

const ModuleInternals = Module as unknown as {
  _load: (request: string, parent: unknown, isMain: boolean) => unknown;
  _resolveFilename: (request: string, parent: unknown, isMain: boolean) => string;
};
// @hebcal/core is ESM-only and tsx loads src/ as CommonJS (see smoke-summary-retry.ts).
const resolveFilename = ModuleInternals._resolveFilename;
ModuleInternals._resolveFilename = function (request, parent, isMain) {
  if (request === '@hebcal/core') return path.resolve(__dirname, '../node_modules/@hebcal/core/dist/esm/index.js');
  return resolveFilename.call(this, request, parent, isMain);
};
const realLoad = ModuleInternals._load;
ModuleInternals._load = function (request, parent, isMain) {
  let resolved = request;
  try {
    resolved = ModuleInternals._resolveFilename(request, parent, isMain);
  } catch {}
  return stubs[resolved] ?? realLoad.call(this, request, parent, isMain);
};
(globalThis as { smokeStubs?: typeof stubs }).smokeStubs = stubs;
Module.registerHooks({
  load(url, context, nextLoad) {
    const file = url.startsWith('file:') ? fileURLToPath(url.split('?')[0]) : '';
    const stub = stubs[file];
    if (!stub) return nextLoad(url, context);
    const names = Object.keys(stub).map(name => `export const ${name} = fake.${name};`).join('\n');
    return { format: 'module', shortCircuit: true, source: `const fake = globalThis.smokeStubs[${JSON.stringify(file)}];\n${names}\n` };
  },
});

// ---- One scheduled run ----

/** Friday 16 October 2026, 07:00 in Israel: every user's morning hour. */
const MORNING = il('2026-10-16 07:00:00');
/** Saturday 17 October 2026, 07:00 in Israel: inside Shabbat. */
const SHABBAT_MORNING = il('2026-10-17 07:00:00');
/** Friday 16 October 2026, 19:00 in Israel: every user's evening hour. */
const EVENING = il('2026-10-16 19:00:00');

function reset(users: UserConfig[], startAt = MORNING) {
  batch = users;
  plans = new Map();
  eventsOf.clear();
  clock = startAt;
  logs = [];
  marks = [];
  telegramSent = [];
  aiCalls = 0;
  usageCounted = [];
  captured = [];
  reads = [];
  pushes = [];
  pushResult = { sent: 1, total: 1, retriable: false };
  store.clear();
}

const pushesOf = (name: string) => pushes.filter(p => EVERYONE.find(u => u.id === p.userId)?.name === name).map(p => p.input);
const readsOf = (name: string) => reads.filter(r => r.startsWith(`${name} `));
const logged = (prefix: string) => logs.filter(l => l.startsWith(prefix));

async function main() {
  const summary = await import('../src/services/telegram/summary');
  const { briefingItems, buildBriefingBody, BRIEFING_NAME_MAX_CHARS } = await import('../src/services/web-push/briefing-line');
  const sleeps: number[] = [];
  summary.__setSummaryConcurrencyForTests(1);
  summary.__setScheduledRetrySleepForTests(async (ms: number) => {
    sleeps.push(ms);
    clock += ms;
  });
  const realLog = console.log;
  for (const level of ['log', 'info', 'warn', 'error'] as const) {
    console[level] = (...args: unknown[]) => {
      logs.push(args.map(a => (typeof a === 'string' ? a : a instanceof Error ? a.message : JSON.stringify(a))).join(' '));
    };
  }

  // ---- The line ----

  const ev = (summaryText: string, start: string, over: Partial<CalendarEvent> = {}): CalendarEvent =>
    ({ summary: summaryText, start, end: start, calendarId: 'c', calendarName: 'c', ...over });

  await check('the line: all-day items first, then the timed ones by start (the input order does not matter), times 24 hours on the person\'s clock', async () => {
    const events = [
      ev('Pickup', '2026-12-01T14:00:00.000Z'),
      ev('Dentist', '2026-12-01T07:00:00.000Z'),
      ev('School trip', '2026-12-01', { end: '2026-12-02' }),
    ];
    same(briefingItems(events, IL), [{ name: 'School trip', time: null }, { name: 'Dentist', time: '09:00' }, { name: 'Pickup', time: '16:00' }], 'Jerusalem, winter');
    same(briefingItems(events, 'America/New_York').map(i => i.time), [null, '02:00', '09:00'], 'New York is 24 hours too, not 2:00 AM');
    // Israel's summer time: the same UTC instant is an hour later on the clock
    same(briefingItems([ev('Dentist', '2026-07-01T07:00:00.000Z')], IL).map(i => i.time), ['10:00'], 'Jerusalem, summer');
    same(briefingItems([ev('Dentist', '2026-10-25T22:30:00.000Z')], IL).map(i => i.time), ['00:30'], 'the night summer time ends (UTC+2 from 01:00 on the 25th)');
  });

  await check('the line: an item that started before the day or has only a date has no time; a reminder by zman has its zman; a carried-over open reminder and a nameless item are not listed', async () => {
    const events = [
      ev('Sleepover', '2026-12-01T18:00:00.000Z', { daySpan: 'continues' }),
      ev('Conference', '2026-11-30T08:00:00.000Z', { daySpan: 'spansDay' }),
      ev('Call the plumber', '2026-12-01', { eventType: 'reminder', reminder: { ownerIsViewer: true, creatorIsViewer: true } }),
      ev('Light candles', '2026-12-01', { eventType: 'reminder', reminder: { ownerIsViewer: true, creatorIsViewer: true, zmanAt: '2026-12-01T14:41:00.000Z' } }),
      ev('Old bill', '2026-11-28', { eventType: 'reminder', reminder: { ownerIsViewer: true, creatorIsViewer: true, open: true, openSince: '2026-11-28' } }),
      ev('   ', '2026-12-01T08:00:00.000Z'),
      ev('', '2026-12-01T08:30:00.000Z'),
      ev('Late', 'not a date'),
    ];
    same(briefingItems(events, IL), [
      { name: 'Sleepover', time: null }, { name: 'Conference', time: null }, { name: 'Call the plumber', time: null }, { name: 'Late', time: null },
      { name: 'Light candles', time: '16:41' },
    ], 'items');
  });

  await check('the line: a name is one line and at most 40 characters, cut on whole characters (an emoji is not split)', async () => {
    const long = briefingItems([ev('Parents\' evening\nat the\t\tschool, with a very long name indeed', '2026-12-01')], IL)[0].name;
    assert(!/\s{2}|\n|\t/.test(long) && Array.from(long).length <= BRIEFING_NAME_MAX_CHARS && Array.from(long).length >= BRIEFING_NAME_MAX_CHARS - 2 && long.endsWith('…'), `name ${JSON.stringify(long)}`);
    const emoji = briefingItems([ev('\u{1F600}'.repeat(60), '2026-12-01')], IL)[0].name;
    assert(Array.from(emoji).length === BRIEFING_NAME_MAX_CHARS && !/[\uD800-\uDFFF](?![\uDC00-\uDFFF])/.test(emoji.replace(/[\uD800-\uDBFF][\uDC00-\uDFFF]/g, '')), `emoji name ${JSON.stringify(emoji)}`);
    same(briefingItems([ev('Short', '2026-12-01')], IL)[0].name, 'Short', 'a short name is as it is');
  });

  const six = ['A', 'B', 'C', 'D', 'E', 'F'].map((n, i) => ev(n, `2026-12-01T0${i + 1}:00:00.000Z`));
  const bodyOf = async (events: CalendarEvent[], lang: string) => (await buildBriefingBody(events, 'UTC', lang))?.body;

  await check('the line: up to three names, then "and N more" in the language\'s own plural form (en, he, ru)', async () => {
    same(await bodyOf(six.slice(0, 2), 'en'), 'A 01:00, B 02:00', 'two items');
    same(await bodyOf(six.slice(0, 3), 'en'), 'A 01:00, B 02:00, C 03:00', 'exactly three: no "more"');
    same(await bodyOf(six.slice(0, 4), 'en'), 'A 01:00, B 02:00, C 03:00 and 1 more', 'en, one more');
    same(await bodyOf(six, 'en'), 'A 01:00, B 02:00, C 03:00 and 3 more', 'en, three more');
    same(await bodyOf(six.slice(0, 4), 'he'), 'A 01:00, B 02:00, C 03:00 ועוד אחד', 'he, one more');
    same(await bodyOf(six.slice(0, 5), 'he'), 'A 01:00, B 02:00, C 03:00 ועוד שניים', 'he, two more');
    same(await bodyOf(six, 'he'), 'A 01:00, B 02:00, C 03:00 ועוד 3', 'he, three more');
    same(await bodyOf(six.slice(0, 4), 'ru'), 'A 01:00, B 02:00, C 03:00 и ещё 1', 'ru, one more (a no-break space after the one-letter word)');
    same(await bodyOf(six, 'ru'), 'A 01:00, B 02:00, C 03:00 и ещё 3', 'ru, three more');
    same(await bodyOf(six.slice(0, 4), 'fr'), 'A 01:00, B 02:00, C 03:00 and 1 more', 'an unknown language reads as English');
  });

  await check('the line: an empty day has no line', async () => {
    assert((await buildBriefingBody([], IL, 'en')) === null, 'empty events');
    assert((await buildBriefingBody([ev('', '2026-12-01')], IL, 'en')) === null, 'only a nameless item');
  });

  // ---- The batch ----

  reset([MAYA, MAYA_HE, NODEV, TAMAR, OFFER]);
  const daily = await summary.sendDailySummaryToAll({ filterByHour: true });
  const dailyLogs = [...logs];

  await check('daily: an account with no chat and a device gets one briefing push (kind, title "Today", the home, the day\'s tag, its language), built from the day it reads', () => {
    same(pushesOf('Maya'), [{
      kind: 'briefing', title: 'Today', body: 'Dentist 09:00, Pickup 16:00', url: 'https://famcal.test/en/dashboard?user_id=31', tag: 'briefing-today', lang: 'en',
    }], 'Maya\'s push');
    same(pushesOf('Michal'), [{
      kind: 'briefing', title: 'היום', body: 'Dentist 09:00, Pickup 16:00', url: 'https://famcal.test/he/dashboard?user_id=32', tag: 'briefing-today', lang: 'he',
    }], 'Michal\'s push');
  });

  await check('daily: no model call and no usage for a push; the Telegram account in the same batch gets its AI summary, unchanged, and no push', () => {
    same(telegramSent.map(s => [nameOfChat(s.chatId), s.text]), [['Tamar', 'Summary for Tamar']], 'Telegram sends');
    assert(aiCalls === 1, `the model was called ${aiCalls} times (only Tamar\'s summary should)`);
    same(usageCounted, ['34:textSummaries'], 'usage counted');
    same(pushesOf('Tamar'), [], 'Tamar\'s pushes');
  });

  await check('daily: the push account is marked sent with the same dedup key as a chat; the account with no device is skipped with today\'s line and read not at all; text summaries off reads and sends nothing', () => {
    same(marks.sort(), ['Maya daily 2026-10-16', 'Michal daily 2026-10-16', 'Omer daily 2026-10-16', 'Tamar daily 2026-10-16'].sort(), 'dedup marks');
    same(readsOf('Nadav'), [], 'Nadav\'s reads');
    assert(dailyLogs.includes('[Summary] Skipping user 33: no Telegram and no WhatsApp to deliver to'), `no skip line: ${JSON.stringify(dailyLogs.filter(l => l.includes('33')))}`);
    same(pushesOf('Nadav'), [], 'Nadav\'s pushes');
    same(readsOf('Omer'), [], 'Omer\'s reads');
    same(pushesOf('Omer'), [], 'Omer\'s pushes');
    assert(dailyLogs.includes('[push-briefing] skipped user 35: text summaries are off'), `no off line: ${JSON.stringify(dailyLogs.filter(l => l.includes('push-briefing')))}`);
  });

  await check('daily: processed counts the briefings and summaries that went out (Maya, Michal, Tamar), not the skipped', () => {
    assert(daily.processed === 3, `processed ${daily.processed}`);
  });

  reset([EMPTY]);
  eventsOf.set(EMPTY.id, () => []);
  await summary.sendDailySummaryToAll({ filterByHour: true });
  await check('daily: an empty day sends nothing, says so in one line, and is marked', () => {
    same(pushes, [], 'pushes');
    same(logged('[push-briefing]'), ['[push-briefing] skipped user 37: nothing on today'], 'log lines');
    same(marks, ['Elad daily 2026-10-16'], 'marks');
  });

  reset([MAYA], EVENING);
  const tomorrow = await summary.sendTomorrowSummaryToAll({ filterByHour: true });
  await check('tomorrow: the evening briefing reads tomorrow, "Tomorrow", its own tag and dedup key', () => {
    same(pushesOf('Maya'), [{
      kind: 'briefing', title: 'Tomorrow', body: 'Dentist 09:00, Pickup 16:00', url: 'https://famcal.test/en/dashboard?user_id=31', tag: 'briefing-tomorrow', lang: 'en',
    }], 'Maya\'s push');
    same(readsOf('Maya'), ['Maya 2026-10-17'], 'the day read');
    same(marks, ['Maya tomorrow 2026-10-16'], 'marks');
    assert(tomorrow.processed === 1, `processed ${tomorrow.processed}`);
  });

  reset([MAYA], EVENING);
  eventsOf.set(MAYA.id, (u, day) => [allDay(u, day, 'Trip'), timed(u, day, 'Late', '23:30', '23:59'), timed(u, day, 'Early', '06:00', '07:00'), timed(u, day, 'Lunch', '12:00', '13:00'), timed(u, day, 'Dinner', '19:30', '21:00')]);
  await summary.sendTomorrowSummaryToAll({ filterByHour: true });
  await check('tomorrow: all-day first, then by start, three names and the rest counted, from the real day read', () => {
    same(pushesOf('Maya').map(p => p.body), ['Trip, Early 06:00, Lunch 12:00 and 2 more'], 'body');
  });

  // The pause
  reset([PIA], SHABBAT_MORNING);
  const paused = await summary.sendDailySummaryToAll({ filterByHour: true });
  await check('the pause: inside Shabbat nothing is read or sent and no dedup mark is written (the pause check that was already there)', () => {
    same(pushes, [], 'pushes');
    same(reads, [], 'reads');
    same(marks, [], 'marks');
    assert(paused.skippedPause === 1 && paused.processed === 0, `result ${JSON.stringify(paused)}`);
    assert(logs.includes('[pause] skipped daily summary for 36'), `no pause line: ${JSON.stringify(logs)}`);
  });

  // Push off
  reset([MAYA]);
  delete process.env.VAPID_PRIVATE_KEY;
  await summary.sendDailySummaryToAll({ filterByHour: true });
  process.env.VAPID_PRIVATE_KEY = 'smoke-private-key';
  await check('push off (a key not set): the account is skipped as before, with today\'s line, and nothing is read or sent', () => {
    same(pushes, [], 'pushes');
    same(reads, [], 'reads');
    same(marks, [], 'marks');
    assert(logs.includes('[Summary] Skipping user 31: no Telegram and no WhatsApp to deliver to'), `no skip line: ${JSON.stringify(logs)}`);
  });

  // No device took it
  reset([MAYA]);
  pushResult = { sent: 0, total: 1, retriable: true };
  const retried = await summary.sendDailySummaryToAll({ filterByHour: true });
  await check('a push no device took and a push service asked to retry: not counted, not marked (so a rerun in the hour can try again)', () => {
    same(marks, [], 'marks');
    assert(retried.processed === 0, `processed ${retried.processed}`);
    assert(logs.includes('[push-briefing] user 31: no device took the today briefing and a push service asked to try again; not marked sent'), `no retry line: ${JSON.stringify(logs.filter(l => l.includes('push-briefing')))}`);
  });

  reset([MAYA]);
  pushResult = { sent: 0, total: 0, retriable: false };
  const gone = await summary.sendDailySummaryToAll({ filterByHour: true });
  await check('a push whose devices were all gone (404/410 deleted them): not counted, marked (nothing to retry)', () => {
    same(marks, ['Maya daily 2026-10-16'], 'marks');
    assert(gone.processed === 0, `processed ${gone.processed}`);
  });

  // A broken read
  reset([BREAKER, MAYA]);
  plans.set(BREAKER.id, ['boom']);
  const broke = await summary.sendDailySummaryToAll({ filterByHour: true });
  await check('a read that breaks: logged and sent to Sentry, no push and no mark for that account, and the next account is still served', () => {
    same(pushesOf('Boaz'), [], 'Boaz\'s pushes');
    same(pushesOf('Maya').length, 1, 'Maya\'s pushes');
    same(marks, ['Maya daily 2026-10-16'], 'marks');
    same(captured.map(c => c.context), ['summary-batch-user'], 'Sentry');
    assert(broke.processed === 1, `processed ${broke.processed}`);
  });

  // No calendar
  reset([NOCAL]);
  await summary.sendDailySummaryToAll({ filterByHour: true });
  await check('no calendar: a browser account with a place and the weather on gets no weather-only message (that is a chat message): nothing is sent, read or marked, and the skip is logged', () => {
    same(pushes, [], 'pushes');
    same(reads, [], 'reads');
    same(marks, [], 'marks');
    same(captured, [], 'Sentry (a weather lookup would have been tried and blocked)');
    assert(logs.includes('[Summary] Skipping user 40: No calendars'), `no skip line: ${JSON.stringify(logs)}`);
  });

  // Google retry
  reset([GAIA]);
  plans.set(GAIA.id, ['fail', 'ok']);
  await summary.sendDailySummaryToAll({ filterByHour: true });
  await check('a Google read that fails once is set aside and retried, as for a chat: the push goes after the wait, once, and is marked once', () => {
    same(sleeps.length, 1, 'waits');
    same(pushesOf('Gaia').length, 1, 'Gaia\'s pushes');
    same(marks, ['Gaia daily 2026-10-16'], 'marks');
  });

  reset([GAIA]);
  sleeps.length = 0;
  plans.set(GAIA.id, ['fail', 'fail']);
  await summary.sendDailySummaryToAll({ filterByHour: true });
  await check('a Google read that fails twice: the outage run reads FamCal only, the line is FamCal\'s items and the log says Google\'s are missing', () => {
    same(readsOf('Gaia'), ['Gaia 2026-10-16', 'Gaia 2026-10-16', 'Gaia 2026-10-16 famcal'], 'reads');
    same(pushesOf('Gaia').map(p => p.body), ['Dentist 09:00, Pickup 16:00'], 'body');
    assert(logs.includes('[push-briefing] user 38: Google\'s events are missing from the today read (outage); the line has FamCal\'s items'), `no outage line: ${JSON.stringify(logs.filter(l => l.includes('push-briefing')))}`);
    same(marks, ['Gaia daily 2026-10-16'], 'marks');
  });

  await check('no title, address or token reached a log line, and nothing used the network', () => {
    // Every line of the last scenarios, plus the batch before them
    const everything = [...dailyLogs, ...logs].join('\n');
    assert(!/Dentist|Pickup|School trip|famcal\.test|smoke-public-key|smoke-private-key/.test(everything), `a log line holds content: ${everything.split('\n').filter(l => /Dentist|Pickup|famcal\.test|smoke-/.test(l)).join(' | ')}`);
    same(networkAttempts, [], 'network');
  });

  summary.__setScheduledRetrySleepForTests(null);
  console.log = realLog;

  let passed = 0;
  for (const r of results) {
    if (r.ok) passed++;
    realLog(`${r.ok ? 'OK  ' : 'FAIL'} ${r.name}${r.ok ? '' : `\n     ${r.reason}`}`);
  }
  realLog(`\n${passed}/${results.length} passed (process TZ=${process.env.TZ ?? 'unset'})`);
  process.exit(passed === results.length ? 0 : 1);
}

main().catch(err => {
  console.error(err);
  process.exit(1);
});
```

- [ ] **Step 2: Run it to see it fail** — `$RUN smoke-push-briefing`. It stops at once with `Cannot find module '../src/services/web-push/briefing-line'` (exit 1): the line, the batch's push branch and the copy do not exist yet.

- [ ] **Step 3: Implement** — in this order (each file is edited with its exact old → new text; "Old" appears exactly once in the file at the commit this plan was written against, and the "New" text replaces it).

#### 3a. Copy: `bot.push.briefingToday`, `briefingTomorrow`, `briefingMore`

`messages/*.json` are edited as plain text lines (they are not byte-identical to `JSON.stringify` output, so never rewrite them with a JSON tool). Run this from the repo root. It adds the three keys to `bot.push` in all three files as plain text lines, creates `bot.push` (before `bot`'s first key) when it does not exist, leaves a key that is already there alone, and checks the file still parses. Nothing is left behind (it is read from stdin).

```bash
python3 - <<'PY'
import re, json

KEYS = {
    'en': [
        ('briefingToday', '"Today"'),
        ('briefingTomorrow', '"Tomorrow"'),
        ('briefingMore', '"{count, plural, other {and # more}}"'),
    ],
    'he': [
        ('briefingToday', '"היום"'),
        ('briefingTomorrow', '"מחר"'),
        ('briefingMore', '"{count, plural, one {ועוד אחד} two {ועוד שניים} other {ועוד #}}"'),
    ],
    'ru': [
        ('briefingToday', '"Сегодня"'),
        ('briefingTomorrow', '"Завтра"'),
        ('briefingMore', '"{count, plural, other {и\\u00a0ещё #}}"'),
    ],
}

for lang, entries in KEYS.items():
    path = f'messages/{lang}.json'
    lines = open(path, encoding='utf8').read().split('\n')
    start = lines.index('  "bot": {')
    end = next(i for i in range(start + 1, len(lines)) if lines[i] in ('  },', '  }'))
    push = next((i for i in range(start + 1, end) if lines[i] == '    "push": {'), None)
    have = set()
    if push is not None:
        close = next(i for i in range(push + 1, end) if lines[i] in ('    },', '    }'))
        have = {m.group(1) for l in lines[push + 1:close] for m in [re.match(r'^      "(\w+)":', l)] if m}
    new = [(k, v) for k, v in entries if k not in have]
    if not new:
        continue
    if push is not None:
        lines[push + 1:push + 1] = [f'      "{k}": {v},' for k, v in new]
    else:
        block = ['    "push": {'] + [f'      "{k}": {v}' + (',' if i < len(new) - 1 else '') for i, (k, v) in enumerate(new)] + ['    },']
        first = next(i for i in range(start + 1, end) if re.match(r'^    "\w+": ', lines[i]))
        lines[first:first] = block
    open(path, 'w', encoding='utf8').write('\n'.join(lines))
    json.loads('\n'.join(lines))
print('bot.push updated in en, he, ru')
PY
npx tsx scripts/check-i18n-parity.ts
```

Expected: `bot.push updated in en, he, ru` and `i18n parity OK`. (On a repo where `bot.push` did not exist, the diff of each file is the five-line block `"push": { … },` right after `"bot": {`.) Notes on the words, so nobody "fixes" them:
- `briefingMore` is the whole phrase inside the plural, so each language owns its "and": en `and 3 more`; he `ועוד אחד` / `ועוד שניים` / `ועוד 3` (one, two, other); ru `и ещё 3` with a no-break space (` `) after the one-letter word `и`. `check-i18n-parity` reads `bot.*` as plain `{word}` arguments (src/lib/bot-messages.ts `getBotMessage`'s rule), so it does not parse or require plural forms here; the smoke pins what each language prints.
- he/ru are written for the language (not translated): the Hebrew titles are the bare words היום / מחר; the Russian ones Сегодня / Завтра.

#### 3b. `src/services/web-push/briefing-line.ts` (new)

```ts
/**
 * The line a morning or evening briefing carries as a browser notification (web push spec §2.2, §3.5): the day's items
 * as "Dentist 09:00, Pickup 16:00 and 2 more". It is built from the day the summary already reads (readSummaryEvents in
 * telegram/summary.ts), with no model call and nothing counted against the plan. All-day items come first, then the
 * timed ones by start; the first three are named and the rest are a count. Times are the person's own clock, 24 hours.
 *
 * No log line here holds a title: the callers log counts.
 */

import { createTranslator } from 'next-intl';
import type { CalendarEvent } from '../../types';
import { getBotMessages } from '../../lib/bot-messages';
import { instantToWallTime } from '../../utils/wall-time';
import { normalizeLocale } from '../../utils/locale';

/** How many items the line names; the rest are "and N more". */
export const BRIEFING_NAMES_SHOWN = 3;
/** A name longer than this is cut with an ellipsis, so three names and their times stay a line. */
export const BRIEFING_NAME_MAX_CHARS = 40;

export interface BriefingItem {
  name: string;
  /** 'HH:mm' on the person's clock; null for an item with no time on this day. */
  time: string | null;
}

/** One line of text: newlines and runs of spaces become one space, and a long name is cut on whole characters. */
function shortName(summary: string | undefined): string {
  const name = (summary ?? '').replace(/\s+/g, ' ').trim();
  const chars = Array.from(name);
  return chars.length > BRIEFING_NAME_MAX_CHARS ? `${chars.slice(0, BRIEFING_NAME_MAX_CHARS - 1).join('').trimEnd()}…` : name;
}

/**
 * The day's items in the order the line names them: those with no time on this day first (an all-day event, one that
 * started before the day, a reminder with only a date), then the timed ones by start, ties in the order given. A
 * carried-over open reminder (reminder.openSince) belongs to an earlier day and is not listed; an item with no name is not
 * listed. A reminder by zman is timed by its zman (reminder.zmanAt, which shapeDayReminders sets).
 */
export function briefingItems(events: CalendarEvent[], timezone: string): BriefingItem[] {
  const untimed: BriefingItem[] = [];
  const timed: Array<BriefingItem & { at: number }> = [];
  for (const event of events) {
    if (event.reminder?.openSince) continue;
    const name = shortName(event.summary);
    if (!name) continue;
    const startedBefore = event.daySpan === 'continues' || event.daySpan === 'spansDay';
    const instant = startedBefore ? undefined : event.start?.includes('T') ? event.start : event.reminder?.zmanAt;
    const at = instant ? new Date(instant) : null;
    if (!at || Number.isNaN(at.getTime())) {
      untimed.push({ name, time: null });
      continue;
    }
    timed.push({ name, time: instantToWallTime(at, timezone).slice(11, 16), at: at.getTime() });
  }
  timed.sort((a, b) => a.at - b.at);
  return [...untimed, ...timed.map(({ name, time }) => ({ name, time }))];
}

/**
 * The notification's text for a day, or null when nothing is on it (the caller sends nothing). `items` is how many the day
 * has, for the caller's log line.
 */
export async function buildBriefingBody(events: CalendarEvent[], timezone: string, language: string | null | undefined): Promise<{ body: string; items: number } | null> {
  const items = briefingItems(events, timezone);
  if (items.length === 0) return null;
  const named = items.slice(0, BRIEFING_NAMES_SHOWN).map((item) => (item.time ? `${item.name} ${item.time}` : item.name));
  const more = items.length - named.length;
  if (more <= 0) return { body: named.join(', '), items: items.length };
  // "and 2 more" takes its language's plural form (ICU, through next-intl's translator, as zman-view.ts does)
  const locale = normalizeLocale(language);
  const bot = await getBotMessages(locale);
  const t = createTranslator({ locale, messages: { push: bot.push }, namespace: 'push' });
  return { body: `${named.join(', ')} ${t('briefingMore', { count: more })}`, items: items.length };
}
```

```ts
export async function getAllUsers(): Promise<UserConfig[]> {
  // The device count rides along (one more column per row): the scheduled summaries ask canReachByPush
```

**`src/services/telegram/summary.ts`, edit 3 of 13** — the new `readSummaryEvents` helper, just above `prepareSummaryForUser`'s doc comment.

Old:
```ts

/**
 * Builds one user's summary. `summaryDate` is the tomorrow flag (set: tomorrow). `opts`
 * comes from the callers (calendar home spec §3.1):
```
New:
```ts

/**
 * One user's summary day, read: the day (the caller's, else the user's local date, plus one calendar day for tomorrow),
 * the day through `fetchFunction` (FamCal only in the outage run), shaped to the day, with the built-in calendar's
 * reminders added: done ones leave the day, past-due ones are marked open, and older open ones join after the day's own
 * (shaping would drop them). Anyone else's events pass through as they are. The AI summary (prepareSummaryForUser) and
 * the browser notification's line (sendBriefingPush) both read their day here, so the two never disagree about what is
 * on it. `gap` is the Google half the read lacks (unreachable on demand, or a reconnect), unset in the outage run.
 */
async function readSummaryEvents(
  user: UserConfig,
  fetchFunction: SummaryFetch,
  summaryDate: Date | undefined,
  opts: { day?: DayKey; read?: DigestRead; googleUnavailable?: boolean } | undefined,
  userTimezone: string,
  calendarIds: string[]
): Promise<{ day: DayKey; googleOut: boolean; gap: DigestEvents['googleGap']; events: CalendarEvent[]; openRemindersMore: number }> {
  const { localDate, nextDate, shapeEventsForDay } = await import('../../utils/day-events');
  const today = localDate(new Date(), userTimezone);
  const day = opts?.day ?? (summaryDate ? nextDate(today) : today);
  // The outage run: Google failed this summary twice, so the day is read from FamCal only. Only
  // a user with a Google token reads Google (sourcesFor), so no other user is affected.
  const googleOut = opts?.googleUnavailable === true && !!user.googleRefreshToken;
  const fetched = await fetchFunction(user, calendarIds, userTimezone, day, opts?.read, googleOut ? FAMCAL_ONLY : undefined);
  // The read kept FamCal without Google's half: unreachable on demand, or a reconnect
  const gap = googleOut ? undefined : fetched.googleGap;
  const dayEvents = shapeEventsForDay(fetched, day, userTimezone);
  const { events, moreCount: openRemindersMore } = await addDigestReminders(user, dayEvents, day, userTimezone);
  return { day, googleOut, gap, events, openRemindersMore };
}

/**
 * Builds one user's summary. `summaryDate` is the tomorrow flag (set: tomorrow). `opts`
 * comes from the callers (calendar home spec §3.1):
```

**`src/services/telegram/summary.ts`, edit 5 of 13** — `BatchPlatform`, `BriefingOutcome` and `sendBriefingPush`, between `sendReconnectNotice` and `sendSummaryToAll` (insert after the closing `}` of `sendReconnectNotice`).

Old:
```ts
}

/**
 * Generic function to send summary to all users
```
New:
```ts
}

/** Where a scheduled summary goes: a chat (deliveryPlatformFor), or, for an account with no chat, its browsers. */
type BatchPlatform = DeliveryPlatform | 'push';

/**
 * What one account's browser briefing came to: 'sent' (a device took it), 'empty' (nothing on the day, so nothing sent),
 * 'off' (the person turned text summaries off), 'none' (no device took it and none is worth retrying: they are gone or
 * refused), 'retry' (no device took it and a push service said to try again).
 */
type BriefingOutcome = 'sent' | 'empty' | 'off' | 'none' | 'retry';

/**
 * A no-chat account's briefing as a notification in its browsers (web push spec §3.5). The caller has already asked the
 * pause and the hour/days/dedup filters, as for a chat. It reads the day through readSummaryEvents, as the AI summary
 * does, and sends one short line built from it (buildBriefingBody): no model call, no usage counted against the plan,
 * no voice. A day with nothing on it sends nothing and says so in one log line. Google's half missing from the read is
 * logged and the line is FamCal's items; the dead-token notice is not pushed (the copy-to-Google message and the home's
 * banner tell the person). A failed read throws, for the caller's Google retry and failure handling, as for a chat.
 */
async function sendBriefingPush(
  user: UserConfig,
  fetchFunction: SummaryFetch,
  summaryDate: Date | undefined,
  runOptions: SummaryRunOptions
): Promise<BriefingOutcome> {
  const which = summaryDate ? 'tomorrow' : 'today';
  if (user.textSummaryEnabled === false) {
    console.log(`[push-briefing] skipped user ${user.id}: text summaries are off`);
    return 'off';
  }
  const { resolveUserTimezone } = await import('../../lib/timezone');
  const timezone = await resolveUserTimezone(user);
  const assignments = await getCalendarAssignmentsForUser(user);
  const { events, googleOut, gap } = await readSummaryEvents(user, fetchFunction, summaryDate, runOptions, timezone, assignments.map(a => a.calendarId));
  if (googleOut || gap) console.log(`[push-briefing] user ${user.id}: Google's events are missing from the ${which} read (${googleOut ? 'outage' : gap}); the line has FamCal's items`);

  const line = await buildBriefingBody(events, timezone, user.language);
  if (!line) {
    console.log(`[push-briefing] skipped user ${user.id}: nothing on ${which}`);
    return 'empty';
  }
  const language = normalizeLocale(user.language);
  const { sendWebPush } = await import('../web-push/send');
  const result = await sendWebPush(user, {
    kind: 'briefing',
    title: await getBotMessage(language, summaryDate ? 'push.briefingTomorrow' : 'push.briefingToday'),
    body: line.body,
    url: appPageUrl(language, 'dashboard', user.id),
    // The next briefing of the same day replaces a waiting one on the device
    tag: summaryDate ? 'briefing-tomorrow' : 'briefing-today',
    lang: language,
  });
  if (result.sent > 0) return 'sent';
  if (result.retriable) {
    console.log(`[push-briefing] user ${user.id}: no device took the ${which} briefing and a push service asked to try again; not marked sent`);
    return 'retry';
  }
  return 'none';
}

 * Generic function to send summary to all users
```

```ts
    // on the first attempt, the retry and the outage run. `markedFor` dates the dedup mark
```

- Test: `scripts/smoke-pairing-accept.ts`, `scripts/smoke-reminder-actions.ts`, `scripts/smoke-google-sync-worker.ts` (a check or two added to each; no new smoke file, because each already holds the world that code runs in)

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: `sendWebPush` (Task 2: `sendWebPush(user: PushUser, input: PushSendInput, now?) → { sent, total, retriable }`, never throws, logs its own `[push] user <id>: <kind> sent to N of M devices` line), `canReachByPush`, `PUSH_DEVICE_COUNT`, `pushDeviceCountOf` (Task 7, `web-push/reach.ts`), `appPageUrl(language, 'dashboard', userId)` (`src/lib/web-session.ts:74`, the tap address every task uses). `canReachByPush` is `pushConfigured() && !hasChat(user) && user.pushDeviceCount > 0`, so a Telegram or WhatsApp account is never pushed to, and nothing here repeats the chat test.
- Produces: nothing later tasks use.

**How it behaves (spec §2.2, §3.5):**
- **Invite accepted** (`notifyInviterOfAccept`): an inviter with no chat and a device on gets one push: kind `partner-accepted`, title `bot.push.inviteAccepted` with `{name}` (or `inviteAcceptedNoName` when the acceptor has no name), body `inviteAcceptedBody`, the home (`appPageUrl(lang, 'dashboard', inviterId)`), tag `partner-accepted`. Not paused (it answers the partner's action; `pair-handler.ts` stays `NOT_PAUSED` in `scripts/smoke-pause.ts`, so the user handed to `sendWebPush` is just `{ id }`). With no device the line `[pair] inviter <id> has no chat; accept notice not sent` is unchanged. The acceptor's name goes in as it is (plain text, not HTML-escaped: this is not Telegram HTML).
- **Partner's Done** (`notifyDone`): pushed only to a person with no chat at all who has a device on and is not suspended (`canReachByPush` holds the no-chat test: a person with a WhatsApp phone or BSUID and no Telegram id is not pushed to): kind `partner-done`, **silent** (as the Telegram note is), title `bot.push.doneBy` with `{name}` = who did it, body = the reminder's title, the home, tag `nr-done-<seriesId>`. A WhatsApp-only person, a suspended one, one with no device and any person when push is off stay unsent with today's line `[done note] skipped user <id>: no Telegram`. Not paused (`actions.ts` stays `NOT_PAUSED`).
- **Google reconnect** (`sendReconnect`): the no-chat branch pushes when a device is on: kind `reconnect`, title `bot.push.reconnectTitle` (the Telegram title without its `<b>`), body the first sentence of the same message the chat gets (`messageToken` or `messageScopes`), url **the page the Telegram button opens** (`/refresh-token?user_id=<id>` for a dead token, `/<lang>/calendars?user_id=<id>` for a missing permission; worked out once and shared by both channels), tag `google-reconnect`. `outage()` stamps `googleSyncNotifiedAt` only when `sendReconnect` returns true, and this returns true only when `sent > 0`: so a push no device took leaves the outage un-stamped (CLAUDE.md: a send skipped is never marked sent), and a person who connects a chat later is still told. The pause check for this send is already in `outage()` (`isPaused(…, 'Google reconnect message')`); nothing here asks it again. The user handed to `sendWebPush` carries the pause fields (`{ ...user, googleRefreshToken: tokenOf(user.googleRefreshToken) }`, as the `isPaused` call above it builds it), because `reconnect` is in `PAUSED_KINDS`: its lifetime ends before the person's next Shabbat window. The partner notices pass only `{ id }`: they are not in `PAUSED_KINDS`.
- The account's device count rides in the worker's existing user select (`USER_SELECT` gets `...PUSH_DEVICE_COUNT`), and in `notifyInviterOfAccept`'s and `notifyDone`'s selects: no extra query anywhere.
- `sendWebPush` is loaded with `import()` in each of the three places, so the many smokes that load these modules without pushing never load the sender.

- [ ] **Step 0: Preconditions (read, change nothing)**
  - Tasks 1-10 landed: `src/services/web-push/{config,reach,send}.ts` exist.
  - **Task 7 already changed `actions.ts`** (the edits of 3c start from that state): it imports `{ PUSH_DEVICE_COUNT, pushDeviceCountOf }` from `'../web-push/reach'` (3c adds `canReachByPush` to that same import) and `notifyDone`'s select already holds `id, language, telegramId, whatsappPhone, whatsappBsuid, suspendedAt, unreachableSince` and `...PUSH_DEVICE_COUNT`. If the select is not in that form, make it so, each key once. Its logic (`if (user && !user.telegramId)`) is unchanged by Task 7.
  - **Task 7 already set the VAPID keys in `scripts/smoke-reminder-actions.ts`** (`webpush.generateVAPIDKeys()` after `ENCRYPTION_KEY_PREVIOUS`): `grep -n VAPID scripts/smoke-reminder-actions.ts` shows them; 1b adds no keys of its own. The other two smokes set fake ones with `||=`.
  - Same for `bot.push`: earlier tasks add keys to it; 3a adds five more and leaves existing keys alone.

- [ ] **Step 1: Write the failing tests** — add the checks to the three existing smokes (each hunk is an exact old → new edit of the file as it is at the plan's commit; the fakes they use are the file's own: the pairing smoke's `seed()`/`userRow()`/`sent`, the reminder smoke's `household()`/`act()`/`botCalls`, the worker smoke's `seedCopy()`/`pass()`/`sent`/`logs`). Each gets the VAPID keys set to fake text (push counts as configured; nothing signs) and a stub of `src/services/web-push/send.ts` that records `(user, input)` and answers `{ sent: 1, total: 1, retriable: false }` (the worker smoke can change the answer per check).

New:
```ts
  });

  await check('#18 an inviter with no chat (an account made on famcal.bot) and a browser with notifications on is told by one push, in their language (kind partner-accepted, the acceptor\'s name, the home, not paused: only their id goes with it); with no browser on, or push off, the old line and nothing is sent; an inviter with a chat gets no push', async () => {
    const { notifyInviterOfAccept } = await load('src/services/telegram/pair-handler.ts');
    const { buildUrl } = await load('src/config/urls.ts');
    const problems: string[] = [];
    const want: Record<string, { title: string; body: string; noName: string }> = {
      en: { title: 'Noam accepted your invite', body: 'You\'re paired now.', noName: 'Your invite was accepted' },
      he: { title: 'ההזמנה שלכם התקבלה על ידי Noam', body: 'אתם מחוברים עכשיו.', noName: 'ההזמנה שלכם התקבלה' },
      ru: { title: 'Ваше приглашение принято: Noam', body: 'Теперь вы связаны.', noName: 'Ваше приглашение принято' },
    };
    const quiet = console.log;
    const lines: string[] = [];
    console.log = (...args: unknown[]) => { lines.push(args.map(String).join(' ')); };
    try {
      for (const lang of ['en', 'he', 'ru']) {
        for (const name of ['Noam', '']) {
          seed();
          Object.assign(userRow(1), { telegramId: null, language: lang, _count: { pushDevices: 2 } });
          await notifyInviterOfAccept(1, name);
          const w = want[lang];
          const expected = [{ user: { id: 1 }, input: { kind: 'partner-accepted', title: name ? w.title : w.noName, body: w.body, url: buildUrl(`/${lang}/dashboard?user_id=1`), tag: 'partner-accepted', lang } }];
          if (show(pushes) !== show(expected) || sent.length !== 0) problems.push(`${lang}${name ? '' : ' (no name)'}: pushes ${show(pushes)}, sent ${show(sent)}`);
        }
      }

      lines.length = 0;
      seed();
      Object.assign(userRow(1), { telegramId: null, _count: { pushDevices: 0 } });
      await notifyInviterOfAccept(1, 'Noam');
      if (pushes.length !== 0 || sent.length !== 0 || !lines.includes('[pair] inviter 1 has no chat; accept notice not sent')) problems.push(`no browser on: pushes ${show(pushes)}, sent ${show(sent)}, lines ${show(lines)}`);

      lines.length = 0;
      seed();
      Object.assign(userRow(1), { telegramId: null, _count: { pushDevices: 1 } });
      const savedKey = process.env.VAPID_PRIVATE_KEY;
      delete process.env.VAPID_PRIVATE_KEY;
      try {
        await notifyInviterOfAccept(1, 'Noam');
      } finally {
        process.env.VAPID_PRIVATE_KEY = savedKey;
      }
      if (pushes.length !== 0 || sent.length !== 0 || !lines.includes('[pair] inviter 1 has no chat; accept notice not sent')) problems.push(`push off: pushes ${show(pushes)}, sent ${show(sent)}, lines ${show(lines)}`);

      seed();
      Object.assign(userRow(1), { _count: { pushDevices: 3 } });
      await notifyInviterOfAccept(1, 'Noam');
      const tgNotice = toChat(5550001);
      if (pushes.length !== 0 || tgNotice.length !== 1 || !tgNotice[0].text?.includes('Noam accepted your invite')) problems.push(`Telegram inviter with browsers on: pushes ${show(pushes)}, sent ${show(sent)}`);

      seed();
      Object.assign(userRow(1), { telegramId: null, whatsappPhone: '+972500000001', messagingPlatform: 'whatsapp', _count: { pushDevices: 3 } });
      await notifyInviterOfAccept(1, 'Noam');
      if (pushes.length !== 0 || sent.filter(m => String(m.chatId) === '+972500000001').length !== 1) problems.push(`WhatsApp inviter with browsers on: pushes ${show(pushes)}, sent ${show(sent)}`);
    } finally {
      console.log = quiet;
    }
    assert(problems.length === 0, problems.join('; '));
  });

  await check('#12 mutual accept at once: the loser gets ALREADY_PAIRED_TOGETHER (service, chat and API), and only the winner\'s inviter is told', async () => {
    const { acceptInvite } = await load('src/services/pairing-service.ts');
```

Old:
```ts
    assert(lines.filter((l) => l.startsWith('[done note]')).join('|') === `[done note] skipped user ${RAZIEL}: no Telegram`, `logged: ${lines.join('|') || '(nothing)'}`);
```

New:
```ts
    assert(lines.filter((l) => l.startsWith('[done note]')).join('|') === `[done note] skipped user ${RAZIEL}: no Telegram`, `logged: ${lines.join('|') || '(nothing)'}`);
    assert(captured.length === 0, `captured ${json(captured)}`);

  /** Raziel (Hebrew) is the creator who hears of Dana's Done; `chat` says what chat he has, `devices` how many browsers have notifications on. */
  const razielIs = (chat: 'none' | 'whatsapp' | 'telegram', devices: number, extra: Row = {}) => {
    const raziel = db.users.find((u) => u.id === RAZIEL)!;
    Object.assign(raziel, { telegramId: chat === 'telegram' ? BigInt(tg(RAZIEL)) : null, whatsappPhone: chat === 'whatsapp' ? '+972500000002' : null, _count: { pushDevices: devices }, ...extra });
  };
  const doneNoteLines = async (run: () => Promise<unknown>) => {
    const lines: string[] = [];
    const log = console.log;
    console.log = (...args: unknown[]) => { lines.push(args.map(String).join(' ')); };
    try {
      await run();
    } finally {
      console.log = log;
    }
    return lines.filter((l) => l.startsWith('[done note]'));
  };

  await check('who, web push: a creator with no chat and a browser on is told by one silent push (kind partner-done, "Done by Dana" in his language, the reminder\'s title as the text, the home); nothing goes to a chat; the Done stands', async () => {
    reset();
    razielIs('none', 2);
    const lines = await doneNoteLines(async () => {
      const r = await act(DANA, MILK_KEY, 'done');
      assert(r.status === 'ok' && stateFor(MILK, MILK_KEY.originalStartsAt)?.doneByUserId === DANA, json(r));
    });
    assert(sends().length === 0, `sent ${json(botCalls)}`);
    assertJson(pushes, [{
      user: { id: RAZIEL },
      input: { kind: 'partner-done', title: 'בוצע ע״י Dana', body: 'Buy milk', url: 'http://localhost:3000/he/dashboard?user_id=2', tag: `nr-done-${MILK}`, lang: 'he', silent: true },
    }], 'the push');
    assert(lines.length === 0 && captured.length === 0, `logged ${lines.join('|')}, captured ${json(captured)}`);
  });

  await check('who, web push: only a person with no chat at all is pushed to: a WhatsApp-only creator, a suspended one, a creator with no browser on, and any creator when push is off are not (the skip line as before); a Telegram creator gets the Telegram note and no push', async () => {
    const skip = `[done note] skipped user ${RAZIEL}: no Telegram`;
    for (const [label, setUp] of [
      ['WhatsApp only', () => razielIs('whatsapp', 2)],
      ['suspended', () => razielIs('none', 2, { suspendedAt: new RealDate(clock) })],
      ['no browser on', () => razielIs('none', 0)],
    ] as const) {
      reset();
      setUp();
      const lines = await doneNoteLines(() => act(DANA, MILK_KEY, 'done'));
      assert(pushes.length === 0 && sends().length === 0, `${label}: pushes ${json(pushes)}, sent ${json(botCalls)}`);
      assert(lines.join('|') === skip, `${label}: logged ${lines.join('|') || '(nothing)'}`);
    }
    reset();
    razielIs('none', 2);
    const savedKey = process.env.VAPID_PRIVATE_KEY;
    delete process.env.VAPID_PRIVATE_KEY;
    try {
      const lines = await doneNoteLines(() => act(DANA, MILK_KEY, 'done'));
      assert(pushes.length === 0 && lines.join('|') === skip, `push off: pushes ${json(pushes)}, logged ${lines.join('|') || '(nothing)'}`);
    } finally {
      process.env.VAPID_PRIVATE_KEY = savedKey;
    }
    reset();
    razielIs('telegram', 2);
    await act(DANA, MILK_KEY, 'done');
    assert(pushes.length === 0 && sends().length === 1 && sends()[0].chatId === tg(RAZIEL) && sends()[0].silent === true, `Telegram creator: pushes ${json(pushes)}, sent ${json(botCalls)}`);
  });

```

```ts
    Object.assign(DB.user[0], { googleRefreshToken: '', telegramId: null, whatsappPhone: null, whatsappBsuid: null, _count: { pushDevices: devices }, ...o });
  // Read through functions: TypeScript would narrow a repeated `pushCalls.length` or `sent[0].via` after the first assert
  const pushed = () => pushCalls.length;
  const via = () => sent.map((s) => s.via).join();
  const reconnectTexts = (lang: string) => {
    const bot = nodeRequire(path.join(ROOT, `messages/${lang}.json`)).bot;
    const first = (text: string) => text.slice(0, text.indexOf('. ') + 1);
    return { title: bot.push.reconnectTitle as string, token: first(bot.googleSync.reconnect.messageToken), scopes: first(bot.googleSync.reconnect.messageScopes) };
  };

  await check('token, web push: an account with no chat and a browser on is told by one notification (kind reconnect, the title without its bold, the message\'s first sentence, the page the Telegram button opens) and stamped told once; nothing goes to a chat; the copies go to token', async () => {
    seedCopy();
    webAccount(1);
    seriesRow('a', 'home');
    itemRow('L', 'a');
    await pass();
    assert(link('L').state === 'token' && sent.length === 0, `link ${link('L').state}, sent ${show(sent)}`);
    assert(pushCalls.length === 1 && pushCalls[0].user.id === TAL, `pushes ${show(pushCalls)}`);
    const { url, ...rest } = pushCalls[0].input;
    assert(show(rest) === show({
      kind: 'reconnect', title: 'Copies to Google are paused',
      body: 'FamCal can\'t reach your Google Calendar right now, so your FamCal calendars aren\'t being copied there.', tag: 'google-reconnect', lang: 'en',
    }), `input ${show(rest)}`);
    assert(String(url).startsWith('http') && String(url).endsWith(`/refresh-token?user_id=${TAL}`), `url ${show(url)}`);
    assert(DB.user[0].googleSyncNotifiedAt?.getTime() === NOW.getTime(), 'not stamped told');
    DB.googleSyncLink.forEach((l) => { if (l.state === 'token') l.state = 'ok'; });
    await pass();
    assert(pushCalls.length === 1, `a second notification in one outage ${show(pushCalls)}`);
  });

  await check('scopes, web push: a missing permission opens the calendars page in the person\'s language; the notification is in he and ru as the chat\'s message is (title and first sentence)', async () => {
    for (const lang of ['en', 'he', 'ru']) {
      seedCopy();
      webAccount(2, { googleRefreshToken: 'tok-1', googleScopes: SCOPES.slice(0, 2), language: lang });
      seriesRow('a', 'home');
      itemRow('L', 'a');
      await pass();
      const want = reconnectTexts(lang);
      assert(link('L').state === 'scopes' && pushCalls.length === 1, `${lang}: link ${link('L').state}, pushes ${show(pushCalls)}`);
      const input = pushCalls[0].input;
      assert(input.title === want.title && input.body === want.scopes && input.lang === lang && String(input.url).endsWith(`/${lang}/calendars?user_id=${TAL}`), `${lang}: ${show(input)} want ${show(want)}`);
      assert(!String(input.title).includes('<b>') && String(input.body).endsWith('.'), `${lang}: markup or a cut sentence in ${show(input)}`);
      if (lang !== 'en') assert(input.body !== reconnectTexts('en').scopes, `${lang}: the English text`);
    }
    for (const lang of ['he', 'ru']) {
      seedCopy();
      webAccount(1, { language: lang });
      seriesRow('a', 'home');
      itemRow('L', 'a');
      await pass();
      assert(pushCalls[0].input.body === reconnectTexts(lang).token && String(pushCalls[0].input.url).endsWith(`/refresh-token?user_id=${TAL}`), `${lang} token: ${show(pushCalls)}`);
    }
  });

  await check('web push: no device took it (a push service asked to retry): not stamped told, logged with the id, the copies still go to token; a later outage can tell them', async () => {
    seedCopy();
    webAccount(1);
    seriesRow('a', 'home');
    itemRow('L', 'a');
    pushResult = { sent: 0, total: 1, retriable: true };
    await pass();
    assert(link('L').state === 'token' && pushCalls.length === 1, `link ${link('L').state}, pushes ${pushCalls.length}`);
    assert(!DB.user[0].googleSyncNotifiedAt, `stamped ${show(DB.user[0].googleSyncNotifiedAt)}`);
    assert(logs.includes(`[google-sync] user ${TAL}: no device took the reconnect notification; not marked told`), `logged: ${show(logs.filter((l) => l.startsWith('[google-sync]')))}`);
    assert(!logs.some((l) => l.includes('reconnect message sent')), 'said it was sent');
  });

  await check('web push: not for a Telegram account or a WhatsApp-only one with a browser on (their message is unchanged), and not when push is off; the account with no chat then gets the old skip line and no stamp', async () => {
    seedCopy();
    Object.assign(DB.user[0], { googleRefreshToken: '', _count: { pushDevices: 2 } });
    seriesRow('a', 'home');
    itemRow('L', 'a');
    await pass();
    assert(pushed() === 0 && via() === 'telegram', `Telegram: pushes ${show(pushCalls)}, sent ${show(sent)}`);
    seedCopy();
    Object.assign(DB.user[0], { googleRefreshToken: '', telegramId: null, whatsappPhone: '+972500000001', _count: { pushDevices: 2 } });
    seriesRow('a', 'home');
    itemRow('L', 'a');
    await pass();
    assert(pushed() === 0 && via() === 'whatsapp', `WhatsApp: pushes ${show(pushCalls)}, sent ${show(sent)}`);
    seedCopy();
    webAccount(1);
    seriesRow('a', 'home');
    itemRow('L', 'a');
    const savedKey = process.env.VAPID_PRIVATE_KEY;
    delete process.env.VAPID_PRIVATE_KEY;
    try {
      await pass();
    } finally {
      process.env.VAPID_PRIVATE_KEY = savedKey;
    }
    assert(pushed() === 0 && via() === '' && !DB.user[0].googleSyncNotifiedAt, `push off: pushes ${show(pushCalls)}, sent ${show(sent)}`);
    assert(logs.includes(`[google-sync] user ${TAL}: no chat; no reconnect message`), `logged: ${show(logs.filter((l) => l.startsWith('[google-sync]')))}`);
  });

  await check('pause, web push: an outage that would notify a browser in Shabbat waits for after it, as the chat\'s message does: nothing is pushed, stamped or changed on Saturday, and the next pass after Shabbat pushes once', async () => {
    seedCopy();
    webAccount(1, { pauseOnShabbat: true, zmanimCommunity: 'sephardi', latitude: 31.778, longitude: 35.235, elevationM: 754 });
    seriesRow('a', 'home');
    itemRow('L', 'a');
    const before = logs.length;
    await pass(new Date('2026-10-17T07:00:00Z'));
    assert(link('L').state === 'ok' && pushed() === 0 && DB.user[0].googleSyncNotifiedAt === null, `Shabbat: link ${link('L').state}, pushes ${show(pushCalls)}`);
    assert(logs.slice(before).some((l) => l.startsWith('[pause] skipped Google reconnect message for 1')), `logged: ${show(logs.slice(before))}`);
    await pass(new Date('2026-10-17T16:00:00Z'));
    assert(link('L').state === 'token' && pushed() === 1 && pushCalls[0].input.kind === 'reconnect', `after Shabbat: link ${link('L').state}, pushes ${show(pushCalls)}`);
  });

```

#### 3a. Copy: `bot.push.inviteAccepted`, `inviteAcceptedNoName`, `inviteAcceptedBody`, `doneBy`, `reconnectTitle`

`messages/*.json` are edited as plain text lines, never rewritten with a JSON tool. Run this from the repo root (it adds the five keys at the top of `bot.push` in all three files, creates `bot.push` when it does not exist, leaves a key that is already there alone, and checks the file still parses):

```bash
python3 - <<'PY'
import re, json

KEYS = {
    'en': [
        ('inviteAccepted', '"{name} accepted your invite"'),
        ('inviteAcceptedNoName', '"Your invite was accepted"'),
        ('inviteAcceptedBody', '"You\'re paired now."'),
        ('doneBy', '"Done by {name}"'),
        ('reconnectTitle', '"Copies to Google are paused"'),
    ],
    'he': [
        ('inviteAccepted', '"ההזמנה שלכם התקבלה על ידי {name}"'),
        ('inviteAcceptedNoName', '"ההזמנה שלכם התקבלה"'),
        ('inviteAcceptedBody', '"אתם מחוברים עכשיו."'),
        ('doneBy', '"בוצע ע״י {name}"'),
        ('reconnectTitle', '"ההעתקה ל-Google מושהית"'),
    ],
    'ru': [
        ('inviteAccepted', '"Ваше приглашение принято: {name}"'),
        ('inviteAcceptedNoName', '"Ваше приглашение принято"'),
        ('inviteAcceptedBody', '"Теперь вы связаны."'),
        ('doneBy', '"{name}: готово"'),
        ('reconnectTitle', '"Копирование в\\u00a0Google приостановлено"'),
    ],
}

for lang, entries in KEYS.items():
    path = f'messages/{lang}.json'
    lines = open(path, encoding='utf8').read().split('\n')
    start = lines.index('  "bot": {')
    end = next(i for i in range(start + 1, len(lines)) if lines[i] in ('  },', '  }'))
    push = next((i for i in range(start + 1, end) if lines[i] == '    "push": {'), None)
    have = set()
    if push is not None:
        close = next(i for i in range(push + 1, end) if lines[i] in ('    },', '    }'))
        have = {m.group(1) for l in lines[push + 1:close] for m in [re.match(r'^      "(\w+)":', l)] if m}
    new = [(k, v) for k, v in entries if k not in have]
    if not new:
        continue
    if push is not None:
        lines[push + 1:push + 1] = [f'      "{k}": {v},' for k, v in new]
    else:
        block = ['    "push": {'] + [f'      "{k}": {v}' + (',' if i < len(new) - 1 else '') for i, (k, v) in enumerate(new)] + ['    },']
        first = next(i for i in range(start + 1, end) if re.match(r'^    "\w+": ', lines[i]))
        lines[first:first] = block
    open(path, 'w', encoding='utf8').write('\n'.join(lines))
    json.loads('\n'.join(lines))
print('bot.push updated in en, he, ru')
PY
npx tsx scripts/check-i18n-parity.ts
```

Expected: `bot.push updated in en, he, ru` and `i18n parity OK`. About the words:
- No emoji and no HTML (the Telegram texts carry `🎉`, `✅` and `<b>`; these are separate keys on purpose, and Telegram's own texts are unchanged). `{name}` is filled by `getBotMessage`'s plain `{word}` replacement.
- `doneBy` reuses the wording of Telegram's `nativeReminders.doneBy` without its emoji and title (the title is the body); he `בוצע ע״י {name}` and ru `{name}: готово` are those texts' own forms. `inviteAccepted` in he and ru is worded so it needs no gender for the acceptor (he `ההזמנה שלכם התקבלה על ידי {name}`, ru `Ваше приглашение принято: {name}`), as the existing chat texts are.
- `inviteAcceptedNoName` is the one key beyond the spec's list: the chat notice has a nameless form, so the notification does too (an acceptor with an empty name would otherwise print the literal `{name}`).
- `reconnectTitle` is the Telegram title with its `<b>` taken off, not stripped from it at runtime.

```ts
    if (result.sent > 0) return true;
    console.log(`[google-sync] user ${user.id}: no device took the reconnect notification; not marked told`);
    return false;
  }
  // No device either: not stamped, so a chat connected later is told
  console.log(`[google-sync] user ${user.id}: no chat; no reconnect message`);
  return false;
}

/** A message's first sentence: up to its first ". ", "! " or "? " (all of it when it is one sentence). The reconnect messages are two sentences; a notification carries the first. */
function firstSentence(text: string): string {
  const end = text.search(/[.!?](\s|$)/);
  return end === -1 ? text.trim() : text.slice(0, end + 1).trim();
}

```

Decisions taken here (flag for review):
1. **The wording lives once, in `scripts/lib/legal-push.ts`**, and both the Step 3 script (which writes the messages) and the new smoke L6 (which holds them to it) read it. The lib is generated word for word from spec §8, and the Step 3 script also checks that every text is in the spec file, so a typo in the lib cannot reach the messages.
2. **The positions are the spec's, literally.** P1, P2 and P6 land after the last entry of their lists (P1 after "We keep your place at the level of a town or neighbourhood…", P2 after "**Card or bank details.**", P6 after "When you open the web app, your browser loads…"), as the spec's `.6`, `.5` and `.14` say. P12 is the only insert inside a list (`shifts` in its op).
3. **Russian typography is applied by the script**, not typed: a no-break space (U+00A0) after a one-letter word (в, с, к, о, у, и, а, я) and before an em dash, with the same regular expression `smoke-site-truth` T6 uses. The lib keeps Russian with plain spaces; every comparison reads U+00A0 as a plain space (`plain`).
4. **The message files are rewritten only in their `legal` part**, which round-trips byte for byte through `JSON.stringify(…, null, 2)` (the script checks it); every other key, and the compact arrays elsewhere in the files, stay as they are, so the script is safe to run after Tasks that added `bot.push.*` or `settings.push.*`.

- [ ] **Step 1: Write the failing test**

1a. Create `scripts/lib/legal-push.ts` with exactly this content:

```ts
// What the Privacy Policy and the Terms say about browser notifications (spec 2026-11-05-web-push §8, approved by the
// owner 2026-11-06): where each sentence goes in messages/<lang>.json and what it says. smoke-signin-legal L6 holds the
// messages to it. scripts/lib/ is not a smoke (ci-smokes.sh runs scripts/smoke-*.ts only).
//
// The texts are binding, owner-approved wording (CLAUDE.md): they change only on the owner's word, in all three languages
// at once, with a new LEGAL_UPDATED (src/site/legal.ts). Russian is written here with plain spaces; messages/ru.json
// binds a one-letter word and a dash with a no-break space (smoke-site-truth T6), so every comparison reads U+00A0 as a
// plain space (`plain`).

export type Locale = 'en' | 'he' | 'ru';
export type LegalDoc = 'privacy' | 'terms';

/** One edit: where it goes (`legal.<doc>.sections.<path>`, list items by position) and what the text there says after it. */
export type PushLegalOp = { id: string; doc: LegalDoc; path: string } & (
  /** A new list item at that position. `shifts` (only when it goes inside the list) starts the item that moves one place on. */
  | { kind: 'item'; text: string; shifts?: string }
  /** The string there is exactly this. */
  | { kind: 'whole'; text: string }
  /** This sentence ends the string there. */
  | { kind: 'append'; text: string }
  /** This sentence stands in the string there, just before `before`. */
  | { kind: 'insert'; before: string; text: string }
  /** The string there holds `to` where it held `from`. */
  | { kind: 'swap'; from: string; to: string }
);

/** A string with its no-break spaces read as plain spaces (the same length, so a position in one is a position in the other). */
export const plain = (s: string): string => s.replace(/ /g, ' ');

/** The node at a dotted path (list positions are numbers) under a value. */
export function nodeAt(root: unknown, dotted: string): unknown {
  let node = root;
  for (const key of dotted.split('.')) {
    if (node === null || typeof node !== 'object') return undefined;
    node = (node as Record<string, unknown>)[key];
  }
  return node;
}

/** Why the messages don't say what the op approves, or null when they do. `sections` is `messages.legal[op.doc].sections`. */
export function problem(op: PushLegalOp, sections: unknown): string | null {
  const node = nodeAt(sections, op.path);
  if (typeof node !== 'string') return `${op.id} ${op.path}: ${node === undefined ? 'nothing there' : 'not a string'}`;
  const text = plain(node);
  switch (op.kind) {
    case 'whole':
    case 'item': {
      if (text !== op.text) return `${op.id} ${op.path}: says "${text.slice(0, 90)}"`;
      if (op.kind === 'item' && op.shifts) {
        const next = nodeAt(sections, op.path.replace(/\d+$/, (n) => String(Number(n) + 1)));
        if (typeof next !== 'string' || !plain(next).startsWith(op.shifts)) return `${op.id} ${op.path}: the item after it is not the one that starts "${op.shifts}"`;
      }
      return null;
    }
    case 'append':
      return text.endsWith(` ${op.text}`) ? null : `${op.id} ${op.path}: does not end with "${op.text.slice(0, 60)}…"`;
    case 'insert':
      return text.includes(`${op.text} ${op.before}`) ? null : `${op.id} ${op.path}: lacks "${op.text.slice(0, 60)}…" before "${op.before}"`;
    case 'swap':
      return text.includes(op.to) && !text.includes(op.from) ? null : `${op.id} ${op.path}: lacks "${op.to.slice(0, 60)}…" or still says "${op.from.slice(0, 60)}…"`;
  }
}

/**
 * The twenty edits in each language, in the spec's order: the Privacy Policy's P1-P15, then the Terms' T1-T5. P12 is the only
 * item that goes inside a list (the ban item moves one place on, and the payment record after it); every other new item ends
 * its list.
 */
export const PUSH_LEGAL: Record<Locale, PushLegalOp[]> = {
  en: [
    { id: 'P1', doc: 'privacy', path: 'keep.blocks.1.6', kind: 'item', text: 'If you turn on notifications in a browser: for each phone or computer, the address its push service gave that browser for FamCal’s notifications, two keys that encrypt them so only that browser can read them, and when you turned them on there.' },
    { id: 'P2', doc: 'privacy', path: 'notKeep.blocks.0.5', kind: 'item', text: '**Notifications in your browser.** We write each one when we send it, and don’t store it. It stays on your device until it’s cleared there, and your device’s settings decide whether it shows on the lock screen.' },
    { id: 'P3', doc: 'privacy', path: 'use.blocks.0.0', kind: 'whole', text: 'To run the features you use: your calendar, reminders, briefings and the voice show, notifications in your browser, weather, Hebrew dates and zmanim, the Shabbat and Yom Tov pause, sharing with your partner, and your Google connection.' },
    { id: 'P4', doc: 'privacy', path: 'google.blocks.1.0.items.2', kind: 'whole', text: 'send event alerts on Telegram or in your browser;' },
    { id: 'P5', doc: 'privacy', path: 'google.blocks.1.3', kind: 'append', text: 'If you turn on notifications in your browser, an event’s title, time and place can be in a notification, encrypted on its way so that only your browser can read it, not the push service that carries it.' },
    { id: 'P6', doc: 'privacy', path: 'processors.blocks.1.14', kind: 'item', text: '**Your browser’s push service,** if you turn on notifications: the push service your browser uses (its maker picks it, for example Apple’s, Google’s, Mozilla’s or Microsoft’s) delivers FamCal’s notifications to your browser. For each notification, it gets your browser’s notification address, our server’s IP address, and the notification’s time, size and delivery settings, such as how long it may wait. The content is encrypted for your browser, so the push service can’t read it. Which service it is depends on your browser, and it runs under its operator’s own privacy policy.' },
    { id: 'P7', doc: 'privacy', path: 'processors.blocks.3.3', kind: 'whole', text: 'Telegram, Meta and Google run their own apps under their own privacy policies, and the push service your browser uses runs under its operator’s own.' },
    { id: 'P8', doc: 'privacy', path: 'cookies.blocks.1', kind: 'whole', text: 'The web app also saves a few view choices in your browser, such as which tab you were on. If you turn notifications on, it also installs a small FamCal program in your browser (a service worker): it only shows FamCal’s notifications and opens the app when you tap one, and it saves no pages. Your browser then keeps the subscription its push service made for FamCal. All of these are needed for the site and the app to work. We use no ad or tracking cookies.' },
    { id: 'P9', doc: 'privacy', path: 'retention.blocks.0.9', kind: 'item', text: '**Notifications in your browser:** each device until you turn them off or sign out there; until you sign out on all devices, or remove a way to sign in (the device you remove it on stays); until your account gets a chat (Telegram or WhatsApp), is suspended or banned, or is deleted. If the browser or its push service ends them, we delete the device at the next notification. We keep up to 10 devices: turning on an 11th removes the oldest. A notification waiting at a push service for a device that’s off is dropped there within a day.' },
    { id: 'P10', doc: 'privacy', path: 'rights.blocks.5.1', kind: 'insert', before: 'You can also ask us to stop them', text: 'To stop notifications in a browser, turn them off in Settings in that browser, or block FamCal’s notifications in your browser’s or device’s settings.' },
    { id: 'P11', doc: 'privacy', path: 'rights.blocks.7.0', kind: 'swap', from: 'your plan, your activity log', to: 'your plan, the list of devices you get notifications on, your activity log' },
    { id: 'P12', doc: 'privacy', path: 'rights.blocks.9.5', kind: 'item', shifts: 'if we banned the account', text: 'a notification still waiting at a push service for a device that’s off, until it’s delivered or dropped (within a day);' },
    { id: 'P13', doc: 'privacy', path: 'euUk.blocks.2.0', kind: 'whole', text: 'To run FamCal for you (your account and its sign-in methods, calendar, settings, Google connection, partner sharing, briefings, notifications in your browser, and reading what you send the bot): our agreement with you.' },
    { id: 'P14', doc: 'privacy', path: 'security.blocks.0.5', kind: 'item', text: 'Each notification to a browser is encrypted for that browser alone, so the push service that carries it can’t read it.' },
    { id: 'P15', doc: 'privacy', path: 'euUk.blocks.4', kind: 'append', text: 'A notification reaches your browser’s push service, wherever it is, because you turned notifications on, so that it can be delivered to you.' },
    { id: 'T1', doc: 'terms', path: 'service.blocks.4.0', kind: 'whole', text: 'reminders and event alerts (an account made on famcal.bot can get them as notifications in the browser until it connects Telegram);' },
    { id: 'T2', doc: 'terms', path: 'service.blocks.5', kind: 'swap', from: 'Briefings and reminders reach you once you connect Telegram in Settings.', to: 'Reminders, event alerts and a short notification that lists the day’s events reach you on each phone or computer where you turn notifications on (on iPhone and iPad, after you add FamCal to the Home Screen).     { id: 'T3', doc: 'terms', path: 'delivery.blocks.0', kind: 'whole', text: 'A reminder or a briefing can arrive late, or not at all, for example when Telegram, WhatsApp, your browser’s push service or one of our providers has a problem, or when your device is off or blocks notifications.     { id: 'T4', doc: 'terms', path: 'account.blocks.0.5', kind: 'item', text: 'Notifications show your reminders’ and events’ titles, times and places on each device where you turn them on and, depending on its settings, on its lock screen.     { id: 'T5', doc: 'terms', path: 'liability.blocks.3', kind: 'whole', text: 'Telegram, WhatsApp, Google, your browser’s push service and our providers run their own services under their own terms, and we aren’t responsible for them.' },
  ],
  he: [
    { id: 'P1', doc: 'privacy', path: 'keep.blocks.1.6', kind: 'item', text: 'אם מפעילים התראות בדפדפן: לכל טלפון או מחשב, הכתובת ששירות ההתראות (push) של הדפדפן נתן לדפדפן הזה בשביל ההתראות של FamCal, שני מפתחות שמצפינים את ההתראות כך שרק הדפדפן הזה יכול לקרוא אותן, ומתי הפעלתם שם את ההתראות.' },

    { id: 'P5', doc: 'privacy', path: 'google.blocks.1.3', kind: 'append', text: 'אם מפעילים התראות בדפדפן, הכותרת, השעה והמקום של אירוע יכולים להופיע בהתראה. ההתראה מוצפנת בדרך, כך שרק הדפדפן שלכם יכול לקרוא אותה, ולא שירות ההתראות שמעביר אותה.' },
    { id: 'P6', doc: 'privacy', path: 'processors.blocks.1.14', kind: 'item', text: '**שירות ההתראות (push) של הדפדפן שלכם,** אם מפעילים התראות: את ההתראות של FamCal מעביר לדפדפן שלכם שירות ההתראות שהדפדפן משתמש בו, לפי בחירת החברה שיצרה אותו (למשל השירות של Apple, של Google, של Mozilla או של Microsoft). על כל התראה, השירות מקבל את כתובת ההתראות של הדפדפן שלכם, את כתובת ה־IP של השרת שלנו, ואת השעה, הגודל והגדרות המסירה של ההתראה, כמו כמה זמן היא יכולה לחכות. התוכן מוצפן בשביל הדפדפן שלכם, ולכן שירות ההתראות לא יכול לקרוא אותו. איזה שירות זה תלוי בדפדפן שלכם, והוא פועל לפי מדיניות הפרטיות של החברה שמפעילה אותו.' },
    { id: 'P7', doc: 'privacy', path: 'processors.blocks.3.3', kind: 'whole', text: 'טלגרם, Meta ו־Google מפעילות את האפליקציות שלהן לפי מדיניות הפרטיות שלהן, וגם שירות ההתראות שהדפדפן שלכם משתמש בו פועל לפי מדיניות הפרטיות של החברה שמפעילה אותו.' },
    { id: 'P8', doc: 'privacy', path: 'cookies.blocks.1', kind: 'whole', text: 'אפליקציית הרשת שומרת בדפדפן שלכם גם כמה העדפות תצוגה, כמו הלשונית שבה הייתם. אם מפעילים התראות, היא מתקינה בדפדפן גם תוכנה קטנה של FamCal (service worker), שרק מציגה את ההתראות של FamCal ופותחת את האפליקציה כשמקישים על אחת מהן, ולא שומרת דפים. הדפדפן שומר אז גם את המינוי ששירות ההתראות שלו יצר בשביל FamCal. כל אלה נחוצים כדי שהאתר והאפליקציה יעבדו. אנחנו לא משתמשים בעוגיות פרסום או מעקב.' },
    { id: 'P9', doc: 'privacy', path: 'retention.blocks.0.9', kind: 'item', text: '**התראות בדפדפן:** כל מכשיר נשמר עד שמכבים בו את ההתראות או יוצאים בו מהחשבון; עד שיוצאים מהחשבון בכל המכשירים, או מסירים דרך כניסה (המכשיר שבו מסירים אותה נשאר); עד שמחברים לחשבון צ׳אט (טלגרם או וואטסאפ), עד שהחשבון מושעה או נחסם, או עד שהוא נמחק. אם הדפדפן או שירות ההתראות שלו מבטלים את ההתראות, אנחנו מוחקים את המכשיר בהתראה הבאה. אנחנו שומרים עד 10 מכשירים: הפעלה במכשיר ה־11 מוחקת את הוותיק שבהם. התראה שמחכה בשירות ההתראות למכשיר כבוי נמחקת שם תוך יום לכל היותר.' },
    { id: 'P10', doc: 'privacy', path: 'rights.blocks.5.1', kind: 'insert', before: 'אפשר גם לבקש מאיתנו להפסיק לשלוח אותן', text: 'כדי להפסיק התראות בדפדפן, מכבים אותן בהגדרות באותו דפדפן, או חוסמים את ההתראות של FamCal בהגדרות של הדפדפן או של המכשיר.' },
    { id: 'P11', doc: 'privacy', path: 'rights.blocks.7.0', kind: 'swap', from: 'את המסלול, את רישום הפעילות', to: 'את המסלול, את רשימת המכשירים שבהם הופעלו התראות, את רישום הפעילות' },
    { id: 'P12', doc: 'privacy', path: 'rights.blocks.9.5', kind: 'item', shifts: 'אם חסמנו את החשבון', text: 'התראה שעדיין מחכה בשירות ההתראות למכשיר כבוי, עד שהיא נמסרת או נמחקת (תוך יום לכל היותר);' },
    { id: 'P13', doc: 'privacy', path: 'euUk.blocks.2.0', kind: 'whole', text: 'להפעלת FamCal בשבילכם (החשבון ודרכי הכניסה אליו, היומן, ההגדרות, החיבור ל־Google, השיתוף עם בן או בת הזוג, הסיכומים, ההתראות בדפדפן, וקריאת מה ששולחים לבוט): ההסכם שלנו איתכם.' },
    { id: 'P14', doc: 'privacy', path: 'security.blocks.0.5', kind: 'item', text: 'כל התראה לדפדפן מוצפנת בשביל הדפדפן הזה בלבד, כך ששירות ההתראות שמעביר אותה לא יכול לקרוא אותה.' },
    { id: 'P15', doc: 'privacy', path: 'euUk.blocks.4', kind: 'append', text: 'התראה מגיעה לשירות ההתראות של הדפדפן שלכם, בכל מדינה שבה הוא נמצא, כי הפעלתם התראות, וכדי שתגיע אליכם.' },
    { id: 'T1', doc: 'terms', path: 'service.blocks.4.0', kind: 'whole', text: 'תזכורות והתראות לפני אירועים (חשבון שנפתח ב־famcal.bot יכול לקבל אותן כהתראות בדפדפן, עד שמחברים אליו את טלגרם);' },
    { id: 'T2', doc: 'terms', path: 'service.blocks.5', kind: 'swap', from: 'סיכומים ותזכורות מגיעים אליכם אחרי שמחברים את טלגרם בהגדרות.', to: 'תזכורות, התראות לפני אירועים והתראה קצרה עם רשימת האירועים של היום מגיעות אליכם בכל טלפון או מחשב שבו מפעילים התראות (באייפון ובאייפד, אחרי שמוסיפים את FamCal למסך הבית). אחרי שמחברים את טלגרם, הן מגיעות בטלגרם, וההתראות בדפדפן נפסקות.' },
    { id: 'T3', doc: 'terms', path: 'delivery.blocks.0', kind: 'whole', text: 'תזכורת או סיכום יכולים להגיע באיחור, או לא להגיע בכלל, למשל כשיש תקלה בטלגרם, בוואטסאפ, בשירות ההתראות של הדפדפן או אצל אחד הספקים שלנו, או כשהמכשיר כבוי או חוסם התראות. התראה שמחכה למכשיר כבוי נמחקת אחרי זמן מה (תוך יום לכל היותר), ולא נשלחת שוב.' },
    { id: 'T4', doc: 'terms', path: 'account.blocks.0.5', kind: 'item', text: 'התראות מציגות את הכותרות, השעות והמקומות של התזכורות והאירועים שלכם בכל מכשיר שבו הפעלתם אותן, ולפי ההגדרות של המכשיר גם במסך הנעילה. כל מי שרואה את המכשיר יכול לקרוא אותן, ולכן כדאי לכבות אותן במכשיר שגם אחרים משתמשים בו.' },
    { id: 'T5', doc: 'terms', path: 'liability.blocks.3', kind: 'whole', text: 'טלגרם, וואטסאפ, Google, שירות ההתראות של הדפדפן שלכם והספקים שלנו מפעילים את השירותים שלהם לפי התנאים שלהם, ואנחנו לא אחראים להם.' },
  ],
  ru: [
    { id: 'P1', doc: 'privacy', path: 'keep.blocks.1.6', kind: 'item', text: 'Если вы включили уведомления в браузере: для каждого телефона или компьютера — адрес, который служба push-уведомлений выдала этому браузеру для FamCal, два ключа, которыми уведомления шифруются так, что прочитать их может только этот браузер, и когда вы включили там уведомления.' },
    { id: 'P2', doc: 'privacy', path: 'notKeep.blocks.0.5', kind: 'item', text: '**Уведомления в браузере.** Мы составляем каждое уведомление в момент отправки и не храним его.
```
